Ko-fi help

This guide holds Ko-fi’s own help, gathered from help.ko-fi.com in November 2026. Ko-fi is a site where creators take tips, memberships, commissions and shop sales from their supporters. It covers getting started, ways to earn, payments, and using Ko-fi with other platforms. Patreon, a similar site, has a guide of its own in this collection.

Left out on purpose: legal pages such as terms and privacy policies, pages for law enforcement and developers, and Android-only articles, as this collection covers Windows, the web, iPhone and iPad, and Alexa. The categories are the publisher’s own.

The help text belongs to its publisher and is gathered here unchanged for accessible reading. This guide’s own arrangement and wording are available under CC BY-SA 4.0.

Account settings

Blocking and limiting users on Ko-fi

Ko-fi is a friendly and supportive community, and we want to keep it that way! If you’ve had a bad experience with a user, you can block or limit them. This can remove them as a follower, stop them from paying or messaging you, and let you control who comments on your posts and images.

How to block a user

To block a user, they need to have a Ko-fi account.

  • Go to their page (ko-fi.com/theirpage) or find their transaction in Payments and Orders.
  • Click the three dots “…”.
  • Select “Block.”
  • Confirm that you want to block this user.

After you block a user

  • The blocked user can’t support you in future.
  • The blocked user can’t send you direct messages.
  • The blocked user’s future comments on posts will be hidden.
  • If they are following you, they will be removed from your followers list.
  • The blocked user won’t be notified that they’ve been blocked.

Do blocked users lose access to content?

No, they will only lose the features listed above. To stop access to supporter-only content, you need to cancel or refund their payment. More info here.

Do past comments disappear when blocked?

Only comments made after blocking will be hidden.

Can blocked users make comments, and who can see them?

Yes, blocked users can make comments. However, their comments will be hidden from public view and the creator’s view. Blocked users will still see their comments as normal.

How to unblock a user?

  • Go to their page or find their transaction in Payments and Orders.
  • Click the three dots “…”.
  • Choose “Unblock”.

Will unblocked user comments reappear?

Yes, comments made while blocked will reappear.

Note

If they are harassing you or you believe they are breaking Ko-fi’s terms of service, please report their page by using this guide here.

If you have any questions or have trouble blocking a user, feel free to send any questions our way.

Removing a follower

If you want to prevent a follower from receiving your updates, you can remove them from following you. Here’s how:

  • Go to your followers list on your home dashboard.
  • Locate the follower you wish to remove.
  • Click to remove them, then confirm.

Your follower won’t receive any notification about being removed and won’t receive updates from you anymore.

Can removed followers follow again?

Currently, removed followers can re-follow your page. Although, you can consider blocking them using the steps above.

If you need help or want to report something, feel free to send any questions our way.

Control who can message you directly

If you want to manage who can send you direct messages, you can set preferences and limit users after they message you.

Control who can message you

  • Go to your Page Settings under Direct Messages.
  • Choose from:
    • Anyone who supports me: Allows any supporter to message you.
    • Only supporters who I message first: You initiate the conversation.

Control messages from specific users

To limit users after they message you:

  • Go to your Direct Messages.
  • Click on “…” on the top right, to view options for managing the conversation.
  • From here you can:

Mute this user: stop receiving notifications for replies.

Block this user: stop receiving messages from this user

Delete conversation: remove this conversation from your message history, though the user will still see it until they delete it.

For more information on direct messaging, check out our help article.

Control who can comment on your content

If you want to manage who can comment on your posts, follow these steps:

  • Go to your Page Settings under “Comments”.
  • Choose between these options:
    • Anyone with a Ko-fi account can comment
    • Only supporters can comment

For more information on posts, check out our help article.

Will blocking a user make them unable to view my page?

No, Ko-fi profiles are public. Blocked supporters can still view your page but they will no longer be able to comment, message or support you.

Can you block someone that does not have a Ko-fi page?

Currently, the user must have a Ko-fi page for you to block them.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any question our way. 💌

Cancel your Ko-fi Gold subscription

We understand if you’ve decided to cancel your Gold subscription. By canceling, you won’t be charged again on your next billing date. Rest assured, you’ll continue to enjoy all your benefits until the end of your subscription period.

Even without Gold, you can still access features like the Ko-fi Shop, Memberships, Commissions, and more, instead with a 5% service fee for each transaction. In most cases, you might consider becoming a Ko-fi contributor to maintain exclusive Gold features, such as your custom “coffee” metaphor and page color. You can opt out of contributor status whenever you like.

How to cancel Ko-fi Gold

To cancel your Gold subscription, simply follow these steps:

  • Navigate to your Account & Billing.
  • Under subscriptions, locate your “Ko-fi Gold Subscription.”
  • Select the three dots and choose “Cancel Gold.”
  • Once completed, you won’t be charged again, but you’ll retain your benefits until the subscription renewal date.

Can’t find the “Cancel Gold” button?

If you subscribed to Gold using PayPal and can’t locate the “Cancel Gold” button, you can easily unsubscribe from your PayPal dashboard. Here’s how:

  • Log into your PayPal account.
  • Navigate to the ‘pre-approved payment’ section for Ko-fi Labs Limited.
  • Next to the status, click on ‘Cancel’ to stop renewal of the subscription.

Once you confirm the cancellation, your Gold subscription will not renew, and is now cancelled.

What happens after you cancel your Ko-fi Gold subscription?

Even after canceling your Ko-fi Gold subscription, you’ll continue to enjoy its benefits until the end of your subscription period. Once that period expires, in most cases, we’ll automatically transition you to contributor status. We do that so you don’t lose any premium features.

Here’s what you need to know:

  • With contributor status, the main difference is that a 5% service fee will be deducted from each payment you receive rather than paying a monthly/yearly Gold payment. Only pay a fee when you get paid.
  • All your memberships, recurring payments, and most changes to your page and settings will remain after your Gold subscription expires, so no need to worry about losing any customizations.

For more details on the fees Ko-fi takes or how to opt out of contributor status, you can refer to our guide here.

Why am I still being billed if I canceled?

First, check your Account & Billing to confirm that your subscription has been canceled. If you canceled after the auto-billing date and still see charge attempts, contact us here and we’ll sort it out for you.

We’re here if you need us or if you have any suggestions. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

Changing email notification settings

Stay on top of your Ko-fi updates by customizing your email notifications. You can choose whether you want to receive product updates, welcome emails, updates from creators, and more. Whether you’re a creator or a supporter, you can manage your preferences from the same page.

Editing your preferences

Choose which notifications about your Ko-fi account you’d like to receive.

  • Go to your Account Settings.
  • Scroll to your Notification Settings.
  • Choose your preferences. You’ll see a list of notifications. Check or uncheck the ones you want to receive.
General notifications

These are emails sent regarding your Ko-fi account activity.

Creator notifications

Choose which notifications you want to receive for each creator you follow.

How often will I receive emails?

We aim to keep our emails relevant and not overwhelming.

  • Welcome emails: Just a few to get you started.
  • Account activity emails: As activity happens.
  • Direct Message emails: When you receive a new message.
  • Weekly roundup emails about Direct Messages: Once a week, when there’s activity.
  • Subscription/membership emails: When there’s a change.
  • Ko-fi updates and newsletters: A few times a month.
  • New content from creator: As activity happens.
  • New product/services from creator: When it’s added.

I’m not receiving notifications

If you’re not getting emails from us, try these steps:

  • Check your notification settings. Make sure you’re opted into the emails you want in your Notification Settings.
  • Add Ko-fi to your safe sender list. Add hello@ko-fi.com and ko-fi@ko-fi.com to make sure our emails reach your inbox.
  • Check your Spam/Junk/Social folders. Sometimes our emails get sent to a different folder.
  • Check your browser notifications are turned on. For browser notifications, click the bell at the top of your page, scroll down, and tap Turn On. Make sure your browser is not blocking these.

If you’re still not receiving notifications, reach out to us here. We’re happy to help!

Unsubscribe from all emails

Unsubscribe from all notifications by following these steps:

  • Go to your Account Settings.
  • Scroll to your Notification Settings.
  • Uncheck all boxes to opt out of notification emails.

Note

We must send certain emails about account security and specific payments. We need to send these for legal reasons.

We’re here if you need us or if you have any suggestions. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

Delete my account

Sorry to see you go! 😢 We’ve got your back if you need to delete your account. Don’t forget, we’re always here to help if you have questions or concerns.

In a nutshell

  • Head to Account & Billing
  • Scroll to the bottom and click “Delete Account”
  • Confirm deletion via the email we’ll send you
  • That’s it! Account deleted 🎉

Removing your account

Head to Account & Billing, scroll to the bottom, and Delete Account.

An email will be sent to the account email address to confirm your decision to delete your page.

Note

If you don’t receive an email, check your junk or spam folder for an email from ko-fi@ko-fi.com. Still didn’t get the email? Reach out to us here.

What happens when my account is deleted?

What happens depends on whether you’re a creator or a supporter.

Creator Supporter

As a creator, deleting your account will permanently remove everything from Ko-fi. That includes your transaction history, posts, supporter lists, and any other account information.

All active subscriptions to your page will be automatically canceled.

Once your account is deleted, there’s no way to recover it or any of the information tied to it.

Tip

Looking to stop accepting payments but don’t want to delete your account? You can disconnect your PayPal or Stripe from your Payment Settings at any time. You can always reconnect them when you’re ready to start receiving payments again.

Note that any active subscriptions will still renew even if your PayPal/Stripe is disconnected since they’re tied to your payment provider rather than your Ko-fi account.

For more information, check out our Privacy Policy and our Terms.

When a supporter deletes their account, it will remove their account information from Ko-fi. However, this does not cancel any ongoing subscriptions. If your supporter wants to stop a subscription, they will need to cancel it before deleting their account.

You can provide them with our step-by-step guide here.

For more information, check out our Privacy Policy and our Terms.

Too many emails in your inbox?

No worries! You won’t have to delete your account. You can adjust your notification settings at any time.

Finding it tough to grow your support base?

Growing an audience can be tricky, and monetizing your creations even more so. But we’re here to help with a series of guides to promote your page. Whether you’re just starting out or could use specific advice from our blog, don’t hesitate to reach out or join our lively Discord Community.

We’re committed to helping you every step of your creator journey.

Feedback or suggestions

We’re all ears! If you have ideas or feedback to make Ko-fi better, we’d love to hear from you.

Take a moment to fill out this short survey and help us improve.

FAQ

Can I delete an account I no longer have access to?

If you don’t have access to the email linked to your Ko-fi account, you won’t be able to delete it yourself.

We can’t delete accounts on your behalf, but get in touch with us and we’ll help update your email address so you can log in and manage your account.

Can I recover my deleted account?

Once your account is deleted, it can’t be recovered.

However, you can create a new account and choose the same username again, as long as it’s still available.

I accidentally made two accounts. Can you delete one for me?

We’re not able to delete accounts for you, but reach out to us and we’ll help you regain access to the duplicate so you can delete it yourself.

Just make sure you’re logged into the right account before deleting, as deletion is permanent.

How can I claim my username again?

Once your account is fully deleted, your username will become available. You can then create a new account and claim it again.

If someone else claims the username after deletion, we won’t be able to make it available.

Can I deactivate my account instead of deleting it?

If you’d like to step back without fully deleting your account, you can disconnect your payment methods to stop receiving new payments while keeping your page active.

Note

Keep in mind that monthly tips and memberships will still renew, even if your payment method is not connected. If you don’t want to receive payments, be sure to cancel any subscriptions first.

Does Ko-fi support other languages?

Currently, Ko-fi is only available in English. But we know how important it is to support creators from all over the world. As we grow we will be looking into ways to translate Ko-fi into other languages.

While we work on it, here are some tips to help you get the most out of Ko-fi:

  • Use translation tools: Tools like Google Translate or browser plugins/add-ons such as DeepL Translate can automatically translate the site for you. You can suggest these to your supporters as well.
  • Create multilingual content: You can write your About section, posts, and captions in multiple languages to connect with a wider audience and make your content more accessible.
  • Let us know which language you’d like to see on Ko-fi by sending a feature request! We read every single one.

We appreciate your patience and understanding as we work to improve Ko-fi for all creators!

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

How do I activate my account?

To make the most of Ko-fi, you need to activate your account. If things don’t go as planned, like a missing email, an expired link, or getting stuck halfway don’t worry. This guide will help you sort it out and get back on track in no time.

My verification link doesn’t work or has expired

Verification links expire after a while to keep your account secure. If your link isn’t valid, you might see this error:

If this happens, here’s what to do:

Enter the verification code manually
- Open your email from Ko-fi@ko-fi.com with the subject line ‘Confirm Your Email.’
- Copy the code in the email.
- Go to the Ko-fi activation page and enter the code.
  • Your account should now be activated. 🎉
  • If you’re not sure how to access the activation page, check the next section below.
Resend the verification email
- Go to the Ko-fi activation page to send a new code.
- You’ll get a new email with a link to activate your account. Just click ‘Activate Account’ as normal.
  • Your account should now be activated. 🎉
  • If you’re not sure how to access the activation page, check the next section below.
  • Note
  • If the previous code hasn’t been used, you might receive the same one.
  • If the new link still doesn’t work, reach out to us, and we’ll help you get sorted.

I can’t find the activation page

If you can’t find the activation page, no problem!

  • If you’re a Creator: Log in again with your email and password. The activation prompt will reappear so you can activate your account.
  • If you’re a Supporter: Log in with your email and password, then go to Payments & Orders. The activation page will appear, and you can activate your account from there.

Your account should now be activated. 🎉

If you’re still having trouble, reach out to us, and we’ll help you get sorted.

I’m not receiving verification emails

If you’re not receiving verification emails, try these steps:

  • Check the email displayed for any typos or an incorrect domain. You can also check your Account & Billing to confirm your account email.
  • Search for emails from Ko-fi@ko-fi.com or look for the subject line ‘Confirm Your Email.’
  • Check your junk or spam folder, as emails can sometimes end up there.
  • Still not seeing the email? Contact Ko-fi Support, and we’ll assist you.

If you’re still having trouble, we’re here to help. Send us a message, and we’ll get things sorted. 💌

I think my account has been compromised

If you notice unfamiliar activity on your Ko-fi account or you’ve received a notification from Ko-fi that doesn’t look right, you should follow these steps to secure your account.

Quick steps to secure your account

  • Change your password to prevent others from accessing your account
  • Enable Two-Factor Authentication (2FA) for an extra security boost
  • Consider changing your Account Email to an address that no one else knows
  • Contact us with a brief explanation of what you’ve noticed, the email address associated with your account, and any other information that you believe may be helpful

Signs your account might be compromised

  • If you get a link from Ko-fi saying your email address or payment method (like PayPal or Stripe) has been changed, and you didn’t do it, that’s a sign something might be up.
  • If you’ve received a notification from Ko-fi about a payment, but haven’t received the money into your PayPal or Stripe account.
  • If you’re sure you’re using the right login details but still can’t get into your account, or if you’re not getting an email when you click the ‘forgotten password’ link, something might be wrong.
  • Other clues might be finding content you didn’t post, messages you didn’t send, or payments to another Ko-fi page that you didn’t make.

Keep a lookout for these signs, and if you see any of them, make sure to quickly take steps to protect your account.

Ways to secure your account

Change your password Log in to your account, go to Account Settings, and click on ‘Change your password’ to view the Change Password screen.

Be sure to choose a strong, unique password that you haven’t used elsewhere.

We suggest:

  • Using a combination of lowercase and uppercase letters, numbers, and symbols
  • Excluding any personal information and real words
  • Changing your password regularly, but always prioritising strength and uniqueness

If you find your are locked out, select ‘Forgot password?’ on the login screen. You can then safely reset your password via email. If you signed up to Ko-fi using Google or Facebook, you should also change your passwords for those services.

Enable Two-Factor Authentication (2FA) We strongly recommend enabling Two-Factor Authentication (2FA). In doing so, a 6-digit code will be required each time you log in and further improve your account’s security. For guidance on setting up 2FA see our article here.

Consider changing your account email If you think someone has access to the email account you use on Ko-fi, consider updating the email address that you use to log in to Ko-fi. In Account Settings select ‘Change your email address.’ Enter your new email, and we’ll send a confirmation to your existing email. This ensures that nobody can change your email address without accessing your email account.

Contact us If you can’t log in or complete the steps above for any reason and suspect that your account has been compromised, don’t hesitate to reach out to us - we’ll respond as quickly as we can.

Payments not received

If someone has sent you a payment on Ko-fi, but you haven’t received the money you should check your Payment Settings to make sure your Ko-fi page is connected to your payment method.

Check carefully for any discrepancies in the email addresses used. If it’s not your account then disable payments by toggling off ‘Accept Payments’, then follow the steps above to secure your account before reactivating payments.

If you are confident the payment methods are connected to your owned accounts, check your Payments History for the status of those payments.

Also check the payment methods directly by logging into your PayPal or Stripe account. It’s best to do this on the full ‘desktop’ version of the payment service instead of any apps which may only show partial information. Check for any issues with your account or issues highlighted on that specific payment.

If you’re still having trouble, don’t hesitate to contact the support team.

Resetting your password

Forgot your password? Password resets can be done by following these steps:

  • Click or tap the Forgot password option on the login page.
  • Enter the Ko-fi handle/username or the email address linked to the Ko-fi page to receive the reset link.
  • Look for an email with the subject “Your Password Reset Link” from Ko-fi@ko-fi.com.
  • Use the link provided in the email to redirect to the password change screen. Change the password and regain access to the Ko-fi page.

Tip

For added security, creators can explore the guide to keeping a Ko-fi account safe.

What to do if the password reset link doesn’t arrive

Creators who haven’t received their reset link can try these steps:

  • Check that the reset link isn’t in the spam/junk folder.
  • Confirm that the email address the reset link is being sent to is the one linked to the Ko-fi page. It’s typically the address that already receives emails from Ko-fi.
  • If the link still hasn’t arrived, please submit a support ticket for assistance.

What to do if the password reset link shows an error page

If a creator opens their reset link and sees the error message “Oops. We hit a small glitch. We’ll fix it as soon as we possibly can.”, they can try opening the reset link in another browser or on another device.

How to reset a password when logging in with Twitter, Twitch, Google, or Facebook

Creators who log in with Twitter, Twitch, Google, or Facebook can still reset their Ko-fi password.

A creator’s Ko-fi page is linked to the email address they use to log in to that social account. They can request a password reset for their Ko-fi page, and the reset link will be sent to that email address.

Creators who want to change their login method can read the help article on updating a Ko-fi account email address.

Still having trouble resetting a password?

Creators who are still having trouble can raise a support ticket with the email address and handle of the Ko-fi page they’re trying to access. We’re here to help! 💌

Setting up two-factor authentication (2FA)

By enabling 2FA, you’ll protect your account with both your password and a code from your email or authentication app. It’s a simple step to keep your account safe and your work secure. 🔒

Enabling 2FA

You can secure your Ko-fi account by setting up 2FA with either an authentication app or your email. Both options provide an extra layer of protection, ensuring only you can access your account. Choose the method that works best for you, and follow the steps below to get started.

Email Authentication app

If you prefer a simpler way to secure your Ko-fi account, you can set up 2FA using your email. With this option, each time you log in, you’ll receive a one-time code directly to your inbox. You’ll need this code and your password to get into your account. Follow the steps below to enable 2FA via email.

  • Go to your Account & Billing and under Two-factor authentication, tap Set up.
  • Select Email and tap Next.
  • Check your inbox for an email from Ko-fi@ko-fi.com titled “Your 2FA Verification Code is…”
  • Enter the code and hit Verify.

And that’s it! You’ve successfully set up email 2FA.

With an authenticator app like Google Authenticator, LastPass, or Microsoft Authenticator, you’ll receive a unique code on your mobile device each time you log in. This code is required in addition to your password, making it much harder for anyone else to access your account. Follow the steps below to set up 2FA with an authenticator app.

  • Go to your Account & Billing. Head to Two-factor authentication, and choose Set up.
  • Select Authentication app and tap Next.
  • Scan the QR code with your authentication app. If you can’t scan the QR code, copy/paste the secret code into your authentication app.
  • Enter the code shown in your authenticator app.
  • Next, enter your mobile number for SMS backup in case you lose access to your authenticator.
  • Enter the SMS code and tap Verify.

Voila! Your account is now secured with 2FA.

We’re here if you need us or if you have any suggestions. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

Frequently Asked Questions

My account has been compromised/hacked

If you suspect your account has been compromised, follow our guide here to secure your account and contact us.

I lost access to my email

If you can’t receive 2FA emails due to lost email access, you can reach out to us here.

We’ll ask some security questions to make sure it’s you and help you change your account email.

For more info, check out our help article here.

I lost access to my authenticator app

Can’t access your authenticator? You can send an SMS code to your number instead.

If you lost access to that phone number or you’re not receiving the SMS code, contact us and we’ll lend a hand once we verify it’s you.

Can I enable 2FA without a phone number?

We ask for a phone number to ensure you can still access your account if you lose access to your authentication app.

If you’d rather not use a phone number, we suggest enabling 2FA via email instead.

I received an error entering my phone number

If you receive an “Incorrect format” error when entering your phone number, make sure you enter your number with:

  • No spaces
  • No special characters (such as spaces or dashes)
  • No country code

Also be sure that you’re not using a VoIP, such as a Google Voice number.

If you’re still getting the error, contact us and we’ll be happy to help.

How do I disconnect 2FA?

Need to remove 2FA from your account? Follow these steps.

  • Navigate to your Account & Billing.
  • Under Two-factor authentication, tap .
  • Select Disable.

If you can’t log in to your account to disconnect, reach out and we’ll help!

I didn’t receive my 6-digit email code

  • Check your spam or junk folder in case the email was filtered there.
  • Make sure you’re checking the inbox of the same email address linked to your Ko‑fi account and 2FA setup.
  • If you’ve tried both and still haven’t received your code, please contact Ko‑fi Support so we can help.

Switch to dark mode

Switch to dark mode to keep your eyes comfy and save energy. Dark mode offers a softer, darker background with light text and elements. Try it out!

How do I switch on dark mode?

  • When logged in, head to the top right corner of your page and click on the menu button .
  • Scroll to the bottom and tap the moon button .

How do I turn off dark mode?

Easy! Just click on the menu icon (top right corner of your page) and scroll all the way down. There, you’ll find a sun symbol . Give it a click, and you’re back to the bright side!

Does dark mode change what visitors see?

Switching to dark mode changes the look for you, not for your visitors. They’ll see your page just as they always have unless they have turned on dark mode. It’s your personal view that gets the cool, dark theme!

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send a request our way. 💌

Updating your Ko-fi account email address

Creators can update their Ko-fi login email whenever they like. This article covers changing the account email address, fixing a typo, and switching from social login to email login.

How creators change their Ko-fi account email address

Need to update a Ko-fi account email? No problem! Creators can follow these steps:

  • Head over to the Account Settings page.
  • Click Change your email address.
  • Enter the new email address, then confirm it in the Confirm your new email address field.
  • Note
  • If Ko-fi spots a typo in a common email domain (like “gmial.com” instead of “gmail.com”), Ko-fi suggests a fix so the confirmation lands in the right inbox.
  • Click Send confirmation email. Ko-fi sends a confirmation email from ko-fi@ko-fi.com. Creators should double-check the registered email address mentioned in it before confirming.
  • When the email arrives, click Confirm this change to finalize the process.
  • Once the change is confirmed, Ko-fi sends an email to the creator’s original address titled “Your Ko-fi email has been changed successfully” to confirm the change is complete.
  • Return to Account Settings, give the page a quick refresh, and check that the account email has been updated.

That’s it! The account email is now up to date. 🎉

How to fix a mistake after changing the account email

Typos happen, and creators can fix them themselves in seconds.

Haven’t confirmed the change yet? If a creator spots a mistake after clicking Send confirmation email, the creator can head back to Account Settings and enter the correct address. Ko-fi sends a fresh confirmation email to the right inbox.

Already confirmed the change to the wrong address? Creators should check their original inbox for the email titled “Your Ko-fi email has been changed successfully” and click Undo this change to revert it right away.

How to fix a typo in the email used at sign-up

If there’s a typo in a creator’s email at sign-up, the creator can simply register a new page using the correct email address. If the email hasn’t been verified, the preferred username should still be available.

Creators who need a hand can feel free to reach out to support.

Changing the account email for creators who signed up using X (Twitter), Google, Facebook, or Twitch

Want to keep logging in with a social account, but receive emails somewhere else?

Creators can add an email address in Account Settings following the steps above. The creator continues to log in using their social media account, and all emails and notifications are sent to the newly added address.

Switching from social media login to email login

Creators who’d like to log in using their email address rather than their social media account need to set up a password and login email for their Ko-fi account. Creators can set both from Account Settings.

What to do if a creator has lost access to their Ko-fi account email

Don’t worry, most email mix-ups can be fixed without waiting for support. Before reaching out, creators can check if one of these gets them back on track:

  • Recently changed the account email and it’s not verified yet? Head to Account Settings and re-enter the correct address. Ko-fi re-sends the confirmation.
  • Change already confirmed? Look for the “Your Ko-fi email has been changed successfully” email in the original inbox and click Undo this change.
  • Signed up with X, Google, Facebook, or Twitch? Creators can still log in with their social account and update the account email in Account Settings.

Still locked out? Creators can reach out to support with the following details, and Ko-fi Support will get it sorted:

  • Ko-fi page URL
  • Registered Ko-fi page email address
  • Preferred new email address

The preferred new email must not already have a Ko-fi page associated with it. If it does, the creator needs to delete the page linked to that email first.

Once the request is submitted, the Ko-fi Support team reviews the information or reaches out to carry out some checks. These checks can take up to 7 days to make sure the account changes hands safely.

What should a creator do after an unexpected email about an account email change?

Received an email about an account email change that the creator didn’t make? Here’s what to do:

  • If the creator is still logged in, reset the password right away. Then head to Account Settings and change the account email back.
  • If the change was confirmed without the creator, use the Undo this change button in the email sent to the original address.
  • If the account may have been compromised, check out the guide on compromised accounts for further help.
  • Can’t access the account? Reach out as soon as possible, and Ko-fi Support will help recover it.

We’re here if creators need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

Why Ko-fi asks for your location

When creators earn on Ko-fi, some pay us a small service fee or a monthly subscription fee. Like any business, Ko-fi has to account for sales tax or VAT on its own income, and the amount of tax we owe on that fee depends on where you, our customer, are based.

To calculate this correctly, we need to know your country. For creators in the US and Canada, we also need your ZIP or postal code.

For clarity, your location has nothing to do with the payments you receive from your supporters, which always go directly to you. It also doesn’t replace or affect your own tax responsibilities as a creator.

Note

Your supporters’ payments are never held or processed by Ko-fi. They go straight to your own connected PayPal or Stripe account.

View or update your location

Your location details are stored in your account settings. You can view or update them at any time.

  • Head to your Account settings
  • Find the Your location section and click View or update
  • You’ll be asked to confirm your identity with a 6-digit code sent via your chosen verification method. If you haven’t set up 2FA yet, you’ll receive this by email
  • Once verified, your current location details will be displayed. Click Update location to make changes
  • In the Update your location pop-up, select your Country from the dropdown. Click Save when done

Note

If you’re based in the US or Canada, you’ll also be asked to enter your ZIP or postal code.

Your data and privacy

Ko-fi collects your country (and ZIP or postal code for US and Canada-based creators) to calculate the tax Ko-fi owes on its own service and subscription fees. Here’s how we handle it:

  • Your country is stored securely and is not displayed on your public Ko-fi page
  • It is only shared with tax authorities where required by law, and only in an anonymized form that cannot be traced back to you individually
  • If you delete your account, your data is anonymized and cannot be attributed to you

For full details on how Ko-fi handles your personal data, see our Privacy Policy.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

FAQ

Why am I being asked for my location now?

Ko-fi is required to account for sales tax or VAT on its own service and subscription fees. The amount owed depends on where you’re based, so we need to collect your location to calculate this correctly.

Will my location be visible on my Ko-fi page?

No. Your location details are never displayed on your public Ko-fi page.

What if I entered my location incorrectly?

You can update your location at any time in your Account settings. Find the Your location section, verify your identity, and click Update location to make corrections.

Can I delete my location data?

If you delete your Ko-fi account, your data is anonymized and can no longer be attributed to you. Note that anonymized data may be retained for tax reporting purposes as required by law.

When do I need to add my location?

You’ll only need to add your location if you’re prompted to do so. If you haven’t seen a prompt, no action is needed right now.

Adding content

Create a Poll on Ko-fi

With polls, you can ask your supporters what they’d like to see next from you, how they feel about your latest work, or whatever else you’d like to know! This guide will show you how to set up your poll for supporters, members or everyone. Polls are a fantastic way to boost engagement and connect with your community.

How Polls work

Simply draft your question, set your choices, and decide who can vote—everyone or just those who have supported you before. You can also determine how long the poll will run. Once the poll ends, we’ll notify you and all the participants.

Set up your Poll

Ready to engage your audience with a poll? Here’s how to get started:

Go to Polls (Your about page > Add Something > Create Poll) to set up your poll.

Create Poll settings Polls post

  • Input the question you want to ask your supporters.
  • Add a description or some more context around your poll.
  • Add up to 50 choices for your poll.
  • Allow multiple answers for your poll.
  • Set your poll length.
  • Add a featured image to your poll. Use an image ratio of 2:1 or 600x300 for best results.
  • Decide which audience can participate in your poll. More details on this below.
  • You can publish your poll immediately, schedule it for later, or save it as a draft if you’re not ready to go live.
  • Manage any scheduled posts or drafts whenever you need.

Tip

Poll results always show percentages unless you toggle the option “Show vote results as a number”.

You as the creator will always see both the number of votes and the percentages when viewing the poll.

🎉 Voilà! Your poll is now set up and ready for sharing!

Note

Once your poll is published, you won’t be able to edit the question or the choices.

Share your Poll

Once your poll is live, use your link to share it far and wide.

When you share your link, you’ll have a social preview ready. Share your poll on your socials to drive more engagement!

Choose who can vote on your poll

Choose who gets to participate in your poll:

  • Everyone: Open to all, but voters need a Ko-fi account.
  • All Monthly Supporters: Exclusively for those who support you monthly.
  • Members: Specific membership tiers to ensure the right people have access.

What happens when your poll ends?

As soon as your poll wraps up, we’ll send a notification to your followers who voted, liked, or commented and also send them an email. You’ll also receive an email when your poll ends.

Can I end a poll early?

Absolutely! If you need to wrap up your poll sooner than planned, just head to your poll, click ‘Edit’, and then ‘End Poll’. To shorten the duration, select your new end time and hit ‘Save’.

Got questions or need a hand setting up your first poll? We’re here to help. Feel free to submit a request our way. 💌

FAQ

I’ve made an error on my poll

If you have made an error, you can edit your poll (Head to your poll > Click three dots > Edit), you can change the audience, description, and duration.

Can a voter change their vote?

Once a vote has been cast, it cannot be changed.

Do my followers, supporters, or members get notified when I post a poll?

Yes, if they are eligible to vote, they will receive an email invitation to participate in your poll from hello@ko-fi.com.

How can I find out when my poll ends?

You can check the exact ending time of your poll by navigating to your Poll > Edit. The end time will be displayed in blue.

Are polls anonymous?

Currently, you won’t find out who voted on your poll.

Creating posts? Let’s make them shine! ✨

Posts are your go-to magic tool for sharing all sorts of updates. Whether it’s making an announcement with a quick update, sharing a captivating story through a blog post, showcasing your latest work-in-progress with an image post, or giving a sneak peek into your creative process with a behind-the-scenes video or a snippet of your newest audio track.

Use Ko-fi Posts to:

👋 Introduce your page

🎁 Offer rewards

🔗 Share links to your cool stuff

📣 Give updates on your projects

🔒 Offer early access

🎧 Share exclusive audio clips

🏆 Explain your goal

🎞️ Show your creative process through video

This article will guide you through the different kinds of posts you can make, how to create them, and how to share with your audience.

ko-fi.com/leafbear/posts

5 Different types of posts on Ko-fi

When posting, you’ve got 5 types to choose from:

Quick Updates are great for sharing a quick note about what’s new with you.

Blog Posts are perfect for longer stories. Mix in some images and videos to make it more entertaining.

Image Posts are similar to a blog post but spotlights the image you upload. Share a finished drawing or a works-in-progress. Any image you upload goes to your gallery. (Upload limits: 25mb for free users and 60mb for contributors.)

Video Posts embed your videos from YouTube, Vimeo, Twitch, or Instagram. Makes it a breeze for followers to watch right from your posts tab.

Audio Posts feature your tunes or podcast directly on Ko-fi or embed tracks from SoundCloud, MixCloud, or Spotify. Let listeners stream directly from your posts tab.

Quick UpdateBlog PostImage PostVideo PostAudio Post

webby’s quick post

ArtSirenMJ’s blog post

marcmakescomics image post

Stylusboy’s video post

28 Dales Later’s audio post

Creating your post

Ready to make your first post? Here’s how:

  • Log in and head to your page.
  • Click the Create button, then Add Something.

Pick the type of post you want to make. Except for quick posts which get posted right away, they’re pretty similar in setup. Each type shines a spotlight on your chosen media:

  • Image Post - Your image takes center stage.
  • Blog Post - It’s all about your words.
  • Video - Your embeds (from platforms like YouTube or Vimeo) get the spotlight.
  • Audio - Your exclusive audio, ready to play.

Formatting your post

Once you’ve chosen your media, you’ll land on the post editor page.

Post Editor On your Ko-fi page

  • The title describes what your post is about. It’s also used to create the link to the post and pops up when shared on social media.
  • Body content is where you add the main content of your post.
  • Add a featured image using the browse button and add an image. Your image will display at the top of the article, in previews, and on social shares. For your post to look its best, try to upload an image with an aspect ratio of 2:1 or 600 x 300px.
  • Embedded content URL is where you paste in links of your videos (like YouTube) or audio (think SoundCloud).
  • Use the toolbar to control the styling of your text and layout, add links, embed videos and images from here.
  • Label your post with tags. They’re not for searches now, but will be handy when we improve the tag function.
  • Pick the audience who sees your post from everyone, monthly supporters, or specific membership tiers (more info below).

Either press publish, click the arrow to save a draft or schedule the post to go live at a point in the future.

Voila 🎉 your post is now live.

Note

You can always edit your posts by tapping your post and selecting the pencil and notepad icon . Just remember, quick updates can’t be edited.

Preview, publish, draft or schedule a post

If you are not quite ready to publish your post, you’ve got a few choices at the bottom right of the editor. Click the down arrow to:

  • Publish now: Publishes your post straight away.
  • Schedule: Pick a date and time, and let Ko-fi post it for you later. (more info below)
  • Preview: Take a sneak peek at how your post will look live.
  • Save draft: Saves your post as a draft until you decide to post it. Note that “Save draft” only appears if you’ve disabled Autosave.

And there you have it, your post is all set!

Note

To find your drafts or scheduled posts, just click “See drafts & schedule”. On mobile, tap “Add something” then “See drafts & schedule”. On desktop, it’s under the posts tab.

Share your posts

Once your post is live, any followers or supporters will be notified on their Ko-fi page, they will also get an email. Don’t forget to share it with your followers on social.

Share a post by going to the post you want to share, scroll to the bottom of your post. Then press the link button to copy your link or publish directly to X or Facebook.

If you’ve added a featured image, it’ll show up as the preview image when you share it on social media. If not, no worries – Ko-fi’s got a preview image ready for you.

Remember, sharing is caring! Keep your fans in the loop with your latest updates.🌟

Adding images to your post

You can add images to a blog post via the plus button on mobile and then the image symbol on your toolbar.

You can add an image to your post via an image URL or from your files. You can use popular formats like JPG, JPEG, GIFs or PNG. Images added to blog posts should be under 10MB.

Once your images are uploaded, you can resize your image, add alt-text, links or even caption your image. When your image is posted, it will also appear in your gallery.

Multi-image posts

With multi-image posts, you can add up to 8 pics to a single post and let viewers swipe through. Its a nice way to display works in progress or share your creative process, and display your work!

ko-fi.com/aliciayellowlodge

How to create a multi-image post?
  • Tap ‘Add Something’ (or on desktop, ‘Create’ > ‘Post Something’) > choose ‘Image’ > select up to 8 images.
  • Add a title, description, and choose your audience (Everyone, Supporters, or Members if you’ve set up Membership Tiers). Tap ‘Post images’!
  • See your post on your feed and share it on social!

Creating albums

Albums help you keep your gallery tidy and make it easier for supporters to browse your work. Every image you upload appears in your main gallery, so albums are a simple way to group related images together.

To create an album, tap the folder icon.

Add a title, write a short description, and choose whether you want a gallery or comic layout. (These settings can be edited at any time.)

Adding images to your album

You can add images to an album in two ways:

Upload new images to an album

  • Open the album you want to add to.
  • Click Add image.
  • Upload your new image as normal.

Move existing images into an album

  • Go to your Gallery.
  • Click the image you want to move.
  • Select the three dots.
  • Choose Move to album.

Setting your album order

Pick which albums appear first by adjusting the order in your Gallery.

Tap the Sort button and choose the order that works best for you.

You can sort your albums in a few different ways:

  • Newest: Shows your albums from newest to oldest.
  • Last updated: Albums you updated most recently will appear first.
  • Most items: Albums with the most images are shown at the front.
  • Custom ordering: Drag and drop your albums into any order you like.

Tip

Pin an important album to the top of your gallery so it’s always easy to find.

Tap the pin icon on any album, and it will appear before all others.

You can only have one pinned album at a time.

Adding links to your post

Want to point your fans to another post or let them download content? Adding links to your posts is super easy. Whether it’s your latest social media update, a cool blog, or a downloadable file, here’s how to do it:

  • Select the text you want to turn into a link in your post.
  • Click the Link icon.
  • Paste in the URL and voila – your text is now a clickable link!

Want it to open in a new tab? Just check the “Open in new tab” option.

Tip

Use Dropbox or Google Drive to share files or folders. Just add the link to your post using the link feature.

Embed a video in your post

Got a cool video to share? Embedding videos in your posts is super easy, whether it’s a YouTube vlog, a creative Instagram reel, or a Twitch stream highlight, here’s how to do it:

  • Hit the plus icon, then choose the video icon in your toolbar.
  • Paste your shareable URL from platforms like YouTube, Vimeo, Instagram, TikTok, Dailymotion, or Twitch.

You can even set your video to autoplay when your post is clicked.

Need more details? Check out these handy guides:

How to share a Vimeo video

How to use Ko-fi with Youtube

How to use Ko-fi with Instagram

Bring your posts to life and keep your supporters engaged with awesome video content! 🎥✨

Upload your audio post

Want to share a new track, podcast episode, or voice note? You can upload audio or embed it from another platform.

  • From your page, click Create > Post Something > Audio.
  • Upload your file (if you’re a Contributor) or paste a link from SoundCloud, MixCloud, or Spotify.

Supporters can hit play and listen right from your post.

Select an audience for your posts

You get to choose who can see your posts. Maybe you want to give a special sneak peek to your supporters or offer exclusive content to those who join your membership tiers.

Here’s how you can set your audience:

  • Everyone - open to all. Anyone can view this post.
  • All monthly supporters - everyone giving recurring tips or members of any tier.
  • Tier members - select the minimum tier for access. Higher tiers automatically get access too.

Feel free to change the audience for your posts whenever you like.

Want more info on setting your audience? Check out these handy articles:

  • Offering supporter-only content
  • Ko-fi Memberships and Membership Tiers
  • How memberships work alongside monthly supporters

Schedule your posts

Want to post at specific time but can’t be there to hit ‘publish’? No worries, schedule it! You can set your posts to go live at a specific date and time in the future.

Note

The schedule is based on your local time zone. For example, if you’re in the UK and schedule a post for October 31st at 23:00 GMT, it will show up for your US fans on October 31st at 19:00 CST.

Managing your scheduled posts is easy. Just head to “See drafts & schedule” under your posts tab. Here, you can reschedule, publish early, or even delete your upcoming posts using the handy three-dots menu.

Scheduling gives you the freedom to post when your audience is most active, even if you’re off doing other awesome things. Set it and forget it! 📅✨

Delete a post

You might need to remove a post after it’s been published. No stress, just find the post you want to delete. You’ll see three dots on the post preview – click on them and choose “Delete”.

Remember, once a post is deleted, it’s gone for good. So make sure it’s really the one you want to say goodbye to! 🗑️✨

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send a request our way. 💌

FAQ

What do tags do in posts?

Right now, think of tags as placeholders in your posts. In the future tags could be used to organize your content or help new people discover you. Keep using tags in your posts, and stay tuned for updates!

Are my supporters notified when I post something?

Yep, they sure are! If your supporters are part of the audience you’ve set for your post, they’ll get notified on their page and by email. For instance, if your post is for ‘everyone’, all your followers and supporters will know about it. But if it’s just for your top tier, only those members will get the heads-up.

Can I upload videos directly to Ko-fi?

Not just yet. For now, upload your videos to a supported platform (YouTube, Twitch, TikTok, Dailymotion, Instagram, or Vimeo) and then share the link or embed it in your Ko-fi post.

Can I upload audio directly to Ko-fi?

Yes! If you’re a Contributor, you can upload audio files directly to your posts, and supporters can listen right on your page. Not a Contributor? No worries! You can still share audio by embedding tracks from SoundCloud, MixCloud, or Spotify.

What is the size limit of audio posts?

The maximum file size is 200 MB for audio posts.

Can I edit a quick update?

Not at the moment. Once a quick update is out there, you would have to delete it if something isn’t quite right.

How do I save my posts automatically?

Autosave is switched on when you start a new post. It saves your progress every few seconds, so you don’t have to worry about losing your work.

If you see a green dot next to Draft, your post has been saved. You can close the tab at any time and find the post later in your drafts.

If you turn Autosave off, you’ll need to save your draft manually by clicking Save draft. If you leave the page without saving, your changes may be lost. Sometimes your browser can recover the post if you go back one page, but we recommend keeping Autosave on to make sure everything stays safe.

What features are coming to Ko-fi Posts?

We’re enhancing posts with features which allow you and your viewers to filter your posts. But here are some of your top requests:

  • Tools to organise posts
  • Tools to filter posts
  • RSS feed/ JSON/ Webhook for posts.

Stay tuned, and keep creating amazing content! 🚀🎨

How to share a SoundCloud track on Ko-fi

You can share music you’ve created, podcasts, interviews or just a recording of your thoughts with your Ko-fi followers or supporters directly from SoundCloud.

Sharing a SoundCloud Track on Ko-fi

  • Upload your audio file to SoundCloud
  • If you want to make the audio clip exclusively available on Ko-fi make sure you set the Privacy setting to “Private” (Optional)
  • Click save to publish your clip to SoundCloud
  • Choose to load your sharing link and copy the address of the link
  • Login to Ko-fi, go to your page and add an Audio Post
  • Paste your SoundCloud link into the Url field
  • Add a title and description for the post
  • Choose your audience. Recent supporters & subscribers includes people who’ve supported you in the last 30 days and subscribers (available if you have monthly subscriptions activated) or choose just subscribers to allow only monthly subscribers to hear the clip.
  • Click Preview Post
  • Share the link to your post wherever you’re active. We’ll also send your followers a message to let them know you’ve added a new post.

How to share a Vimeo video on Ko-fi

You can share tutorials, behind the scenes videos, recordings, speed paints or early access videos from Vimeo with followers, supporters or subscribers on Ko-fi.

How to Share a Vimeo Video on Ko-fi

  • Upload your video on Vimeo
  • If you want to make the video exclusively visible on Ko-fi make sure you set the Who can watch to “People with the private link” (Optional)
  • Click Share > Copy video link
  • Login to Ko-fi, go to your page choose Video Post
  • Paste your video link into the Url field
  • Add a title and description for the post
  • Choose your audience. Recent supporters & subscribers includes people who’ve supported you in the last 30 days and subscribers (available if you have monthly subscriptions activated) or choose just subscribers to allow only monthly subscribers to see the video.
  • Click Preview Post
  • Share the link to your post wherever you’re active. We’ll also send your followers a message to let them know you’ve added a new post.

How to share a YouTube video on Ko-fi

Share your exclusive videos and tutorials, behind the scenes content, event recordings, speed painting or early access YouTube videos with your Ko-fi followers, supporters or subscribers.

How to Share a YouTube Video on Ko-fi

  • Upload your video on YouTube

  • Make sure you set the visibility as Unlisted if you only want your Ko-fi audience to see it.

  • Copy the Video link

  • Login to Ko-fi, go to your page, then go to “Create” and “Post Something” then choose Video

  • Paste your video link into the Url field

  • Add a title and description for the post

  • Choose your audience. Recent supporters & members include people who’ve supported you in the last 30 days and anyone who supports you monthly.

  • You can choose to Publish your post or using the drop down preview your post.

You are able to share the link after you post your video. We’ll also send your followers a message to let them know you’ve added a new post.

Offering supporter-only content

Offering your fans exclusive content or perks encourages them to become supporters - it’s also one of the biggest reasons people join a monthly membership.

On Ko-fi, you’re not limited in the types of rewards you can offer supporters. Share exclusive posts, digital files, art commissions, Discord roles, physical items, or even discount codes. There’s so many fun ways to get your fans excited to support you! 😊

How to add supporter-only posts

  • On your page, choose Create > Post Something
  • Select a post type: Image, Blog Post, Video or Audio
  • Add your content to the post
  • In the Audience drop-down, select who should be able to see the content.
  • Tap the to share your post with fans. Sharing directly from Ko-fi is an easy way to remind your audience of what they get for supporting you.

Tip

‘Unlisted’ YouTube videos are a great way to share exclusive video content with your supporters. Learn More.

Let’s take a closer look at these audience options:
  • Everyone (Public): Anyone who visits your Ko-fi page can see this.
  • All Monthly Supporters (Members): Only people who support you every month can see this. One-time supporters can’t.

If you’ve set up Membership Tiers, you’ll see more options:

  • ‘Entry level’ and up: If you’re a member of any of the three tiers, you can see this. Monthly or one-time supporters can’t.
  • ‘Mid-level’ and up: You need to be in either the Mid-level or High Rollin tiers to see this. Everyone else can’t.
  • ‘High Rollin’ only: Only High Rollin tier members get to see this. No one else. 😊

Publishing your exclusive posts

When someone visits your Ko-fi page they’ll see your exclusive content in your page feed, your Posts, and your Gallery tabs. Only supporters of the right level will be able to unlock them.

How to add supporter-only shop or commission items

To pick who can buy or request your Shop or Commission items, head to ‘Who can buy/request this item’ when you’re editing the item. From there you can make some items exclusive to members of specific tiers or all monthly supporters.

Shop items can be digital or physical. You can also price them as free so they become a perk of being part of your Membership.

Commissions can’t be totally free right now, but you can set a special price just for your supporters. 😊

Everyone can see the exclusive items, but there will be a badge on them to show they’re just for members. If someone checks out your item but isn’t a member yet, we’ll nudge them to join so they can unlock it.

Some popular benefits to offer supporters

  • Exclusive content: Offer more posts, videos or other content only available to your biggest supporters.
  • Behind the Scenes: Give a sneak peek into your creative process.
  • Early Access: Share your newest creation before anyone else sees it.
  • Discord Roles: Offer exclusive channels and roles within your community.
  • Shout outs: Personal thank you’s on social, while you stream or in your videos.
  • Direct Messages: Chat directly with supporters, exclusively on Ko-fi.

For more ideas on what benefits to offer your supporters, check out our article “40 Benefits to Offer Your Members”. 😊

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

Frequently Asked Questions

How will supporters know when I post something new?

An email and Ko-fi notification (🔔) is sent to all of your followers, supporters and members.

When do I get paid?

You receive the money upfront and directly into your own PayPal or Stripe account.

How do I set up Membership Tiers?

Check out this article with everything you need to know!

How can I add a discount code just for supporters?

Just like choosing the audience elsewhere, when you create a discount code you can make it exclusive to monthly supporters or specific tiers. You can learn more here!

My supporter can’t see exclusive content, what should I tell them?

If a member can’t see your locked content, there are a few quick things to check first:

  • Make sure they used the same email at checkout and when creating their Ko-fi account
  • Ask them to claim their payment by following this guide
  • Have them check all inboxes (including spam) and alternative email addresses for the payment confirmation email

Still no luck? Ask them to contact us here and include:

  • A link to the creator they supported
  • The payment date and amount
  • The email used at checkout

We’ll help get things sorted as soon as possible!

What happens if I cancel Ko-fi Gold?

As long as you still have Memberships turned on you can still create and manage Supporter-only content. There will be a 5% service fee charged for these payments once your Gold subscription has ended.

Does this mean I have to publish to a schedule?

Absolutely not - there’s no commitment to publish content to any schedule.

Will supporters gain access to all previously posted content when they support?

Yes, once the payment from a supporter clears into your account, they’ll have access to all of the content within the tier they’ve joined and all content available on lower-priced tiers too.

Pinning a post

You can now pin a post to the top of your Feed and Posts tab so it’s the first thing your supporters see. Use a pinned post to highlight important updates, showcase your best work, promote new products, or share a welcome message. 📍

How to pin a post

Pinning a post is quick and simple - here are a few ways to do so.

Pinning from your Posts tab
  • Go to your Posts tab. The link will be ko-fi.com/[your username]/posts
  • Once you’ve located the post you want to pin, tap the three dots.
  • Select “Pin this post”. Now your supporters will see this post at the top of your Feed and Posts tab!

Tip

For more info about making posts, check out our guide here.

Pinning from your Feed

You can also pin a post from your Feed.

  • Go to your page and scroll to your Feed.
  • Once you’ve located the post you want to pin, tap the three dots.
  • Select “Pin this post”. This post will be pinned to the top of your Feed and Posts tab!

Note

You can only pin one post at a time on your Feed and Posts tab. Pinning a new post will replace the current pinned post in both places.

Pinning an image

Pin any type of post, including images.

  • Go to your Posts tab. You can also pin from your Feed.
  • Once you’ve located the image you want to pin, tap the three dots.
  • Select “Pin this post”. Voila! Your image will be the first thing that supporters see on your Feed and Posts tab.
Unpinning a post

Want to unpin a post? Just go to your pinned post, tap the three dots, and select “Unpin this post”.

You can also pin a different post to replace your previous one.

Uploading GIFs on Ko-fi

You can use GIFs in your Posts, Gallery, Commissions, Membership Tiers and Shop.

rys_jhncrl

How GIFs animate on Ko-fi

Ko-fi supports GIFs up to the usual image size limits—20MB for free accounts and 50MB for Contributor.

Here’s how GIFs animate on Ko-fi:

  • Under 250KB: Your GIF will animate in the feed.
  • Up to 4MB: Your GIF will animate when opened.
  • Over 4MB: The GIF will not animate on Ko-fi.

Can I use a GIF for my profile picture and cover image?

Currently, Ko-fi does not support GIFs as profile pictures or cover images.

My GIF size is too large to animate?

If your GIF is too large to animate, try resizing it using a GIF editor like ezgif.com.

  • Under 250KB: Your GIF will animate instantly without needing to be clicked.
  • Up to 4MB: Your GIF will animate when opened.
  • Over 4MB: The GIF will not animate on Ko-fi.

Can I sell GIFs?

Yes, when selling GIFs in your Ko-fi Shop, be sure to upload the highest quality file as the asset. Use a smaller GIF to animate your shop listing (you can add a watermark to the preview version).

Create discount codes & sales

Discount codes and sales let you offer a percentage discount on your entire Shop, specific shop categories, specific shop items, or Commissions.

Say thank you to your supporters, reward your members, or give your Shop a boost during holidays and seasonal events.

A discount code works for anyone who clicks your discount link or enters your code at checkout. Discounts can also apply automatically to all monthly supporters or to specific membership tiers. A sale is even simpler: the discount applies automatically to everyone who visits your Shop or Commissions, no code needed.

Discount codes and sales can both be scheduled with a start and end date, so you can set everything up ahead of time.

Create a discount code

Set up a discount code in just a few steps:

  • Go to More > Discounts and click Add discount.
  • Choose who gets the discount: by link & code, all monthly supporters, or a specific membership tier.
  • Choose Shop items or Commissions. For Shop items, pick all items, specific shop categories, or specific shop items.
  • Set your discount:
  • Your code can include letters, numbers, a period, or a hyphen. Codes are automatically converted to uppercase.
  • Percentage discount must be a whole number from 1 to 100 (no decimals). No need to add the % symbol.
  • Start date is optional. Leave the start date blank for the discount to work right away, or pick a future date to schedule it.
  • End date is optional too. Without an end date, the discount never expires. Discounts end at 23:59 in your time zone on the end date.
  • Click Create, and your discount is ready to share! You can edit or delete the discount at any time.

Start a sale

A sale applies a percentage discount automatically to everyone visiting your Shop or Commissions. Buyers don’t need a code or link, and discounted prices show on your page while the sale is live. Sales are perfect for flash sales, seasonal promos, and short-term offers.

  • Go to More > Discounts and click Start a sale.
  • Give your sale a name if you’d like, e.g. “Weekend sale”.
  • Choose Shop items or Commissions. For Shop items, pick all items, specific shop categories, or specific shop items.
  • Set the Percentage discount as a whole number from 1 to 100.
  • Optionally, add a Start date and End date.
  • Click Start sale, and you’re all set! ✨

Note

Only one sale can exist at a time, including scheduled sales. To schedule a new sale, wait for the current sale to end or select End sale. To offer different discounts at the same time, use discount codes.

How buyers can use your discount code

Buyers can use your discount code by clicking a shareable link or by entering the code at checkout.

By shareable link By discount code

By shareable link

Anyone who clicks your discount link gets the discount applied automatically to eligible shop items or commissions. A message confirms the discount, and prices update to show the savings.

By entering a discount code

Buyers can type your discount code at checkout when Let supporters enter a code at checkout is turned on. The setting is in the Advanced section at the bottom of the Discounts page.

When this setting is off, buyers can only use a discount by clicking your discount link. The setting doesn’t affect sales or member discounts, which apply automatically.

Note

Discounts apply to commission add-ons but not to shipping costs.

Share a discount code or sale

Every discount comes with a shareable image showing the percentage off, what the discount applies to, the code, and your name.

  • On the Discounts page, select Share on the discount you want to promote.
  • Pick a color for your image: dark, cream, blue, or gradient.
  • Share your discount:
  • Download saves the image to add to your own posts.
  • Copy link copies your discount link. Anyone who clicks the link gets the discount automatically.
  • The Facebook and X icons share directly to those platforms.

A few ideas for sharing your discount link or code:

📢 A social post

📧 An announcement in your newsletter

🔒 A members-only post with the code

📌 A pinned post on your page with details of your discounts

How discounts work for monthly supporters & members

Set up a discount for all monthly supporters, or offer one as a perk for joining specific membership tiers. A members-only discount is a great way to say thanks for sticking around.

Members don’t have to do a thing. Discounts apply automatically to any relevant item or commission while members are logged in.

Note

Disabling a membership tier in your Memberships settings stops new supporters from joining that tier, but current members of the tier keep their discount benefits. Deleting the tier removes the discount for everyone in it.

What if a buyer is eligible for more than one discount?

Only one discount applies per order, so discounts and sales don’t stack.

  • Discount links and codes override automatic discounts. A buyer who clicks a discount link or enters a code at checkout gets that discount instead of any active sale or member discount, even if the link or code is a lower percentage.
  • Otherwise, the highest automatic discount applies. For example, if a 15% weekend sale is running and a member has a 10% member discount, the member gets 15% off automatically.

Tip

To make sure your members always get the discount you intended, avoid running more than one automatic discount for the same membership tiers at the same time.

Manage or end a discount or sale

To end a discount early, select Edit and update the end date. To remove a discount completely, select the three dots > Delete, then confirm. A deleted discount stops working, and anyone who tries to use it will see “Sorry, that discount code is not recognized.”

To end a sale early, select End sale on the live sale row at the top of the Discounts page. Select Edit to change a running sale.

Can I offer a discount on a single item?

Yes! When creating a discount code or sale, choose shop items, then select the specific shop item you want to discount. The discount only applies to the items you select.

Tip

In your Shop settings, select the three dots on a listing > Create a discount. The discount form opens with that item already selected.

How discounts work with “Pay what you want” products

With “Pay what you want” turned on, buyers can choose to pay more than your minimum price. A discount only applies when the buyer pays the minimum price. If the buyer chooses to pay more than the minimum, the discount doesn’t apply.

We hope discount codes and sales help you reward your supporters and grow your Shop! Your feedback helps us improve, so please share your feedback on discounts.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

FAQ

Do discounts apply to shipping?

No. Discount codes and sales don’t apply to shipping costs.

Are discount codes single-use?

Discount codes can be used again and again until they reach their end date or are deleted. Usage limits aren’t available yet, but each discount shows how many times it has been used.

Can I offer 100% discounts?

Yes! Set the Percentage discount to 100. This works for digital and physical shop items. If a physical item is discounted to free and shipping is also free, the buyer checks out without payment and only enters shipping details.

Can I schedule a discount or sale to start later?

Yes. Set a future Start date when creating the discount code or sale, and the discount goes live on that date.

Can I offer a discount for multiple purchases or bundles?

Not at the moment. Discounts apply to a single purchase. If this is something you’d love to see, let us know!

How do I offer a discount on Memberships?

Discount codes and sales only apply to shop items and commissions. A membership can qualify a supporter for a discount, but membership payments themselves can’t be discounted.

How do I add tracking links to physical orders?

Adding tracking information for physical orders is a great way to keep your supporters in the loop about their purchase. You can add shipping tracking links for any physical orders you sell through Ko-fi Shop and Commissions.

Step-by-step guide

  • Head over to your Payments & Orders section. Filter to “Shop Orders (not shipped)” or “Commissions (not completed)” to find your pending orders.
  • Find the order you want to update and click on the three dots (…) beside it. This will open more options for that specific order.
  • Select “Mark as Shipped”. This option is where you’ll enter the tracking details.
  • In the provided field, enter the tracking link given by your courier. If a direct link is not available, click “or add tracking number” then input the tracking ID and courier name.
  • Tap “Mark as Shipped” again to complete the update.

Voila 🎉 your tracking link is added, and your order is marked as shipped.

What happens next?

Your supporter automatically receives an email with the tracking link or the courier details. This keeps them informed about the status of their order and reduces queries about where their order is.

How can I send tracking links for physical items for membership benefits?

Currently, for memberships, you’ll need to message your member with any tracking links. We’re working on making this better in the future.

Got questions? We’re here for you. Feel free to submit a request our way. 💌

How Memberships works with Monthly Supporters

Ko-fi Memberships let your biggest fans support you monthly with exclusive rewards. Already using Simple Monthly payments? Wondering about the differences or if you should switch? This guide will help you understand how Memberships work and the differences between the two options.

Definitions

For clarity, we’ll use these terms:

Monthly Supporters: Supporters who chose the ‘monthly’ option when they tip and pay you a recurring payment. Now known as ‘Simple monthly payment (no tiers)’ in the membership selection.

Tier Members: Supporters who became a member of a tier.

Will I lose my Monthly Supporters if I switch to Membership Tiers?

No, your existing Monthly Supporters will remain active, and payments will continue to be processed monthly as usual. However, Monthly Supporters will only have access to content under the audience view “All Monthly Supporters” and below. Read more about audience view in our guide.

Can I convert Monthly Supporters to Membership Tiers?

Yes, you can upgrade your Monthly Supporters to Membership Tiers. Follow the steps in this article.

Can I have Membership Tiers and Simple Monthly Payments switched on at the same time?

You can have either monthly payments or membership tiers active. Change your settings by navigating to Memberships > Memberships Mode and choose between the three options.

With Membership Tiers selected, if supporters choose monthly in the tip panel, they will see your tiers as options. You will also see a Membership tab added to your homepage.

If Simple Monthly Payments (no tiers) is active, supporters will be directed to pay the amount every month.

You can also turn off memberships completely and stick to one-time payments.

Can I create content for my Monthly Supporters and Membership Tiers?

Yes, you can choose the audience each time you add a new post or gallery image. Decide if your post is available only for specific membership tiers, or include your monthly supporters.

Let’s assume you have existing monthly supporters and new tier members in three tiers called Entry Level, Mid-level, and High Rollin’. Here’s a breakdown of what each audience option means:

If you want more of your audience to see the content you will select options closer to the top.

  • Everyone (Public): All visitors to your Ko-fi page, supporters and non-supporters, will see this content.
  • Note: This content will be available to members of tiers priced lower than your former monthly supporter (coffee unit) price.
  • All Monthly Supporters (Members): Monthly Supporters and Members of all tiers will see this content. One-off payments will not see this.
  • ‘Entry level’ and up: If you’re a member of any of the three tiers, you can see this. Monthly or one-time supporters can’t.
  • ‘Mid-level’ and up: You need to be in either the Mid-level or High Rollin tiers to see this. Everyone else can’t.
  • ‘High Rollin’ only: Only High Rollin’ tier members get to see this. No one else. 😊

To learn more about audience options click here.

I have Monthly Supporters, should I switch to tiers?

If you’re considering offering rewards to your monthly supporters (either now or in the future), we recommend creating a single membership tier rather than choosing “Simple monthly payments.” You’re likely to earn more by creating different membership tiers for your supporters and offering rewards. Rewards can be as simple as allowing DMs, giving a social shout-out, or making some exclusive content for members. Learn more here.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

You’ve added Ko-fi to your home screen.
