Set up shipping for your Shop

Creators can set two shipping prices for each physical item: one for solo purchases, and another for when the item ships as part of a larger order, called Price with others.

Shipping prices are calculated using the highest Shipping price in the order, plus the Price with others for each additional item.

To set your shipping prices, check your courier’s rates for each destination based on your item’s packaged size and weight. Learn how to calculate shipping prices.

Create a shipping profile

Shipping profiles let creators set shipping rates once and apply them to as many shop items as they like.

In Shop settings, scroll to the Shipping profiles section and click Create a profile to add a shipping profile.

  • Offer worldwide shipping: Good for global sales at a consistent rate, ideal for lightweight, easy-to-ship items like stickers. Specific destination prices override this rate.
  • Add destination: Continents or countries the creator ships to, each with its own rate. If worldwide shipping is off, only supporters from listed destinations can purchase.
  • Shipping information: Estimated delivery times, the shipping service used, and how supporters will receive tracking updates.

Tip

Many creators keep destinations simple by choosing continents rather than individual countries.

Once all the information is added, click Create profile to finalize and save the shipping profile.

Managing shipping profiles

Creators can edit or delete any shipping profile they’ve already created.

Shipping profiles that have already been created can be edited and deleted from the Shipping profiles section.

Adding a shipping profile to products

To assign a product to a shipping profile, click Assign on that shipping profile.

In the pop-up box, creators search for the item, select its checkbox, and click Assign product to complete the process. This automatically adds the shipping rates and costs from the shipping profile to the assigned products.

Request a phone number at checkout

Some couriers might need a phone number for delivery. When the phone number checkbox is ticked, supporters buying physical items must provide one at checkout. This setting is optional, so creators only need it if their courier asks for a phone number.

To turn this setting on, go to Shop settings and scroll to Additional settings. Ticking the checkbox saves the setting.

Got questions? We’re here for you. Feel free to submit a request our way. 💌

Upgrading Monthly Supporters to Memberships

You can now automatically upgrade existing monthly supporters to your membership tiers.

Memberships are a more flexible way of building a recurring income. They encourage monthly commitments and give creators more ways to offer benefits and rewards. Learn more about Memberships here.

Upgrade Monthly Supporters

The option to “Upgrade My Monthly Supporters” will appear if:

  • you have existing monthly supporters
  • you have set up at least 1 membership tier at a price point equal to or below the amount the monthly supporter paid

By clicking “Upgrade My Monthly Supporters”, all eligible supporters will be automatically assigned to the minimum tier available based on the amount they most recently paid.

Learn more about how Membership Tiers and benefits work in this article.

How will Monthly Supporters be notified of the upgrade?

Each monthly supporter will receive an email notifying them they are now a member of one of your tiers (we use your chosen tier name in the email).

How are tiers assigned?

Monthly supporters are automatically assigned to a tier based on the amount they most recently paid. The amount you receive from each supporter will not change when they become members.

Example: You have 3 monthly supporters you want to upgrade to Members.

  • Supporter A pays $1 per month,
  • Supporter B pays $9 per month,
  • Supporter C pays $12 per month.

If you set up $3, $5, $10, and $15 tiers and choose “Upgrade My Monthly Supporters”:

  • Supporter A remains a monthly supporter at $1 with access to content for “All Monthly Supporters”.
  • Supporter B joins the $5 tier, continues to pay $9 monthly, and gains access to content for All Monthly Supporters and $5 tier.
  • Supporter C joins the $10 tier, continues to pay $12 per month (tiers have a minimum price, not a fixed price).

Learn more about choosing an audience for your posts here.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

FAQ

Can monthly supporters join a tier?

Yes, but joining a tier doesn’t cancel their monthly payment. They need to cancel it separately. Point them to our guide for cancellation here.

Can converted members change their tier?

Yes, they can change tiers on your Memberships page. When changing tiers, price differences for upgrades or downgrades are not refunded at the point of purchase, although the previous tier will be canceled.

Can members change how much they pay?

Yes, they can change their pledge if it’s above the tier’s minimum price. You can point them to our guide here to change their pledge.

Changing the pledge doesn’t move them to a higher tier automatically; they must subscribe to the new tier.

What if I revert back to simply monthly tips?

Reverting affects only new subscribers. Existing members remain on their tiers until you or the member cancel the subscription. Follow our guide here.

What is Ko-fi Gold?

Ko-fi has evolved a lot over the years, and today, every creator can use all of Ko-fi with a creator-friendly 0-5% service fee. That means you can:

  • Accept tips
  • Sell products & commissions
  • Build a membership
  • Share exclusive content
  • Customize your page

…all without needing a subscription!

Note

Consider becoming a Contributor to share 5% of your tips income with us, to help us run the service and unlock cool perks!

A fairer way to earn on Ko-fi

We’ve been listening to creators, and we realized the Gold model wasn’t working for everyone. We noticed that:

  • Many creators felt they had to pay for Gold to use Ko-fi, which isn’t true! Now, every creator can access all tools without a monthly cost.
  • Many Gold creators weren’t earning enough to cover the subscription, while others were earning $1000’s per month, generating lots of help center enquiries from supporters and consuming 100’s of times more resources, but only paying $8. Both of these felt wrong.
  • For most creators, we think the service fee model makes the most sense. You only pay a fee when you earn money.

Ko-fi Gold is still available

Ko-fi Gold is now available for $12/month for new users. This means:

  • If you already have Gold, nothing changes - your subscription will continue to renew as usual at the price you already pay.
  • If you decide to cancel or your plan expires, you’ll move to the contributor model and keep all of your existing benefits.
  • New creators can still sign up for Gold, but only on a monthly plan (annual plans are no longer available).

We’re always listening to creators and looking for ways to make Ko-fi better. We’ll continue improving to make Ko-fi as creator-friendly as possible.

Frequently Asked Questions

What happens if I already have Ko-fi Gold and want to keep it?

Your current Gold subscription will continue to renew either monthly or annually depending on your subscription type.

If you cancel your subscription or it fails to renew you will automatically be switched over to the contributor model so you don’t lose any of your Gold benefits.

What happens if I cancel my Ko-fi Gold membership? Will I lose all my benefits?

Once you cancel your Ko-fi Gold subscription, you will see a confirmation that the subscription will not renew. You will keep all of your Gold benefits until the end of your billing cycle.

Once you’ve reached the end of your billing cycle you’ll become a contributor which maintains your Gold features but instead of a monthly subscription, future transactions will incur a 5% service fee (excluding memberships started while you were Gold).

What if I want to switch off Gold now?

Sure, you’re totally welcome to switch over to the Contributor option. All you need to do is cancel your Gold membership from your account settings.

From the Ko-fi Menu select More > Account Settings, then in subscriptions simply click Cancel. You will keep Gold until the end of your payment period, but your subscription will not renew.

Note, any recurring tips or memberships created with a 0% service fee while you were a Gold subscriber will continue to enjoy the same 0% service fee until they cancel.

What is Ko-fi Contributor?

Consider becoming a Contributor to share 5% of your tip income with us to help us run the service and unlock these cool perks:

  • Change the “coffee” metaphor to… anything you like
  • Claim a shorter Ko-fi username (3 letters and up)
  • Choose a custom color for your page
  • Get insights about your page with Google Analytics
  • Support our team to help us build a better Ko-fi!

What if I’m a Contributor and then I sign up to Gold?

If you’re a Contributor and then subscribe to Gold, you’ll need to turn off ‘Contributor’ status to stop paying the 5% service fee.

Memberships which began before you subscribed to Gold will continue to incur the 5% service fee.

How can I check my subscription duration?

Go to the Ko-fi Menu, select More > Account Settings, and look under “subscriptions”. There, you’ll see the status of your Gold membership. If you pay monthly, the start date of your membership and its monthly renewal date will be displayed. If you pay annually, you’ll see the date when your subscription is set to renew.

Can I update my payment card?

Yes, you can, the process depends on your payment method:

If you paid via card: You can update your payment card details on Ko-fi.

  • Navigate to the Ko-fi Menu.
  • Select More > Account Settings.
  • Look under “subscriptions.”
  • Select “update card.”

Your new card will be charged when your subscription renews.

If you paid via PayPal: You need to update your payment method directly on PayPal.

  • Log into your PayPal account.
  • Click your name in the top right corner.
  • Select “Account Settings.”
  • Click on “Money, Banks and Cards.”
  • Scroll down to “Pre-approved Payments.”
  • Select “Manage Pre-approved Payments.”
  • Choose the subscription for which you want to change the funding source.
  • Click on “Funding Source.”
  • Select the card you want to use.
  • Confirm your choice.

Your new card will be charged when your subscription is due.

Please note that it isn’t possible to switch between card payment and PayPal. If you wish to change your payment method, you would need to cancel your existing subscription and rejoin after that subscription expires.

Is Ko-fi Gold available to new creators?

Yes, you can still join Ko-fi Gold to unlock 0% service fees on all payment options for a fixed monthly cost of $12 p/m, rather than the 5% fee. You’ll also be able to post supporter-only content, schedule posts, change the coffee metaphor and choose a custom page color.

Sign up to Ko-fi Gold here (linked in the last FAQ on this page)

Customizing your page

Change your social preview image and text

You can change the preview image and text that shows on social sites when you share your page. Change it to reflect your own style, tip drives, new rewards or goals!

Tip

Having trouble getting your new preview and text to show up on social? Try adding a “/” or “?” to the end of your URL to nudge those platforms to grab your latest preview image. It may take a few days for the changes to take effect. You can also clear your device’s cache to speed up the process.

How to change your preview image

Head over to your page settings and under “Set your preview image and text” select “Customize preview”.

For the best social previews, choose images with a 2:1 aspect ratio or a size of 600 x 300px. You can use popular file formats like JPEG or PNG.

Hit “Done,” and voila! 🎉 Your social preview is ready.

Tip

Did you know you can customize social previews for specific links, like your shop or commissions? For example, when you share ko-fi.com/yourpage/shop, you can create a unique preview tailored just for your shop, enticing visitors to check out your awesome creations!

Do I have to change my social previews?

Not at all! Ko-fi will generate a social preview for you, so you don’t have to change it if you don’t want to.

Can I get the Ko-fi default social preview back?

Yes, simply head to your page settings, select “Customize preview,” and delete the image you set by selecting “x” at the top right corner of the image. Press done and after a few minutes, the Ko-fi default image will reappear.

I’ve changed my username and my old name is still on my preview?

Head to your page settings, select “Customize preview,” and delete the image set by selecting “x”. Press done and after a few minutes, you’ll see the new Ko-fi default image with your updated username.

We love seeing your custom preview images! Share your page and tag @kofi_button on Facebook or X!

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

Make your page stand out with a cover image

Your cover image is the first thing visitors will see on your page. It’s a fantastic way to express your unique style or brand. Plus, pages with a cover image tend to get more support. 🎨

Prep your image:

Keep these in mind when creating your cover image:

  • Aim for a 3:1 ratio, ideally 1200x400 px.
  • Keep it under 8mb.
  • Use popular formats like JPEG, JPG, or PNG.

Add your cover image

Once you have your perfect image ready:

- Head over to [your page](https://ko-fi.com/manage/mypage) and click the three dots in the top right corner. Then, choose "Set Cover Image".
- Drag and drop your image or click the box to pick one from your device.
- Select your image and... voilà! You're all set!

Note

Remember, changing or removing your cover image is easy. Just click those three dots again and select “Set cover image”.

Will it look good on mobile and desktop?

Your cover image will look great on both. It might get slightly cropped on the sides on mobile, but you don’t need to resize it for different devices.

Mobile Desktop

Text in your cover image

Try to keep your text away from the edges as images may be cropped on mobile views.

Set your status

Your status lets supporters know what you’re up to at a glance, whether you’re open for commissions, taking a break, or heads down on something. Your status shows on your page, at checkout, and in your messages, so fans always know what to expect before they reach out.

How to set your status

  • From your Ko-fi page, tap Set a status next to your profile picture.
  • Type your status, or choose from a quick option like Commissions open, On holiday, Super focused, or Out sick. Your status can be up to 40 characters, and you can change the emoji by tapping on it.
  • Choose when your status should clear under Clear after: Today, 3 days, 1 week, 2 weeks, a specific date, or Don’t clear if you’d rather remove it yourself.
  • Tap Save.

Note

Setting a status is optional, and you can change or remove it at any time from the same menu.

Where your status appears

Your Ko-fi status appears in three places: on your Ko-fi page next to your name, at checkout when a supporter is completing a payment, and in your messages before a supporter sends you one.

See how creators use statuses

Here’s a few ways creators are using their status to keep supporters in the loop:

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

FAQ

Does setting a status pause my Commissions or Shop?

No. Your status is just a visual signal to supporters, and it doesn’t change your availability settings. Learn how to manage your commission slots if you need to pause new requests.

What happens when my status clears?

Your status is removed automatically at the time you chose under Clear after, and your profile simply shows no status until you set a new one.

Can I use an emoji in my status?

Yes, you can add an emoji alongside your custom status text.

How long can my Ko-fi status be?

Your status can be up to 40 characters.

Can supporters set a status too?

No, status is a creator-only feature. Supporters don’t have a status and won’t see an option to set one.

Setting up your social links

Adding your social links to your Ko-fi page helps fans learn more about you, follow your work, and stay up to date across all your platforms. You can add up to 15 links, including social profiles and custom URLs.

How to add a social link

You can add a link to Instagram, X, YouTube, or any other site you use! Just follow these steps:

  • Go to your Profile settings.
  • Scroll to Your social links.
  • Add the link to your social profile or website, then tap Save.
  • That’s it! Your link will appear in the About section on your profile.

Tip

Make sure your link starts with “https://”. If a supported site’s icon doesn’t appear or the link doesn’t work, check for typos or missing parts of the URL.

Custom links or unrecognized sites will show a standard link icon. If you add several custom links, they’ll appear in a drop-down menu.

Removing a social link

To remove an icon or URL from your profile:

  • Go to your Profile settings.
  • Scroll to Your social links.
  • Select Remove next to the link you want to delete.
  • Click Confirm to remove the link from your page, then Save.

If the wrong link is still showing, remove the link and save your changes. Then refresh your page and add the correct link.

Note

If you connect your X, Twitch, or YouTube account to Ko-fi, those links will be set for you automatically. If you later unlink one of these accounts, the icon won’t be removed, so you’ll need to update or delete the link yourself.

Looking for ways to use Ko-fi on social? Check out these articles 👉 Promoting your Ko-fi…

How many social links can I have?

You can add up to 15 social links. Feel free to include all your social profiles and any custom sites you want to share!

For sites like Bluesky, you will need to use the full link to your profile rather than just the handle.

What if I don’t like coffee?

Coffee is just a friendly metaphor used for giving and receiving support, there isn’t actually any coffee involved. Some people just prefer to ask fans to buy them a coffee rather than asking for a ‘tip’.

When someone buys you a ‘coffee’, you receive that sum of money which by default is set to $3 (change the unit price in Settings > Payment).

You get the money directly and instantly into your own PayPal or Stripe account.

Change ‘coffee’ to just about anything

You can change the metaphor from coffee to something that suits you better in Settings > Payment.

We’ve seen everything from Tea, Pizza and Cola to some really fun ideas. You can:

  • Buy a Ramen for Chaokobon
  • Buy a Magic Bean for illusbyjo
  • Buy a Protein Shake 💪🏾 for Elle

What is the coffee counter?

The coffee counter displays how many “coffees” you’ve been bought. For example, if your unit price is set to €1 and you receive a €5 tip, your counter will increase by 5 coffees.

You can turn it on/off from Settings > Page

How to turn off the coffee metaphor

If you think your supporters might find the metaphor confusing or if you would just prefer a simpler experience, you can switch to tip mode from Settings > Payment.

The payment tile on your page will read ‘Support YourName’ instead of ‘Buy a coffee for YourName’. Switch payment settings any time you like.

If you’re having trouble with your coffee metaphor, you can reach out to us here. 💌

Getting started

Beginner FAQs

Just getting started on Ko-fi? Here are some quick answers to help you earn, share your page, and make the most of Ko-fi’s features. ❤️

How do I start earning on Ko-fi?

Start turning your passion into income by connecting a payment method in your Payment Settings.

You can link PayPal, Stripe, or both!

Do I need to wait for a payout on Ko-fi?

Nope! Ko-fi is a direct payment platform, which means your payments go straight from your supporters to your PayPal or Stripe account.

No payout delays - get paid instantly. ✨

What fees does Ko-fi charge?

Ko-fi takes a simple 5% service fee when you use premium features like:

  • Memberships
  • Monthly tips
  • Shop
  • Commissions

You’ll start with Contributor status, which unlocks everything from day one (including a 5% fee on one-time tips). You can opt out at any time for no fees on one-time tips.

How do I share my Ko-fi page to get support?

Share your Ko-fi link on social media, in your bio, email signature, or anywhere your audience connects with you. Check out our Promote your page for tips and templates.

Do I need a PayPal Business account?

Use either a personal or business PayPal account to accept payments on Ko-fi! A business account gives you more control over what information is shared with supporters. Learn more here.

Can I use Stripe in my country?

Stripe works in 40+ countries. Check where Stripe’s available here.

Note: A full Stripe account is needed to accept payments on Ko-fi. Stripe Express accounts aren’t available at this time.

How do I use Ko-fi’s features?

We’ve got step-by-step guides to help you set up everything. Start with:

  • Set your Ko-fi Goal
  • Offer Memberships with exclusive benefits
  • Sell items on your Ko-fi Shop
  • Open Commissions or Requests
  • Offer supporter-only content
  • Set up Stream Alerts

How do I set prices for my work?

We suggest considering the cost of materials and the time taken as a base point. Market research to see what similar items are offered for may help as well. Ultimately, it’s up to you to decide!

What information is shared on Ko-fi?

On Ko-fi, we collect only very basic details from creators, and we make sure we don’t share any more information than we need to.

By default, only your display name is shared. For shop and commission sales, your contact email is also shared (you can set this in your Shop settings and Commission settings).

Payment providers like PayPal and Stripe also share details during a transaction. Learn more here.

How do you pronounce Ko-fi?

Ko-fi is pronounced “Koh-fee” - here’s a clip!

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

Can I search for creators on Ko-fi?

Creators decide how and where their page is seen, with no algorithm deciding who sees their content.

It isn’t possible to search for creators directly on Ko-fi; instead, creators are in control of where their page is shared. For creators wanting fans to find their page, here are some ways to make it easy.

How creators can share their Ko-fi page

Sharing a Ko-fi page helps boost visibility and encourage support! Here are a few places creators can share it.

  • Pin the Ko-fi link in a post or story on X, Instagram, TikTok, Threads, Bluesky, or Mastodon.
  • Include the Ko-fi link in video or image captions, livestream overlays, or artwork credits.
  • Make it front and center in social media bios and personal site footers.
  • Some creators include their Ko-fi in their email signature. Others add a button to their website, or even create a custom QR code for easy access.
  • Promote the Ko-fi page with platforms like Twitch, Facebook, Medium, and Threads. See all the ways to promote a Ko-fi page.

How creators can make their Ko-fi page easy to find

  • Using the same handle on Ko-fi as on social platforms makes it easier for fans to find a creator’s page.
  • When a creator uses the same handle everywhere, fans can often reach the creator’s page by adding that handle to the end of a Ko-fi URL: ko-fi.com/handle
  • Supporters may enter a creator’s name or username alongside “ko-fi” in a search engine, which will usually bring up the creator’s page near the top of results.

Promote your Ko-fi page regularly

Creators can regularly share their Ko-fi link whenever they release new content, have a shop drop, or offer a commission slot. Don’t be shy about reminding fans. For more sharing ideas, check out 50+ creative ways to share a Ko-fi page.

Creators are in control. Happy sharing, and thanks for being part of the Ko-fi community!

Still have questions or need advice? Contact our support team. We’re always here to help!

Connect your Stripe account and start earning ✨

Stripe lets you accept direct payments by card, Apple Pay, and Google Pay all without leaving your Ko-fi page. It’s quick to set up and gives supporters a fast, secure way to pay.

To connect:

  1. Head to Settings > Payment

  2. Click Connect and follow the Stripe setup flow

Once connected, payments go straight to your Stripe account. You can manage payouts, refunds, and subscriptions from your Stripe Dashboard.

💡 Standard Stripe fees apply (around 3% + $0.30), and payouts typically arrive within 7 days.

Stripe offers a direct way for supporters to pay you via their bank card without leaving your page. With Stripe, supporters can also pay super fast using their card (including saved cards) Apple Pay or Google Pay. Connect your Stripe account with just a few clicks. ✨

You can check out the most popular payment methods accepted on Ko-fi here.

Connect your Stripe account

  • Open your page settings, then click on your ‘Payment’ tab
  • Click ‘Connect’ to start linking your Stripe account to Ko-fi
  • A new window will pop up to sign into your Stripe account.
  • If you don’t have a Stripe account, a new window will pop up, prompting you to sign up for a Stripe account. It’s free to create an account.
  • During sign-up, Stripe will ask for some details:
    • Account details: You’ll need to provide your email and set up a password.
    • Emergency backup code: You’ll be given a code in case your device is lost or stolen. Keep it safe!
    • Business or personal details: Stripe will need to know your location, whether you’re a business or an individual, and some personal info.
    • Professional details: Add information about your business or the content you offer. For the “Your Business” section under business details, provide a link to where your content can be found (for example, a website, portfolio, or social media page that features your work). You’ll also need to link a bank account for Stripe to send your payouts.
    • Public details: Choose the details your supporters will see when they complete a payment.
  • After providing all the necessary information, this will redirect you back to your Ko-fi page. Now successfully connected to Stripe, ready to accept payments. 🎉

Note

Stripe will send a verification email. Check your inbox to access your account.

How do I withdraw my money from Stripe?

Payments from your supporters go straight to your Stripe account. From there, you can transfer your funds to your chosen bank account - right away or automatically at your chosen date, known as a “payout”.

Stripe payouts

Payout schedules depend on your location and business type. Usually, your first payout will reach your bank account within 7 days. After that, it’s a rolling 2, 4, or 7-day schedule or you can even set it to transfer instantly for an extra fee (learn more here). You can check this out in the ‘Payout Settings’ of your Stripe Dashboard.

Note

To get your payouts, you need to add a bank account. This is covered when setting up a Stripe account. Learn more about it here.

The supporter experience using Stripe

By adding Stripe as a payment option to your page, supporters will see the option ‘Pay with Card’. With Stripe it’s simple and quick for supporters to pay you directly on your page with just their card or a number of other options.

Faster ways for supporters to pay with Stripe:

By adding Stripe as a payment option, your supporters will be offered more ways to pay. They can use Apple Pay, Google Pay, or securely save their card details for quicker checkouts across the web.

Apple Pay and Google Pay

For one-time payments, relevant supporters using an iOS device will see Apple Pay, and supporters using an Android device will see Google Pay.

If your Stripe account is connected and in good standing, but a supporter doesn’t see these fast checkout options, they should check:

  • They’re using a compatible device with the payment method activated (iOS for Apple Pay & Android for Google Pay).
  • They are making a one-time payment, commission, or shop item purchase. Apple Pay or Google Pay is not available for memberships or recurring payments.
Saved card details

Stripe also offers supporters the option to save their payment details for faster future checkouts. To save their details with Stripe, supporters simply select the option to save their payment details during the card payment process. A phone number is required to receive verification codes for completing payments, and Stripe will use the email used for payment to register the card details.

When the details are saved, each time supporters use the same email to pay you by card/stripe, a verification code will be sent for one-click checkouts.

Supporters can use the same email for faster checkouts if they see the “Link” payment option.

Saved card details apply to one-time payments, commissions, and shop items, just like Apple Pay and Google Pay options.

Supporters’ payment details are securely saved by Stripe and can be managed by the supporter through their link account. For more information, click here.

Disconnecting Stripe from Ko-fi

You can do this by heading to your Ko-fi Settings > Payment options, then clicking the “Connected” button to disconnect.

Remember, if you disconnect, you will stop taking on new payments with Stripe. However, recurring payments will remain active until they are cancelled by you or the supporter. (Learn more about cancelling subscriptions here.)

Note

If you toggle off “Accept Payments” anyone with a direct link to your commissions, membership tiers or shop, will still be able to pay you. Disconnecting Stripe is the way to stop accepting Stripe payments across all Ko-fi features.

Learn more about Stripe through their help centre. For any account creation issues, contact Stripe here.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

FAQ

Do I have to be a business to use Stripe?

Stripe is aimed for businesses of every size, it provides the option for Individual/Sole proprietors which may be appropriate to some creators.

Is Stripe available where I live?

Stripe is available in over 40 countries. Find out if it’s available to you here.

Please note that countries marked as “Extended Network” may not be eligible for a full Stripe Connect account.

Can I use Stripe Express on Ko-fi?

At the moment, Stripe Express isn’t currently supported on Ko-fi. You would need a Stripe Connect account to accept payments on Ko-fi.

Will I be able to see payments made to me using Stripe?

Yes, once you have a Stripe account you will have complete access to your own Stripe Dashboard. You can see payments, control payouts, make refunds and manage subscriptions created on Ko-fi. Your Stripe account isn’t limited to Ko-fi; you can use Stripe as an independent service.

How do payouts work on Stripe?

Payout schedules depend on the country you’re based in and your business type. Usually, your first payout will reach your bank account within 7 days. After that, it’s a rolling 2, 4, or 7-day schedule or you can even set it to transfer instantly for an extra fee (learn more here). You can check this out in the ‘Payout Settings’ of your Stripe Dashboard.

What fees does Stripe charge?

With Ko-fi, your earnings go straight into your own payment account. Stripe charges their regular transaction fees, which can vary depending on your account type, location, and the currencies used. Generally, it’s around 3% + $0.30.

Where can I find my tax forms on Stripe?

Remember, income you receive on Ko-fi is likely subject to income tax. You should familiarize yourself with your country’s tax rules regarding income.

You can download 1099-K forms from Stripe following these instructions, and for any tax invoices follow these instructions.

Plus, you can download all your Ko-fi payments as a CSV from Payment History.

What information is shared by Stripe to my supporters?

After a supporter makes a payment, Ko-fi will send an email confirmation to the supporter, and Stripe will send a receipt. The transaction will also appear in the supporter’s bank statement.

Ko-fi’s email confirmation will share your display name and email.

You have control over whether your contact number is displayed on Stripe receipts. To manage this, log into your Stripe account, click on the icon for Settings > Public Data > and toggle your Support phone number on or off.

We do not influence the information displayed on supporter’s bank statements. This information is provided by third-party payment services like Stripe and may be different depending on the banking institution. For more information, please contact Stripe directly. You can also change the descriptor at any time in your Stripe settings (Settings > Public Details > Descriptors). To learn more about setting up Stripe, check out our guide here.

More ways to protect your privacy with Stripe

If you’d like to keep certain details more private, here are some options you can look into:

Email: You can set up a new email just for Stripe, instead of using your personal one.

Phone number: Use a separate number. Some creators use a virtual number or an eSIM.

Address: Stripe requires a physical address, but this doesn’t have to be your home. You can search for services like a virtual mailbox for extra privacy.

How do I activate Apple Pay and Google Pay?

By adding Stripe as a payment option, supporters who have Apple Pay or Google Pay set up on their device will see the relevant checkout options, with no extra setup required.

If your supporter cannot see the checkout option, check that:

  • Your Ko‑fi is connected to Stripe
  • Your supporter is using a compatible device (iOS for Apple Pay, Android for Google Pay)
  • Your supporter is making a one-time payment, commission, or shop item purchase. Apple Pay or Google Pay is not available for memberships or recurring payments.

What if I receive a dispute with a Stripe payment?

If you ever receive a dispute with a Stripe payment, the first step is to take a look at Stripe’s guides on how to handle disputes and their FAQs:

How to handle disputes

Stripe Disputes FAQs

If you have any questions or need further help, feel free to reach out to us or directly contact Stripe.

As a direct payments service, Ko-fi ensures payments go directly to your account, and while we don’t hold funds, we’re more than happy to provide advice on how to avoid chargebacks in the future.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

What is a Ko-fi Contributor?

How to turn on/off Contributor status

  • Go to Settings > Payment
  • Toggle Contributor on or off
  • That’s it! You can cancel anytime.

Note

Everyone who joins Ko-fi now starts with Contributor status, so you can unlock everything from day one. You can opt out anytime.

Aside from supporting Ko-fi, Contributors get tons of exclusive perks to help them grow and customize their Ko-fi page:

  • Choose a custom theme color – Make your page truly yours.

  • Grab a shorter page name – Get a shorter, memorable Ko-fi link.
  • Change the ‘coffee’ metaphor – Switch it to pizza, bubble tea or whatever makes sense for you.
  • Use Google Analytics – Track and grow your audience with insights.
  • Offer Discord access – Give members instant role access on your server.
  • Direct audio uploads – Share music, podcasts, or audio messages.
  • More perks coming soon!

Direct messages on Ko-fi

You can chat with your supporters directly on Ko-fi. Use messages to get feedback from top fans, update supporters, or offer as a perk for their support. You can also manage your message preferences and control who can message you.

Managing messages

To view your Messages, go to your Messages from the sidebar or click the Messages icon at the top right of the page.

Note

To see the Messages icon on your page, you’ll need to send or receive a message first.

  • Control who can message you. Tap the icon to change who can message you and if you want to receive an email notification when you receive a message or reply.
  • All your conversations will appear in this panel.
  • Click on “…” to view options to manage specific conversations.
  • Mute this user and you will no longer receive notifications on any replies you receive. The user is not notified, but can still send messages.
  • Block this user and you will no longer receive any messages from this user. The user is not notified, and can no longer send messages. More info on blocking and limiting can be found here.
  • Delete conversation will delete this conversation from your message history. The other person will retain it until they choose to delete it. If you’d rather not interact with a particular user, see more on how blocking works here.
  • Click on “…” to delete your own message, or reply to someone else’s message.
  • Instantly send an attachment or GIF.
  • Write your message.
  • When you’re finished writing, press send.

Tip

React to any message by clicking and selecting from the available emojis!

To remove a reaction, just click it and it’s gone.

Commission Messaging

Turn on commission messaging in your Commission setup to accept messages from anyone who wants to discuss commissioning you before they buy.

You can read more about setting up commissions in our help article here.

Sending a message

Creators and supporters can send direct messages once a payment is made. Here’s how you can start the conversation:

Messaging a supporter Messaging a creator

You can send a message to anyone who has supported you.

  • Go to My Supporters and search for the supporter by their name or email.
  • Click the Message button.

Supporters will only be able to message you after they’ve made a payment. This can be a tip, a commission, a shop order, or a membership payment.

  • Go to the creator’s page.
  • Click the Message button or click “Send a Message”.

Note

If someone hasn’t supported you yet, they’ll be prompted to do so when they click the Message button. This option will not appear if you have opted to only accept messages from supporters you message first.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

FAQ

Why can’t I message a creator?

  • Check that you are logged in under the email that you tipped with.
  • Check that your payment shows under Payments & Orders. If you’re not seeing your payment, check your email for an option to link your transaction.
  • Note that creators might choose to only receive messages from supporters they contact first.

If you need help, you can reach out to us here and we’ll be happy to help.

I don’t see the Messages icon!

The icon will appear on the top right of the page if you have sent or received a message.

Otherwise, you can visit your Messages from the sidebar.

Can I turn off DMs entirely?

You can change your Message settings so that only supporters who you message first can message you back.

Do supporters have to have a Ko-fi account to message me?

Yes, supporters need an account to message you. After supporting, they’ll receive an email to quickly create an account. If they lose the email, they can still create an account directly on Ko-fi. Remember, only supporters who have made a payment can send messages.

Can I choose who can message me?

Creators can choose who they receive messages from. Go to Messages and click to access your message settings. Here, you can change who can message you and choose whether to receive an email notification when you get a message.

Choosing “Anyone who supports me” will enable the Message button on your page. This allows non-supporters to message you after they make a payment.

How do I block or mute a conversation?

Head to Messages > choose the conversation you want to block or mute > on the top right corner of that conversation click “…”

You’ll have the option to:

Mute this user and you will no longer receive notifications on any replies you receive. The user is not notified, but can still send messages.

Block this user and you will no longer receive any messages from this user. The user is not notified, and can no longer send messages.

Delete Conversation will delete this conversation from your message history. The other person will retain it until they choose to delete it. If you rather not interact with a particular user, see more on how blocking works here.

How will I know when someone messages me?

We’ll email you a notification with the message, and there will be an alert next to your Messages icon in the Ko-fi header.

I’ve messaged a creator and they haven’t replied?

If you’ve messaged a creator but haven’t received a response after purchasing a commission or shop item, check out our guide on unfulfilled commissions and shop items here.

Can I send a bulk message to my supporters?

Ko-fi doesn’t have a bulk messaging feature right now.

However, you can download the My Supporters CSV to view supporter email addresses and send a mass email outside of Ko-fi.

Discounts just got an upgrade

See everything new, from sales to scheduled discounts.

Does Ko-fi take a fee?

Ko-fi keeps things simple with two options, both free to join, with no monthly cost either way.

Ko-fi Free - Pay 0% on one-time tips. A flat 5% service fee applies to anything else, such as shop items, memberships, monthly tips, and commissions.

Standard - No monthly cost, but a flat 5% fee applies to one-time tips, shop, commissions, monthly tips and memberships too. In return, you unlock perks like supporter-only content, custom branding, and more.

You can toggle Standard mode on or off any time from Settings > Payment. New creators now start with all of Ko-fi unlocked from day one, you’re always free to opt out and keep tips completely fee-free.

Note

All payments go straight into your own PayPal or Stripe account. Your payment processor’s usual fees still apply on top (typically around 3% + $0.30). See below for more on that.

Do payment processors take a fee too?

On Ko-fi, you get paid directly into your own payment account. PayPal and Stripe take their normal transaction fees, which will vary based on the type of account, location, and currencies used, but it’s usually around 3% + $0.30.

Tip

Pick the currency that works best for you. To minimise conversion fees, set your Ko-fi currency to match the one you receive most payments in. Only payments in a different currency will be converted (PayPal and Stripe usually add a small percentage for that).

Currencies available:

  • United States Dollar (USD) $
  • Euro (EUR) €
  • British Pound (GBP) £
  • Australian Dollar (AUD) A$
  • Brazilian Real (BRL) R$
  • Canadian Dollar (CAD) C$
  • Japanese Yen (JPY) ¥
  • Singaporean Dollar (SGD) S$
  • Thai Baht (THB) ฿
  • New Zealand Dollar (NZD) $

Turning Standard mode on or off

Turn Standard mode on or off any time from Settings > Payment. Just tap Turn on “Get all of Ko-fi.”

What happens to platform fees for memberships if I join Ko-fi Gold?

When you join Ko-fi Gold, we aren’t able to update the fees we charge on memberships taken using the Stripe payment method.

You’ll keep paying the 5% platform fee on all memberships taken prior to switching to Gold.

What are “application fees” or “partner fees”?

Just different names for the same thing: Ko-fi’s 5% service fee. Stripe labels it the “Application Fee,” and PayPal shows it as a “Partner Fee.”

If you’ve got any more questions, please don’t hesitate to reach out. 💌

Getting started on Ko-fi

Welcome to Ko-fi! Ko-fi can’t wait to help creators start earning, so here’s how to get started.

This article is for new creators. It covers connecting a payment method, setting up a basic Ko-fi page, sharing the page and a goal with fans, setting the page’s default focus, and the ways creators can earn beyond tips.

The most important thing a creator can do is share their Ko-fi page with their fans. Ko-fi has seen lots of creators share their page and start receiving tips within just a few minutes of joining!

Tip

Creators who skipped any onboarding steps, like adding a profile pic or writing their bio, can edit these any time from Settings.

How to connect a payment method

To earn on Ko-fi, the first thing a creator needs to do is connect a payment method. That’s because on Ko-fi, supporters pay creators, not Ko-fi. Ko-fi thinks this keeps things simple and means creators keep more of their money.

Creators head to Payment Settings, choose PayPal, Stripe, or both, and follow the steps to connect.

Note

PayPal will also show local payment options for relevant supporters, like Venmo in the US. Connecting Stripe lets creators offer options like Apple Pay and Google Pay too. Neat, eh!

Learn more about payment methods

While in Payment Settings, creators can also pick their currency (don’t worry if a creator’s currency isn’t listed, they can still receive payments), switch up the “coffee” metaphor, and add a short thank-you message.

How to set up a basic Ko-fi page

Creators head to the Profile tab to add links to their social profiles, then the Page tab to pick a custom color.

From the Home page, creators can upload a cover image, add a goal, or add an intro video.

The most successful creators keep things simple and don’t overthink it. Just add the basics, and the page is good to go.

How to share a Ko-fi page and tell fans

Since only a small percentage of a creator’s followers see each post, it’s really important for creators to share their Ko-fi page regularly. Don’t worry, there are loads of creative ways to keep things fresh.

Creators can make sure their fans find their Ko-fi page by adding it to all of their social profiles and their link-in-bio.

Then creators can let everyone know they’re on Ko-fi! Most creators keep it low-key at first, and a common launch post goes something like:

“If you’d like to support my [thing you do], you can leave a tip over at Ko-fi.com/[yourpage]”

Creators can share their targets and tip goals with their followers via their chosen platform.

When a creator shares their goal, it gets a custom preview image, and sharing on X even adds a fun emoji progress bar.

To add and share a goal, creators just go to the Home page and choose Add goal. A goal lets supporters know what they’ll be contributing to.

Creators can choose which section visitors see first when they land on their Ko-fi page.

Creators head to Page Settings, scroll down to Page focus, and use the dropdown selector to set their default starting point. This is ideal for creators who mainly want to highlight their Memberships, Shop, or Commissions.

How creators can earn beyond tips

Creators can do so much more on Ko-fi than just earn from tips.

Isn’t it better for a creator’s biggest supporters to have one friendly place to go to support them? Some creators call Ko-fi their “digital home” ❤️

The most popular ways for creators to grow their income:

  • Streaming – connect Twitch or YouTube and earn while streaming, for just 0–5% service fees. Get stream alerts, add a goal overlay, and show payment messages in chat. For more information, check out these articles: Help articles about streaming

How do I get paid?

Getting paid is simple and instant. Just connect PayPal, Stripe, or both in your Payment Settings. No minimum balance, no waiting around.

💻 Create your page - Takes just a few seconds to get started.

💳 Connect a payment method - PayPal and Stripe let supporters pay in their preferred way.

📢 Share your page - Add your link to social media and start receiving payments.

💡 Grow your income - Try tips, memberships, commissions, or open a Ko-fi Shop.

You’ll get paid directly into your PayPal or Stripe account every time someone supports you. No delays, no fuss.

Getting paid on Ko-fi couldn’t be easier. Just link your preferred method of payment - whether it’s PayPal, Stripe, or even both. Whenever someone supports you, the funds are transferred immediately and directly into your own account. ✨

There’s no complicated payout schedule or pesky minimum balance to earn.

1. Create your free page

First thing’s first, head over to Ko-fi.com. It only takes a few seconds to claim your free page, add a few simple details and get started.

Note

Everyone who joins Ko-fi now starts with Contributor status, so you can unlock everything from day one. You can opt out anytime.

2. Connect a payment method

Head over to Payment Settings and connect your *PayPal, Stripe or both. If you don’t have an account with either of those providers, you can set one up as part of the process.

Don’t worry, you won’t be charged anything for connecting a payment method.

Note

PayPal will offer local payment options like Venmo in the US, while Stripe lets supporters pay using Apple Pay and Google Pay.

*PayPal and Stripe take their normal card processing fees. Ko-fi charge just 0-5% service fees, learn more about Ko-fi fees.

3. Share your page and start receiving payments

You’re now ready to start receiving payments. Yay!

Add your Ko-fi page to your socials and share your page wherever you’re active online.

“image-with-shadow” When someone visits your page, they can support you or buy you a ‘coffee’. The coffee bit is just a friendly metaphor. You can change this in Payment Settings.

When someone supports you, you’ll receive a notification and the money will be available in your payment account immediately.

4. Grow your income

Most creators start with one-time tips, but there’s loads of cool ways to grow your income. You can earn from Streaming, build a Membership, let supporters request Commissions or even sell products in your Ko-fi Shop.

Loads of creators tell us they love how simple Ko-fi is to use and how everything is in one place! When you’re ready to offer more, Ko-fi’s ready to grow with you.

FAQ

How do I make money on Ko-fi?

There are four different ways to make money on Ko-fi:

  • Accept Tips (one-off payments from supporters),
  • Offer Membership tiers,
  • Sell products in your Ko-fi Shop
  • Take Commission requests or offer services

How do I withdraw money from Ko-fi?

Ko-fi is a direct payments service, so all payments are sent directly to your chosen payment method instantly (you never hold a balance on Ko-fi).

What are the fees?

PayPal and Stripe take their normal card processing fees. Ko-fi take just 0-5% service fees. Learn more about our fees here.

I want to support a Creator but I can’t see the payment method I want to use?

Creators accept payments using PayPal and Stripe, but it’s up to the Creator to decide which payment methods to offer. Some creators only have PayPal and some only offer card payments through Stripe.

Will Ko-fi add other payment methods?

Currently we only offer PayPal and Stripe, but feel free to request additional options here (we do read every request). The most common requests are Payoneer, Wise, GCash, Payeer and direct bank transfers.

Will Ko-fi implement Non-fungible tokens (NFTs) or cryptocurrency payments?

As always, we are community driven. Many Creators are strongly opposed to NFTs and as such, we don’t have any roadmap plans involving NFTs or cryptocurrency payments.

How do I offer Venmo as a payment method?

For most US Creators, their US Supporters will see Venmo appear at checkout as an option. If not, check that:

  • You are in the US
  • Your Ko‑fi is connected to PayPal
  • Your page currency is set to USD
  • Your Supporter is paying via one-time payments only, Venmo will not appear for memberships or recurring tips

How do I switch to a PayPal Business account?

Discover how to upgrade to a PayPal Business account and enjoy enhanced control and features for your creator journey!

Why switch to a PayPal Business account?

Using a Personal PayPal account means your legal name and email are shared on PayPal receipts and the PayPal dashboard. Switch to a Business account, and you get to:

  • Pick a business name that shows instead of your legal name
  • Choose the email your supporters see

You can learn more about what’s involved in switching to a business account here.

Want to know more about what info is shared? Check out our articles for creators and supporters.

If you have a PayPal Business account, you can view and update the information shown to recipients in your Business settings here.

Switch your PayPal Personal account to Business

Start by logging in to your PayPal account, then follow the simple steps below:

  • Go to your Settings.
  • Under Account Options, click Upgrade to a Business account.
  • Enter your business details and proceed with the on-screen steps.

Voila! You’ve switched to a PayPal Business account. 💼✨

When you’re upgrading to a PayPal Business account, you have two options: either set up a new account with a new email or upgrade your current account with the same email. Should you opt for a new PayPal Business account with a different email, remember to unlink your old PayPal account and link the new one in Ko-fi Settings > Payment.

Note

Since Ko-fi is a direct payment service, your existing monthly or membership payments are linked to your initial PayPal account. If you’ve created a new PayPal Business account instead of upgrading your original one, your subscriptions will still be directed to your old PayPal account. To address this, you can advise your members and monthly subscribers to cancel their current subscription and then re-subscribe using the new PayPal account.

If you have any trouble making the switch, or you have more questions about a PayPal Business account, you can get in touch with PayPal Support here.

Still have questions? Feel free to reach out to us here. We’re here to help! 💌
