Helping your supporters create a Ko-fi account

Supporters with accounts can access exclusive content, track their payments, and stay better connected to you and your work.

If a supporter is having trouble with emails, unlocking content, or signing up with the wrong address, here’s a handy guide you can share or refer to when helping your supporter out.

Do supporters need an account to support me?

Supporters don’t need an account to support you, but having one allows them to access supporter-only content and easily track their payments.

If someone supports you as a guest, Ko-fi will send them a link to connect their payment to an account or create one after the payment is complete.

How can supporters create an account?

Supporters can sign up anytime here.

If they’ve already supported you as a guest, they’ll find a link in their confirmation email to register and link their payment.

Need help walking someone through it? Reach out to us here!

What if a supporter didn’t get their confirmation email?

When a supporter creates an account, they’ll need to confirm their email. They’ll get an email from Ko-fi@ko-fi.com with a link and a code to activate their account.

If they didn’t receive it, here’s what you can advise:

  • Ask your supporter to check spam, junk, social, or trash folders
  • Ask your supporter to have the confirmation email resent by logging into Ko-fi and heading to the Payments & Orders page. They will be prompted to verify again.
  • Ask your supporter to double-check the email used. If it was wrong, they can create a new account on the sign-up page. They will need to reach out to us here to add the transaction to their new page.

Still stuck? Point them to our support team. We are happy to help!

What if a supporter’s verification link is expired?

Verification links do expire after 72 hours. Here’s what they can try:

  • Enter the code manually instead of clicking the link
  • Resend the email from their Payments & Orders page

If that doesn’t work, they can reach out for help.

My supporter signed up with the wrong email?

Mistakes happen! If a supporter used the wrong email, they should contact us here and include:

  • The wrong and correct email addresses
  • Your Ko-fi page link
  • The payment amount and date

We’ll help link the support to the right account.

Can I use a creator account to support other creators?

Yes! Even if someone has a creator account, they can still support others.

If a creator wants to stop accepting payments and just be a supporter:

  • Visit Payment Settings
  • Click “Connected” to disconnect their payment method

They’ll stop receiving new payments but can still support other creators freely.

Why can’t a supporter see my supporter-only content?

Supporter-only content is visible only to logged-in users with an account linked to a valid transaction.

If someone’s having trouble:

  • Confirm they’ve created and logged into a Ko-fi account
  • Make sure they’re using the same email they used when supporting
  • If using social login, the email from their social account must match their checkout email

Our troubleshooting guide can help too.

If a supporter can’t log in with social media

Supporters can log in using Google, X (Twitter), Facebook, or Twitch. If that’s not working, they can:

  • Try another browser or device
  • Log in with their email and password instead. If they don’t have a password they can set one up here.

Still having issues? We’re here to help.

How can a member change their membership?

If they’re already supporting a creator with a monthly tip or as a member of a tier, here’s how a member can switch tiers, update their pledge amount, or change their payment card.

Note:

If a member is looking to cancel their membership, ask them to follow our article here.

Switch tiers

If a member is part of a tier and wants to change to a different tier, they will need to:

  • Log in to their Ko-fi account
  • Head to the creator’s membership page
  • Click Join on the new tier they want.

Once a member joins:

  • They’ll pay for the new tier upfront.
  • Their current tier will stop charging at the end of this billing cycle.
  • Their new tier will start around the same renewal date.

What happens next?

  • Upgraded? Your member will enjoy immediate access to extra perks.
  • Downgraded? Your member will keep their current perks until the end of their billing date.

A member won’t receive money back from their previous membership, but it’s up to the creator if they want to offer a refund. They can message the creator directly to request this.

If a supporter needs to message you, they can follow our guide here.

How can a member manage their membership?

Here’s how they can tweak their membership settings, from updating their payment card to changing their pledge.

  • Ask them to log into the Ko-fi account they used to become a member.
  • Go to More > Account & Billing.
  • Find the Subscriptions section, find the relevant subscription and click the three dots for more options.
Updating their card

A member will be prompted to add their new payment card.

  • The member’s next payment will come from this card.
  • Switching cards takes a few days, so if their renewal is soon, the old card might still get charged.

Note

No Update Card option? Their payment might be going through PayPal.

To update their payment method with PayPal, they can follow these steps:

  • Go to Settings.
  • Click Payments.
  • Select Automatic payments.
  • Select the merchant.
  • Select your current payment method.
  • Select a new payment method or click Link a new card or bank.
  • Click Save.

For a full guide, visit PayPal’s help article here.

Note

Right now, a member can’t switch between PayPal and direct card payments. They will need to cancel and rejoin using the payment method they want to use.

Update your pledge amount

Supporters can choose any amount higher than the minimum. Once updated, their new pledge will change when their next subscription payment is due.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to submit a request our way. 💌

How can a supporter get a refund?

On Ko-fi, payments go directly to the creator, so refunds are handled by them. Each creator sets their own refund policy and manages any refund requests from supporters personally.

This article explains how the process works, what supporters should do if they want a refund, and how creators can manage refund requests smoothly.

How supporters can request a refund

  • Check the creator’s refund policy
  • This is usually listed in their shop or commission listing, membership page, page description, or confirmation email. If a supporter is unsure, they can ask the creator.
  • Contact the creator
  • A supporter can reach out to a creator via Ko-fi Direct Message or their preferred method of communication to request a refund.
  • Allow time for a reply
  • Give the creator time to review requests, and if there is no reply within a reasonable time (usually 7 days), a supporter can contact their payment provider (like PayPal or their card issuer) to file a dispute. A dispute must be within 180 days of the original payment date. Read more about this here.

What to do if a supporter requests a refund

Supporters are encouraged to contact you directly if they’d like a refund. As the creator, you’re responsible for handling refund requests based on your own terms and policies.

Tip

Be sure to set your terms for Shop, Commissions, and Memberships.

Supporters will see this before they make a payment.

How to resolve refunds:
  • Check your messages:
    • Supporters can reach you via Direct Message on Ko-fi.
    • Keep your contact email for Shop and Commissions up to date to avoid missing messages.
    • Note
    • Updating your Ko-fi account email won’t update your Shop/Commission contact info. You can update it in your Shop or Commission settings.
  • Share your refund policy:
    • Point to your refund policy on your Ko-fi page, in your Membership and Shop/Commission listings, or in confirmation emails. This helps set clear expectations.
  • Issue refunds directly:
    • If you agree to a refund, you can issue a full refund directly on Ko-fi up to 180 days after the payment.
    • For partial refunds, you’ll need to process them via PayPal or Stripe.
    • Read our step-by-step guide on issuing refunds here.
  • Respond promptly:
    • Fast replies help avoid disputes or escalations.

Can Ko-fi issue refunds for me?

No, all refunds are managed directly between you and your supporters. Ko-fi does not process payments and cannot issue refunds.

Need advice on refunds? Contact the Ko-fi support team here. We’re happy to help where we can.

FAQ

Who is responsible for issuing refunds on Ko-fi?

You, the creator, are responsible for issuing refunds directly to your supporters. Ko-fi cannot process or mediate refunds.

How do supporters contact me for a refund?

Supporters can send you a Direct Message via your Ko-fi page or use the contact email you provide in your Shop or Commission settings.

Can I issue a partial refund?

Yes, but not via Ko-fi. Use PayPal or Stripe directly for partial refunds. Ko-fi only supports full refunds.

What if I don’t respond to a refund request?

If a supporter doesn’t hear back, they may contact their payment provider (like PayPal or their card issuer) to start a dispute or chargeback.

This could trigger a formal mediation process by the provider and may lead to extra fees for you. Responding quickly can help avoid this.

How much time do I have to issue a refund?

Refunds and disputes must be made within 180 days of the original payment. This is an industry-standard time limit set by the payment providers. After 180 days, refunds are no longer possible.

What happens to exclusive content after a refund?

It depends on the type of payment. You can read more here.

  • Tips → Access is removed immediately
  • Memberships → Access stays until the billing period ends
  • Digital shop orders → Supporters lose the ability to re-download the item

What should I include in my refund policy?

Be clear about when you offer refunds (e.g., non-delivery, damaged goods), how supporters should contact you, and any timeframes for processing.

Can Ko-fi step in if there’s a dispute?

No. Ko-fi isn’t the merchant of record and can’t mediate payments, but our team can offer guidance if you’re unsure what to do. Reach out to us here for more info.

How do supporters join my Discord server?

You can offer Discord roles as a reward for one-time support or joining your membership. These roles can unlock exclusive channels or give extra privileges like badges in your Discord server.

Note

To offer Discord rewards to your supporters, you’ll need to be on the Standard plan. Learn more about all the features available on Standard. If you’re not already on Standard, you’ll be prompted to opt in when you add your Discord server or adjust your roles.

What is Discord?

Discord is a platform where friends and communities chat and hang out. It has voice, text, and video channels organized in servers by topic, so it’s easy to build spaces around your work and interests.

How your supporters claim a Discord role

After you’ve set up Discord rewards and someone supports you, they can claim the role directly from your Ko-fi page.

  • Supporter visits your page. Supporters can go directly to your Ko-fi page (e.g. ko-fi.com/yourname). If they’re not sure where to find it, they can check their email receipt or visit Payments & Orders.
  • In the green box, they’ll click ‘Connect to Discord & Claim’. To do this, they’ll need to be logged in with the email they used to support you. If they paid as a guest, they’ll need to link that payment to a Ko-fi account. You can find more info below.
  • They’ll sign in to Discord and authorize the Ko-fi Bot to assign roles.
  • The role will then be applied in Discord and also shown under Discord Benefits when they visit your Ko-fi page.

If this does not work, the best way might be to contact you and for you to assign the role manually on your Discord server. Any supporter can follow these instructions here to contact their Creator to get support.

If you’re unable to help, submit a support ticket with the specific supporter’s details and our team will look into it!

Supporter can’t see the “Connect to Discord and Claim” button

Ask them to first check that their Discord account is connected to Ko-fi.

To claim a role, their payment needs to be linked to a Ko-fi account. They also need to be logged into that same account.

They can check the Payment & Orders to find their payment to you.

If they supported you as a guest within the last 72 hours, they should have received an email titled Confirm your guest membership purchase. This email has a button to link the payment to an existing Ko-fi account or to create a new one.

If it’s been more than 72 hours or the link doesn’t work, they can raise a support ticket and we’ll help connect the payment.

Role appears on your page but isn’t showing in Discord

Sometimes this happens if the Ko-fi Bot can’t assign roles or another bot in your server is interfering with its permissions.

Ask the supporter to go to their Discord tab on Ko-fi, press the “Connected” button, and re-authorize the Ko-fi Bot.

If that doesn’t fix the issue, check out our troubleshooting steps here to see if you can solve the issue.

If this does not work, the best way might be to contact you and for you to assign the role manually on your Discord server. Any supporter can follow these instructions here to contact their Creator to get support.

If you’re unable to help, submit a support ticket with the specific supporter’s details and our team will look into it!

How supporters can disconnect their Discord account from Ko-fi

They can visit their Ko-fi Discord tab, then press the “Connected” button to disconnect.

This removes the Ko-fi Bot’s permission to update their roles but won’t remove any roles already assigned in your Discord server.

How supporters can remove Ko-fi Bot assigned roles

The Ko-fi Bot automatically assigns and removes roles based on payment status.

To remove a role, supporters can:

  • Disconnect their Discord account from Ko-fi in their Discord settings
  • Leave the server on Discord
  • Wait for their membership to end

Once their membership expires, Ko-fi Bot will remove the role automatically.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

How to explain Ko-fi to your audience

Ko-fi is a fun, friendly way for your supporters to say thanks and help fund your creative work.

How does Ko-fi work?

Creators set up their personalized Ko-fi page and share it with their lovely fans. When you visit a creator’s page, you can choose to tip them and also leave a short message of encouragement if you like, and that’s it! The money goes directly to the creator, acknowledging their hard work and fueling their passion to keep going.

Freedom for the creator

Creators can use Ko-fi as a simple ‘tip jar’ to receive tips or expand it to offer loads more. They can create membership tiers with rewards, set up stream alerts, sell physical or digital products in their Ko-fi shop or even offer commissions and services. All of this from one place!

Creators keep more of their money

Ko-fi charges just 0-5% service fees for the service (most other services take between 12% and 30%). That means more of your money goes directly to the creator. You can learn more about our fees here.

Safe and secure payments

We don’t mess with your money. Payments are sent instantly and directly from you to the creators own PayPal or Stripe account. Ko-fi doesn’t process payments, hold on to the money or make creators wait for payouts.

What’s the coffee part?

Lots of creators choose a metaphor for the tip. By default that’s ‘coffee’, but creators can change it to something else if they like. There’s no actual coffee involved, it can just feel a bit friendlier to buy your creator a coffee!

What type of creators use Ko-fi?

We’ve had millions of creators use Ko-fi over the years. From crafters to spiritual healers, cat rescuers to jazz groups. These are just a few creators we love:

🎨 Artists: Mochi Buddies, Tofu, Chris Sale

🦸‍♀️ Cosplayers: Chrissy Plays Dressup, Black Bettie, Vicious Frockery

✍️ Writers: Lou Yardley, Latoya Shauntay Snell, Shannon Ashley

🎙️ Podcasters: Long Cat Media, Squawking Dead, On Second Watch

💻 Developers: Cyber Barbie, Jenny, Justinpinkney

📺 Streamers: Eric Campbell, Shenanjegans, BobDuckNWeave

▶️ Video Creators: The Girl With The Pilates Mat, Tara, Frank James

🎵 Musicians: Victoria Banks, Kutski, Open Collab

Still have questions about Ko-fi? Send us a request here.

How to help a supporter cancel their membership 💳

Supporters can cancel their membership anytime from their Ko-fi account by heading to Account & Billing > Subscriptions, then choosing “Don’t Renew.”

If they can’t see their membership:

  • Make sure they’re logged in to the right account

  • Ask them to check for a welcome email from Ko-fi

  • The payment might have failed

If they don’t have a Ko-fi account, they can create one using the email link they received. For PayPal subscriptions, they can cancel directly through PayPal.

Supporters can’t reactivate cancelled memberships but can always sign up again if there’s space in the tier.

If a supporter wants to stop their membership to you, they can do it anytime from their own Ko-fi account.

You can share the steps below to guide them through it.

How supporters can cancel their memberships:

Supporters can cancel their memberships in just a few steps:

  • Visit their Account Settings
  • Scroll to “Subscriptions”
  • Click the three dots next to the subscription they want to stop
  • Choose “Don’t Renew”

Their membership status will change to “cancelled”. They can still enjoy access to content and benefits until their membership period ends.

What if a supporter can’t see their membership?

If they have trouble spotting their membership on Ko-fi, here’s a quick guide to help:

  • Confirm they’re logged in first: Make sure they are logged in to the correct account.
  • Check their email: They might have used a different email to sign up. Advise them to quickly search their email inboxes for “Ko-fi” to find the welcome email that says, “You joined [the tier name].”
  • Failed Payment: If they have looked through their account and emails but still can’t find their confirmation email, their payment might have failed.

They can reach out to the Support team here. If they are reaching out about a membership they cannot see, please have them include your Ko-fi page (ko-fi.com/handle), the membership tier they joined, when they paid, and the amount paid.

What if they don’t have a Ko-fi account?

They’ll need an account to manage their memberships through Ko-fi. Just follow the Manage Subscription link in the email they received, and they’ll be guided through the sign-up process.

If their email link isn’t working anymore, they can try to reset their password for the email account they want to access, and this will create their account. You can send them our help article to get it sorted.

For PayPal subscriptions, supporters can manage their membership directly within PayPal without having to make a Ko-fi account.

A member can follow these steps to cancel a subscription via PayPal.

  • Go to Settings.
  • Click Payments.
  • Select Automatic payments.
  • Select the merchant. Any Ko-fi subscriptions will show up under “Ko-fi”. Check the Description to confirm the creator.
  • Cancel the automatic payment.

For a full guide, have them check out PayPal’s help article here.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to submit a request our way. 💌

FAQ

Why was my supporter’s membership cancelled?

A membership can be cancelled by you or the supporter. If it wasn’t cancelled by either of you, it was likely cancelled due to multiple failed payments.

While a supporter can’t reactivate a cancelled membership, they can subscribe again from your Ko-fi page.

My supporter’s membership is overdue

If their membership is overdue and they cancel it, it will end right away.

My supporter has been charged twice for the same membership

This might be a pending charge, which usually clears up in about 5 days.

My supporter would like a refund

As all payments are between you and the supporter, it’s up to you to issue a refund.

You can refund a supporter by following this guide.

Can a supporter reactivate their membership?

Once a membership is cancelled, it can’t be reactivated. To continue supporting you, they’ll need to sign up again on your page.

If the tier is full, you can open a slot for them to rejoin via your Membership settings.

My supporter can’t see creator’s content after payment

If they support a creator to unlock content, they’ll need to create a Ko-fi account, and their payment should be linked to their account. A supporter can check their subscription in their Account Settings or find a record of their payment in the Payments and Orders page.

The common issues are: not having a Ko-fi account, not linking their payment to their account, or simply not having the right access. Let’s walk through some simple steps for them to access content.

Tip

If a supporter believes they were logged in when they supported someone, they can try using a different device or browser. If that doesn’t do the trick, clearing their cache often helps sort out a few hiccups.

If a supporter is having trouble accessing the creator’s content, here’s a few things to check:

Accessing content if a supporter paid as a guest or wasn’t logged in

If a supporter doesn’t have an account or paid as a guest, ask them to check their email for a message from Ko-fi. The subject for the email is usually “You supported [Creator] on Ko-fi”. The email will show who they supported and what they bought. It will also guide them on how to link their purchase to either a new or existing Ko-fi account.

After linking their transaction, they’ll be able to access the content hassle-free.

Unable to access content due to payment or subscription issue

If a supporter is having trouble accessing content due to a payment or subscription issue, here’s what to check:

Subscription overdue: Ask them to verify if their subscription is overdue in their account settings. Follow our guide here to resolve overdue status.

Pending payment: Ask them to check if their payment is pending on the Payments & Orders page. Learn more about pending payments here.

Qualifying shop purchase: Shop purchases may unlock content, but it depends on the creator’s settings. If a supporter is unable to access content after a qualifying shop purchase, the creator may need to enable access. A supporter will need to reach out to the creator for clarification. A supporter can follow the instructions here to message a creator.

Any issues, please raise a support ticket. We’ll need to know what steps they’ve taken, the creator’s page link, and the email entered at checkout. We’re happy to help!

A supporter paid but can’t watch any videos

To watch certain videos, a supporter may need to enable cookies. Follow these steps:

  • Make sure they are logged in and navigate to the creator’s page.
  • Scroll to the bottom of the page to find cookie settings.
  • Select “Accept all cookies” or set their own preferences.
  • After adjusting your settings, refresh the page. Videos should now play without issue.

If they encounter any further difficulties, please ask them to reach out to support. Provide the email they used to support, the creator’s link, details of what they’re trying to access, and any relevant payment information. We’re here to help!

My supporter can’t find their transaction or subscription

Supporters can send you payments on Ko-fi without creating an account. But if they do set one up, it’s easier for them to track payments and manage memberships in one place.

If a supporter says they’ve signed up but can’t see their payment or subscription, this guide can help you understand why and what they can do to fix it.

Checking their email

We send a confirmation email for every one-time payment and the first payment of a membership.

If the supporter wasn’t logged in when they paid, they’ll also get an email prompting them to link the payment or membership to their Ko-fi account. It’ll come from ko-fi@ko-fi.com, with a subject like:

  • Your Ko-fi receipt for supporting [Creator]
  • Don’t miss your rewards from [Creator]
  • You joined [membership]!

They can tap “Confirm your guest purchase” to either log in or create a Ko-fi account.

Once that’s done, they’ll be able to view their payment from the Payments & Orders tab. To manage their membership, they can visit Account & Billing.

Note

Payments can only be linked to the email entered at checkout. If the supporter creates an account with a different email, the payment won’t appear.

My supporter didn’t get an email

Sometimes emails end up in the wrong place or don’t arrive at all. Here’s what supporters can try:

  • Check spam, junk, or promotions folders.
  • Search inboxes for “Ko-fi” or your display name.
  • Add ko-fi@ko-fi.com to their contacts to prevent missing future emails.

If they still can’t find it, it’s worth confirming that their payment was successful by checking your Payments & Orders. Failed payments can sometimes appear as pending on a bank statement.

If they need help, they can contact us with the following:

  • Your Ko-fi page link (e.g. ko-fi.com/yourname)
  • The display name they entered at checkout
  • The email they used at checkout
  • The email of their Ko-fi account
  • The payment date and amount

We’ll do our best to get things connected.

My supporter’s link expired

For security, links to connect guest payments are only valid for 72 hours. After that, they’ll see an error like this:

We can help them connect their payment on our end. Just ask them to contact us with:

  • Your Ko-fi page link (e.g. ko-fi.com/yourname)
  • The display name they used
  • The email they entered at checkout
  • The email of their Ko-fi account
  • The payment date and amount

Note

Since Ko-fi doesn’t process payments directly, we can’t track down payments using card details or PayPal transaction IDs.

Can my supporter cancel their subscription without an account?

Ko-fi is a direct payment service. That means payments go straight from the supporter’s payment method to you. If they didn’t link their subscription to a Ko-fi account, they won’t be able to cancel it through Ko-fi.

  • For PayPal subscriptions, they can cancel directly via their PayPal Automatic Payments by following this guide.
  • For card subscriptions, they must first link the subscription to a Ko-fi account. Once linked, they can follow the steps here.
Can I cancel the subscription for a supporter?

You can cancel the subscription for a supporter if their subscription is tied to a Ko-fi account.

To cancel a subscription, follow our guide here.

Can Ko-fi cancel the subscription for a supporter?

We can’t cancel a subscription on their behalf, but we can help them link it to their Ko-fi account. That way, they’ll be able to manage it themselves.

They’ll just need to get in touch with us and include:

  • Your Ko-fi page link (e.g. ko-fi.com/yourname)
  • The display name they entered
  • The email they used at checkout
  • The email of their Ko-fi account
  • The payment date and amount

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

My supporter is having trouble with their subscription

If a supporter is having issues with their monthly tip or membership like a failed payment, an unexpected cancellation, or trouble updating their card, this guide explains what could be going on and what they can do next.

You’re welcome to share this article with them directly or link to it in your membership terms. It includes step-by-step guidance that could save time for both of you.

Failed membership payment

When a supporter’s payment fails, it’s usually due to issues with their card, PayPal account, or bank. If a payment fails, Ko-fi will automatically retry it a few times before the subscription is canceled.

Supporters will see a “Overdue” label in their Account & Billing, like this:

Ko-fi will automatically retry failed payments several times before canceling a subscription. If the payment keeps failing, the subscription will be canceled.

If their payment isn’t going through, they can try these steps:

Checking card or payment method
  • Balance: Their PayPal or bank account may not have enough funds.
  • Expired card: They’ll need to update their card if it’s expired.
  • Bank restrictions: Some banks block recurring payments. Have them check with their bank or PayPal to see if they declined the charge.
  • Payment processor issues: If they’re using PayPal, they can log in to check for restrictions. If using a card, they may need to contact their bank.
  • Retry the charge: If everything looks okay, they can retry the payment manually or wait for the system to try again.

If they’re still having trouble, PayPal Support or their bank may have more details on why the payment isn’t going through. They can also reach out to us and we’ll be happy to take a look.

Tip

Creators can reduce overdue payments by turning on Stripe’s built-in notification emails. When enabled, Stripe automatically contacts members when a payment needs attention — whether that’s an expired card, updated details, or a 3D Secure check. Learn how to reduce overdue payments with Stripe notifications.

Updating a payment method

If a supporter needs to update their card or payment method, here’s how they can do it depending on how they originally paid:

PayPal Card

PayPal subscriptions must be updated directly in PayPal.

  • Log in to the PayPal account and go to your PayPal Settings.
  • Go to Payments > Automatic Payments.
  • Under the Active tab, choose the merchant. Any Ko-fi subscriptions will show up under “Ko-fi.” Check the description to confirm the creator.
  • Select the current payment method. Supporters can choose an existing payment method or link a new card or bank.
  • Click Save.

The new payment method will be used for future payments.

For a full guide, check out PayPal’s help article here.

If they pay by card, they can update it directly on Ko-fi:

  • Log in to the Ko-fi account linked to the membership.
  • Go to Account & Billing. If they don’t see their subscription, they can check out our help article here.
  • Tap the three dots next to the subscription and select Update Card. Enter the new card details.

Once updated, future charges will be billed to their new card.

Retrying the payment

Subscriptions will automatically retry several times. If a supporter wants to retry the payment manually, some subscriptions allow them to do so from their Account & Billing.

If the payment goes through, they’ll see a confirmation pop-up. If it fails, they’ll see a message explaining why.

Note

Not all subscriptions support manual retry. If your supporter doesn’t see the option, don’t worry - the payment will retry automatically within a few days.

Canceling and restarting a subscription

If payments keep failing, supporters can cancel their membership and start a new one from your page.

Note

If your tier has limited slots and it’s currently full, the supporter won’t be able to rejoin until you add more slots. They may need to message you to open a slot.

To add a slot to a membership tier, go to your membership settings. Learn how to manage tier availability here.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

FAQ

Why was the supporter’s membership canceled?

Subscriptions can be canceled by either you or the supporter. If neither of you canceled it manually, it likely failed due to a payment issue.

Payments will retry a few times before the subscription is automatically canceled.

Note

Subscriptions can’t be reactivated once canceled. A supporter will need to start a new one if they’d like to continue supporting you.

Will they lose access to content if the payment fails?

If a supporter’s payment doesn’t go through, their subscription will remain active while Ko-fi retries the payment.

If the payments continue to fail and the subscription is canceled, they will lose access to supporter-only content.

My supporter’s subscription was charged to the wrong card

By default, payments continue on the original card unless the supporter updates it. Supporters can follow the steps here to update their card.

If they’re still having issues, they can contact us and include:

  • The creator’s Ko-fi page link
  • The email linked to their subscription
  • The last four digits of the correct card

Can supporters rejoin a tier after it’s been canceled?

Yes, if the tier still has slots available. If the tier is full, they may want to send you a direct message to ask about future availability.

You can update your membership slots available via your membership settings. Learn how to manage tier availability here.

Can my supporter switch between PayPal and card payments?

Right now, supporters can’t switch between PayPal and card payments for an existing subscription. They would need to cancel their current subscription and start a new one using their preferred payment method.

Why did a canceled membership renew?

If a supporter cancels after the auto-billing date, the current charge may still go through. Subscriptions renew every 30 days, so it’s best to cancel at least 24 hours before the next billing cycle.

My supporter’s PayPal payment is unclaimed, pending, or declined

Here on Ko-fi, payments go straight to your own PayPal account, quick and direct. If a supporter’s payment shows as ‘pending’, it usually means it’s still being processed .

Note

PayPal says payments that can’t be completed within 30 days are usually cancelled or automatically returned to the supporter’s PayPal account .

How can a supporter check the payment status?

A supporter can keep track of all their payments in their Payments & Orders tab. If a supporter’s payment hasn’t gone through yet, you’ll see a grey status message in the bottom left corner of the transaction details.

A ‘pending’ status indicates your transaction isn’t complete yet. Tap the “i” help dot for more info on your pending payment.

Ask them to check out this PayPal guide to understand different statuses and any actions that need to be taken. PayPal also provides details on how to cancel payments that haven’t been completed here.

Unclaimed or pending status

A payment status of “unclaimed” or “pending” most likely means an issue with your PayPal account. This often occurs when you are not yet fully verified on PayPal.

Possible reasons include being new to PayPal, not confirming their email address, or providing an incorrect email address on Ko-fi. You might also need to accept payments in a new currency if your payment differs from what you’ve received before.

If a supporter reaches out about a pending payment, feel free to share this article with them to help explain what’s happening while you resolve the issue.

Declined payments

A declined payment usually means there’s a problem with the PayPal account or funding method.

Your supporter might need to confirm their bank, verify their card, or switch their funding source on PayPal. Learn more about declined payments and how to resolve them here from PayPal. We also have a help article here to help troubleshoot when your supporter cannot pay with PayPal.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

Supporter account settings FAQ

If your supporter needs to update their email, cancel a subscription, or delete their Ko-fi account, this guide covers the most common account questions.

Lost access to the email linked to their account

No problem, we can help. Just have your supporter follow the steps in this guide under “Lost access to Ko-fi account email”.

Can my supporter update the email they use to log in to Ko-fi?

Yes! They can change your login email at any time.

Here’s our step-by-step guide.

How do supporters cancel a subscription to me?

If a supporter no longer wants to subscribe to your membership, they can cancel at any time:

  • Have them log in to their Ko-fi account and go to More > Account and Billing.
  • Under “Subscriptions,” they’ll see their active memberships.
  • Click “Don’t Renew” next to the subscription they want to end.
  • After canceling, they’ll usually keep access until the end of the current billing period. They won’t be charged again.

You can share this guide with them if they need help managing their membership.

If a supporter tells you they can’t see their subscription, they can reach out to our support team with:

  • The Ko-fi page link they’re subscribed to (your Ko-fi page)
  • The payment date
  • The amount paid
  • The email address used at checkout
  • Their PayPal email (if they paid with PayPal)
  • Any other email addresses they might’ve used

If they’ve requested a refund from you, you can issue one with this guide.

Note

You can also cancel a supporter’s subscription following the steps here. However, you may not be able to cancel the subscription if they don’t have the subscription tied to a Ko-fi account.

How do supporters delete their Ko-fi account?

If your supporter would like to close their Ko-fi account, they can follow the steps in this article.

Make sure they’re aware that deleting an account doesn’t cancel any active subscriptions - they have to cancel them following the guide above before deleting their account.

We’re always here for you! Feel free to submit a request. 💌

Supporter payments FAQ

These are some common questions that your supporters may have about payments, from fees to refunds.

Ko-fi fees

Creators can choose from two options:

  • Set up a basic, free page and only offer tips.
  • Pay a small 5% fee for each payment they receive, and in return get cool extra features.

PayPal and Stripe take their normal card processing fees. (You can learn more about fees here).

When will the creator receive a supporter’s payment?

Usually straight away. When a supporter pays a creator on Ko-fi, it goes straight to the creator’s PayPal or Stripe account. We don’t hold the money for any period and the creator doesn’t have to wait for a ‘payout’.

What information about a supporter is shared when they pay a creator?

When a supporter pays a creator, Ko-fi only shares their chosen display name and email address.

They may be asked for a physical address if they purchase a physical item or subscribe to a membership tier that includes physical items, or if they are in an area where the creator charges sales tax. Their phone number may also be requested (if required for shipping).

On Ko-fi, a supporter is paying the creator directly through their payment method, they’re not paying Ko-fi itself. Please be mindful of the information shared by payment providers, as we’ve summarised below.

If a supporter pays with PayPal

When a supporter pays with PayPal, PayPal shares their legal name and email with the creator inside the PayPal dashboard. If a supporter uses a business account, they can choose what name and email are displayed. Find out more from PayPal here.

If a supporter pays with Stripe (Card)

Creators can also choose to connect their Stripe account to receive card payments. Stripe provides creators with your name, email and part of your address in addition to other normal information merchants are able to view. Find out more from Stripe here.

For more information on what information is shared when supporting a creator on Ko-fi, have a read here.

Can a supporter make a tip message private?

For a supporter to send a private message with their tip, they need to select ‘Private Message’. Only the creator will see the message they write.

Want to know what info is shared with the creator when a supporter tips? Read more here.

Can a supporter make a public message private?

Yes, they need to head to the creator’s About tab on the creator’s homepage.

Then scroll down to find their message. Click on the “three dots” and choose “Make Message Private”.

Don’t worry, the creator will still see their message, it just won’t show up for everyone else.

Can a supporter pay in any currency?

Creators pick their preferred currency for payments. When a supporter pays, it auto-converts to the chosen currency. They should be aware of possible exchange rates and charges - verify these with PayPal.

If they pay using PayPal, they can choose the conversion rate offered by PayPal or the one from their card issuer (the card registered with PayPal).

A supporter can’t see PayPal or pay via card as an option

Each creator chooses which payment methods they offer. They can choose to offer PayPal, direct card payments or both. When the payment method a supporter wants isn’t available, this is because the creator has chosen not to offer that payment method or cannot offer that payment method for certain reasons.

A member’s membership payment is overdue

If a member has supported a creator monthly or joined a creator’s membership tier, their recurring payments might fail and get tagged as ‘overdue’. A member can see the status of their subscriptions through their Account settings tab.

Ko-fi will retry overdue payments within 3 working days. Don’t worry, members can still enjoy the creator’s content until their paid subscription ends.

If payment retries fail a number of times, a member’s subscription may be automatically cancelled. But they can join the creator’s membership again to get immediate access.

An overdue payment could happen because their card has expired, there’s not enough money in their account, or their bank has stopped their payment. Ask the member to check if there’s enough money in their account and deal with any warnings from their bank that might be preventing the payment from succeeding.

If their card has expired, updating it is simple. A member needs to head to their Account Settings > Subscriptions and click on the three dots and then “Update Card”.

Still having problems? Get in touch with us. We’re always ready to help out.

What’s the difference between being a Monthly Supporter and a Member of a tier?

Monthly Supporters: These are supporters who chose the ‘monthly’ option when they tipped. They give a recurring tip. This is now called ‘Simple monthly tips (no tiers)’ in the membership settings.

Members of a Tier: These supporters joined one tier and are entitled to the benefits set for that tier.

How can a member switch to a different tier?

Members can switch their membership tier anytime they like. When they upgrade or downgrade, they’ll be charged the full price of the new tier. Previous payments won’t be refunded or adjusted, but creators can issue partial refunds through PayPal or Stripe if needed.

How can a member make changes:

  • Log in to Ko-fi.
  • Head to the creator’s memberships page.
  • Choose the subscription they want.
  • Join the membership.

And that’s it! Their membership will be updated. 😊

A supporter’s payment won’t go through

Ask your supporter to try using another payment method or device. Ask them to check if their payment provider is blocking the payment. A supporter may need to contact their payment provider to verify the transaction is genuine.

If they are trying to pay by PayPal, check out our guide here.

If they are paying with card, check out our guide here.

Note

On Ko-fi, they are paying the creator directly, and payment issues may be related to the creator’s payment method rather than their own account. So after reading the guides, they can reach out to us and we can check for them.

A supporter has been charged twice for a payment

This might be a pending payment, which typically disappears within 5 days. If it doesn’t, ask your supporter to get in touch with us.

Can a supporter get a refund for their payment?

Payments go straight to creators. If they need a refund, they can contact the creator directly from their profile page:

  • Head to the creator’s page (under their profile pic) > “Send a Message”.
  • A supporter may also be able to dispute the payment directly with PayPal or their bank if they paid by card.

How do discounts work with “Pay What You Want” listings?

A supporter can choose how much they want to pay for a shop item or commission with Pay What You Want. If they choose to pay more than the minimum price, the discount code won’t apply. The discount only works if they pay the minimum price.

For example:

  • If the minimum price is £10 and the supporter pays £10, using the discount code “CAKE20” will reduce the price to £8.

But, if a supporter chooses to pay more than £10, like £20, the discount code “CAKE20” won’t reduce the price.

Need assistance? We’re here to help. Feel free to submit a request. 💌

A supporter has an unexpected charge on their card or PayPal account

Ko-fi transactions are between creators and supporters, meaning only a creator or a payment provider (PayPal/Stripe) can provide a supporter with a refund. However, we’re happy to provide guidance if help is needed.

Depending upon the transaction type, they might be able to dispute through their PayPal account (learn more here). If they paid straight from their bank, their bank can usually help them dispute the transaction.

Switching from creator to supporter

There isn’t currently a way to change your account from a creator to a supporter account.

Don’t worry - you can still support other creators with your creator account!

If you no longer want to receive payments on Ko-fi, you can disconnect your payment accounts from your Payment Settings.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

How can I change my username or display name?

Here’s the difference between your display name and your username on Ko‑fi:

Display name: Your display name is what shows on your Ko‑fi page, your posts and comments, and when you interact with creators on Ko‑fi. You can update it anytime to use your preferred name or brand.

Username: Your username is what shows in your Ko‑fi page link (for example, ko‑fi.com/yourusername). It’s unique, nobody can have the same one at the same time. If you change your username, your page link will change too.

To change your Display name:
  • Head to your Profile tab, look for the Display name field.
  • Enter the new name you’d like to show.
  • Click anywhere outside the box to save.

Your display name usually updates quickly, but sometimes takes a short while to refresh on your’s and other’s page and feed.

To change your Username:
  • Head to your Profile tab, look for the Username field.
  • Type your new username, this will also update your page link (e.g., ko‑fi.com/yournewname).
  • Click anywhere outside the box to save.

It may take 1–2 days for your new username to update everywhere on Ko‑fi.

If your username doesn’t change

Your desired username might already be taken. If you’ve previously made Ko‑fi accounts with that username, delete any unused accounts and wait 7 days before changing your username again. If you haven’t made other accounts, the handle may have already been claimed.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

Unfulfilled commission or shop order

If a supporter reaches out to say they haven’t received something they ordered from you, like a commission, shop order, or other content, it’s important to respond clearly and as quickly as you can. This guide explains what your supporter might check, how to reply, and what to expect if they ask for a refund or file a dispute.

You can share this guide with your supporter before they contact you. It’s also a helpful reference to include in your listing or terms, as it walks through all the key steps and could save them time.

What supporters might check before messaging you

Before getting in touch, a supporter may:

  • Look for an agreed delivery date in the order notes or listing
  • Check their Payment History
  • Review any updates you’ve posted on your Ko-fi page or social media

Most supporters understand that unexpected things can happen. Still, if a delivery date has passed and there’s been no update, they may message you to ask about it.

How to respond to supporters

If a supporter contacts you, try to reply as soon as possible.

A helpful reply includes:

  • A quick status update on their order
  • An updated delivery timeframe (if needed)
  • A friendly message to reassure them you’re working on it

If you can’t complete the order, it’s okay to offer a refund. Supporters appreciate transparency, even if the news isn’t ideal.

To issue a refund, check out our help article here.

What happens if a refund is requested?

Supporters pay you directly using PayPal or Stripe, so any refunds must be issued by you, the creator. Ko-fi can’t refund orders in your place.

You can refund an order by following our guide here.

If you don’t respond, supporters may file a dispute with PayPal or their bank.

We recommend resolving things directly where possible. Disputes can be stressful for everyone involved. If you’re unsure what to do, feel free to contact us for support.

For supporters looking for help

Ko-fi is a direct payment service, meaning your payment goes straight to the creator’s account. Ko-fi doesn’t take the payment or act as an intermediary, so we’re not able to offer refunds or step in if there’s a dispute.

If you’re a supporter with an unfulfilled commission/shop order, here’s what you can do:

  • Check your order notes in your Payments & Orders.
  • Look for updates on the creator’s Ko-fi page or social media.
  • Message the creator directly through their Ko-fi page or via their preferred method of communication. Most payment providers will want to know that you’ve made contact.
  • If there’s no response, you can file a dispute with PayPal or your bank, depending on how you paid.

Note

Keep in mind that disputes can only be filed within 180 days of your purchase.

If you believe you’ve been scammed, you can report a creator, but keep in mind that our Trust & Safety team will only consider reports of unfulfilled goods/services if there is evidence of broader fraudulent activity. In this case, choose “Other” as the report reason.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

FAQ

Can Ko-fi issue a refund for me or my supporter?

No - payments go directly from the supporter to you via PayPal or Stripe, so any refund needs to be issued by the creator.

A supporter may dispute a payment with PayPal or their bank if you do not fulfill their order or issue a refund, which can result in dispute fees, so be sure that you keep an open communication with your supporters.

Is Ko-fi responsible for unfulfilled commissions or shop orders?

As written in our Terms, commissions, shop orders, and other payment agreements are made directly between creators and supporters.

Because of this, we can’t mediate between you and your supporter.

What information do supporters share? Public & private options

When someone supports you on Ko-fi, they’ll enter a few essential details like their preferred username, email, and an optional message. These details are shared directly with you as part of their payment.

Supporters are in control of how their name appears on your page and whether their message is visible publicly or just between the two of you.

Just so you know, payments go straight into your PayPal or Stripe account. Some details like the supporter’s legal name or address may be shared with you by those payment providers as part of their normal process. You can learn more about what information you receive from supporters here.

What details are shared when a supporter makes a payment?

Whether it’s a tip, commission, or shop order, Ko-fi collects the supporter’s display name (or username if logged in) and their email. They can also leave you a public or private message.

These details are used to:

  • Send a payment receipt
  • Deliver rewards or digital products
  • Allow them to create an account to track purchases, access supporter-only content, or contact you via Ko-fi (if messaging is enabled)

Note

If a supporter purchases a physical shop item or joins a membership with physical rewards, we’ll ask for their address so you can ship it.

Names and usernames on your feed

Supporters with an account can choose a display name and profile image that shows when they support you.

If they support you as a guest, they can enter a name or leave it blank to show as “Somebody” on your page. However, if they use an email tied to a Ko-fi account, their Ko-fi username and profile picture will automatically show on your feed, even if they’re logged out. To keep this private, you can suggest they use a different email at checkout.

Supporters with an account can change their name anytime in their Profile Settings.

Note

Display name changes may take a few days to fully update on your feed. If a supporter needs their display name updated urgently, they can contact Ko-fi support.

Supporter email visibility

Every supporter must enter an email address to receive a receipt and any items associated with their payment.

This email address is shared with you, the creator, but it won’t appear publicly on your page.

If a supporter with an account logs out and supports you using the same email linked to their Ko-fi account, their account display name will still show on your feed.

Supporters can update their email from their Account & Billing.

Private & public tip messages

If a supporter chooses to leave a private message, it will only be visible to you. It won’t appear on your public feed.

Here’s how private (first) and public (second) messages appear on your feed. A private message only shows the supporter’s display name. A public message shows both the name and message.

How supporters can make a message private

If a supporter left a public message and later wants to make it private, either you or the supporter can hide it from your feed. You’ll still be able to see the message.

Here’s how it’s done:

  • Visit your Ko-fi page and find their message on your feed.
  • Click the three dots and select Make Message Private.

Supporters need to be logged in to do this. If they supported you as a guest, they can contact you directly or Ko-fi Support to request this change.

Note

If they originally marked the message as private, it can’t be made public later.

Supporter address

We don’t ask for a supporter’s address unless you’re sending something physically. If you’re sending physical shop items or membership rewards, we’ll ask supporters for their postal address and a phone number if required.

They can update their address from their Payments & Orders page at any time.

We only ever share this address with you for shipping purposes.

Do supporters need a Ko-fi account?

No, an account is not needed to support you on Ko-fi!

Supporters can create a Ko-fi account to personalize their display name and access supporter-only content.

With an account, they can:

  • Track their tips and orders
  • Access any exclusive content you have available on Ko-fi
  • Send you a message on Ko-fi (if you have the option enabled)

If a supporter hasn’t verified their account yet, they can do so by visiting Payments & Orders. We’ll send them a code to complete the process.

What details are shared by PayPal and Stripe?

Ko-fi is a direct payment service. Payments go directly into your connected PayPal or Stripe account.

Each provider shares slightly different info:

  • PayPal shares the supporter’s legal name and email
  • Stripe shares the supporter’s legal name, email, the card-linked address, and limited bank details

Supporters can upgrade to a PayPal Business account if they’d like to change the display name shown when they support you.

For a full breakdown of what info is shared, see this article.

Can someone support me anonymously?

When someone supports you on Ko-fi, you’ll always see their display name and email. If you’re sending a physical item, you’ll also get their address.

Because payments go directly through PayPal or Stripe, both providers share some information between you and the supporter.

So, there isn’t a way to support someone on Ko-fi without sharing any details at all.

You can find a full breakdown of shared details from payment providers here.

For more info about data processing, see our privacy policy and terms.

Still have questions? Feel free to reach out to us here. We’re here to help! 💌

What personal information is shared on Ko-fi as supporter?

We know your supporter’s privacy is key, that’s why we only require a little bit of information from them to complete a payment to you (usually just a name and email).

Their payments go directly to you, the creator using PayPal or Stripe. Those payment providers control what is shown on bank statements or in the PayPal dashboard.

Is Ko-fi anonymous?

In short, that depends on how a supporter chooses to support you. When they support you with a tip, the only information shown on Ko-fi is their display name and their tip message. You will be able to see their email, but it won’t be displayed on your page. Supporters can make their tip message private (so you, the creator, see it but no one else).

They can also log out and support you as a guest so they are in full control of what is shared on Ko-fi.

Note

If they don’t want their payment to be linked to their Ko-fi account, they can use a different email address at checkout than the one on their account.

If they buy a physical item or subscribe to a membership tier that includes physical items, they give their address details so that they can receive your goods. Some couriers require a phone number too so you can request that as part of the shipping information.

What personal information is shared by payment providers?

Ko-fi doesn’t control what payment providers share, so your supporter has to reach out to them if they need to know more about their data sharing. Here’s a basic summary:

PayPal payments

Whether they’re using a personal or business PayPal account can affect what information is shared during transactions. Personal accounts typically display their legal name and email. If they prefer more privacy or control, they can consider a PayPal Business account. This allows them to choose the name and email displayed during transactions. Upgrading to a business account is usually free.

Stripe (card) payments

When they pay using their card, Stripe captures the same information it would for a normal e-commerce transaction. Details such as their legal name, email address, bank, and a portion of the address the card is registered to are available to the Stripe account holder. Find out more about the information available to a Stripe merchant here.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

What to do when a supporter can’t pay with PayPal

If a supporter is running into a PayPal error when trying to support you, don’t worry! Remember, you connect your own PayPal and/or Stripe accounts to receive payments, so the issue could be on your side or theirs. Either way, we’ll help you get things working so their support can reach you.

Steps supporters can try to fix PayPal payment issues

When something goes wrong with a PayPal payment, supporters might see one of these error messages:

  • “Something went wrong. Please try again later.”
  • “Unable to process payment, try again later.”
  • “We could not process payment at this time. Please try again later.”

Sometimes they won’t see an error at all, but the payment won’t go through.

Here are some steps they can try:

  • Switch browser or try a private/incognito window. Suggest they try again in a different browser, a private window, or on another device.
  • Check with their payment provider. Their bank might block the payment for safety reasons, especially if it’s outside their normal spending pattern or being sent to a new recipient.
  • Try a different payment method. If one method isn’t working, they can switch to a different funding source when checking out with PayPal, or use a different card.
  • Make sure their PayPal account is verified. Their card should be verified on PayPal. You can point them to PayPal’s support guide here.
  • Keep things up to date. Outdated browsers or devices might not support all PayPal features. Updates can help.
  • Log in to PayPal on a desktop. They should check for any account notices at paypal.com that could be blocking the payment.
  • Reach out to PayPal support. If the issue continues, they can visit the PayPal support page or scroll to ‘Recommended’ and select ‘Message us’ for live chat.

For more details, supporters can explore PayPal’s payment troubleshooting guide under Payments > Payment Declined.

Note

Since supporters pay you directly, the issue might be with your connected PayPal account. Check out the section below to make sure everything’s working on your end.

Still not working? Ask them to contact us with your Ko-fi page link (like ko-fi.com/yourusername) and let us know what they’ve already tried. We’ll help from there.

Trouble with memberships or recurring payments

If a supporter is trying to set up a recurring payment (like a membership), they’ll need to use a bank card that allows subscriptions. PayPal balance or prepaid cards can’t be used for recurring payments.

They should also check that their PayPal account has a verified card or bank account linked.

If they’re still stuck, they can reach out to us.

Other reasons why supporter payments may fail

🌍 Country restrictions

PayPal has different rules depending on the country. Supporters can check their country here. Some examples:

  • Some countries don’t allow PayPal payments to users in the same country.
  • Some restrict commercial PayPal transactions due to local regulations.
  • Some PayPal accounts can only send (but not receive) payments.
💲 Local payment processors

In certain countries, PayPal offers local options like iDeal, Giropay, or Przelewy24. Sometimes these payments look successful but don’t complete. A refund should usually arrive in 5 working days.

Supporters can try using direct PayPal or card payments instead, or contact PayPal or the local provider if needed.

💳 Prepaid cards

PayPal does allow some prepaid cards, but not all. If a supporter’s payment fails:

  • They should check if PayPal accepts their card.
  • Their card provider may be blocking the transaction.
  • Prepaid cards can’t be used for recurring payments like memberships.

If your supporter is still having trouble, check below for what steps you can take.

What you can check when supporters get an error

Sometimes the issue is with your account, not the supporter’s. Here’s a quick checklist to make sure everything’s set up correctly on your end:

  • Email and currency settings: Make sure the email linked to your PayPal account is verified. Also check your account can accept the currencies your supporters are using. You can read more here.
  • Account restrictions or notifications: Log into PayPal on a desktop and look for any notices that might be stopping you from receiving payments.

Still not able to receive payments via PayPal? Check out our help article here.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌
