FAQ

Can supporters use PayPal balance for memberships?

No. PayPal requires a continuous payment method (like a bank card) for subscriptions and memberships.

Can supporters pay using a prepaid card?

Some prepaid cards are accepted, but not all. It’s best to check with PayPal Support or the card provider directly. Prepaid cards can’t be used for recurring payments.

Is PayPal allowed in my supporter’s country?

Supporters can check this PayPal country list or contact PayPal directly for more info on region restrictions.

My supporter was charged but the payment failed. Do they get a refund?

Yes. If a payment fails but shows as charged, PayPal or the payment processor should return it within 3-5 working days. If not, they can contact us.

Why did my supporter’s card payment fail?

If someone is having trouble supporting you with a card payment, don’t worry. On Ko-fi, creators (like you!) connect their own PayPal and/or Stripe accounts to accept payments. That means the issue could be on your end or theirs, but we’re here to help you both get things working.

If your supporter is having trouble paying through PayPal, check out our guide here to help get them sorted.

Helping supporters fix card payment issues

If a card payment fails, your supporter might see an error like this:

  • “Your payment could not be processed.”
  • “We could not process your payment at this time. Please try again later.”

Sometimes they don’t see an error at all, and the payment just doesn’t go through.

Here are a few things they can try:

  • Try a different browser or private mode. Switching browsers or using incognito mode can help. They can also clear their cache or try another device.
  • Disable VPNs or pop-up blockers. These might block verification windows needed to complete the payment.
  • Double-check payment details. The supporter should make sure their card details and billing address are correct.
  • Confirm their card supports foreign payments. If they’re paying in a different currency, their bank might need to authorize the transaction.
  • Check their card provider’s site on desktop. Logging in may show important alerts or security blocks.
  • Contact their card provider. Payments can be blocked due to security concerns, especially if the supporter hasn’t supported you before.
  • Try another payment method. If one card doesn’t work, they can switch to another or try PayPal if available.
  • Update browser or device. Some payment providers require the latest browser or OS versions.
  • Wait before trying again. Too many attempts in a short time can block payments. Waiting 24 hours may help.
  • Contact PayPal support. If they’re paying via card through PayPal, they may need to contact PayPal for help.

Note

Payments go directly to you. If the supporter’s payment method seems fine, the issue could be with your connected PayPal or Stripe account. More on that below.

If they’re still stuck, feel free to contact us on their behalf. Let us know your Ko-fi link (e.g. ko-fi.com/yourname) and what the supporter has already tried.

Issues with recurring payments or memberships

For recurring payments (like memberships), the supporter needs to use a card that supports subscriptions. Prepaid cards can’t be used for these.

Other common reasons for payment failures

🌍 Country restrictions

PayPal and Stripe have different rules depending on the country. If your supporter is in a region with payment limits, this could cause issues.

  • Some countries don’t allow domestic transactions through PayPal due to local laws.
  • Other regions may restrict international transactions altogether.

It’s always worth checking with PayPal or the supporter’s bank for country-specific restrictions.

💳 Prepaid and gift cards

PayPal and Stripe sometimes allow prepaid or gift cards, but not all cards are accepted. If a supporter is using one:

  • Ask them to contact the card provider to check if it’s accepted by Stripe or PayPal.
  • Make sure the card is fully activated and authorized. Some cards may require additional steps to activate.
  • Remember, prepaid cards can’t be used for recurring memberships or subscriptions.

What to check as a creator

Sometimes payment failures happen because of an issue with your connected account.

Log into PayPal or Stripe on desktop and check for any notices or restrictions that might stop payments from going through.

This includes things like identity verification, account setup issues, or country restrictions.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

FAQ

Can supporters use prepaid cards?

Yes, some prepaid cards work, but not all. Have your supporter check with their card provider to confirm they work with PayPal/Stripe.

Note prepaid/gift cards can’t be used for recurring payments like memberships.

A supporter says they were charged but the payment failed. Will they get a refund?

Yes, any pending charges should be automatically refunded by the payment provider within 3-5 business days. The charge may also disappear from their statement as well.

If your supporter is still seeing a charge, they can contact us.

A supporter saw the error: “requirements.disabled_reason”. What does that mean?

This error means your Stripe account cannot accept payments at this time and is likely restricted.

You can log in to Stripe to view any restrictions on your account.

Once you resolve the issue, you can disconnect and connect your Stripe account again here.

A supporter saw “PayPal can’t add this card”. What now?

If they’re paying by card through PayPal, they might see this error. It usually means the card is already linked to a PayPal account - part of PayPal’s security checks.

If that’s the case, advise them to use their PayPal account to make the payment instead.

Integrations

Adding a Ko-fi Button to your WordPress site or blog

Make it simple for your readers to show their support by adding a “Buy Me a Coffee” or “Support Me on Ko-fi” button on your WordPress site. Be it a cute little button, a floating icon, or neatly placed on a widget — it’s all possible within three easy steps. ☕️

Note

You’ll need a WordPress Business account for sites on wordpress.com to install plugins. If you are using the personal plan, you can simply download an image from our brand assets or create your own. Then add your Ko-fi link to it! Like our button below.

Use it to share your:

Ko-fi page: ko-fi.com

Membership Tiers: ko-fi.com

Commissions: ko-fi.com

Shop: ko-fi.com

ko-fi.com/zeeisdigital

Step 1: Install the Ko-fi Plugin 📥

To install the plugin, you’ll need a WP Business account for sites on wordpress.com, or hosting with a third-party service such as Hostgator, GoDaddy, Bluehost, among thousands of others.

  • Go to your WordPress dashboard, click on ‘Plugins’
  • Click ‘Add New’ and type ‘Ko-fi’ into the search bar
  • When you see the Ko-fi plugin, click ‘Install Now’, then ‘Activate’

Step 2: Add your Page Name to settings 🌐

Now that you’ve got the plugin it’s time to set it up, so that WordPress knows where to direct your readers.

  • Go to ‘Settings’ on your WordPress dashboard, then ‘Ko-fi Settings’
  • In ‘Page Name or ID’, enter your Ko-fi username, for example, ko-fi.com/yourusername
  • Press “Save Changes” below

Step 3: Decide Where to Display Your Button 📍

Choose where it should live on your site and make sure your button fits with your content and theme.

🎈 As a floating button on your site

The floating button will always be visible as visitors browse your site. When clicked, it brings up your tip panel for your readers to easily support you without leaving your site.

Change the text and appearance of your button in Settings > Ko-fi Settings

  • In ‘Default Display’ choose if the button should default to showing on all pages or no pages. You can then override the setting on individual pages
  • Update your button text to reflect the action you’d like visitors to take, such as ‘Support My Page’ or ‘Join My Membership’
  • Choose your Button Colour to fit your WordPress theme

Remember to press “Save Changes” below.

Can I choose which posts and pages not to display my Ko-fi button?

Yes, you can choose whether your Ko-fi button appears in specific pages or posts:

  • Head to Dashboard and then either Posts or Pages
  • Find the post or page you want to edit
  • Click Settings on the top right > Choose the Page Tab
  • Find the Ko-fi option at the bottom, hide or show your Ko-fi link
🧩 As an animated button in a widget area which links to your page

ko-fi.com/teaandinksociety

Widget areas and sidebars, like footers on a page, are perfect spots to add Ko-fi widgets.

When a reader clicks the ‘Ko-fi Button’ in a widget area, it takes them to your Ko-fi page.

Add a widget by heading to ‘Appearance’ > ‘Widgets’, selecting your preferred area (like the footer), and pick ‘Ko-fi Button’.

Customize your button by clicking on the widget for available options. Change it to fit your theme and content.

  • Add your Page Name, for example ko-fi.com/yourusername
  • Update your button text to reflect the action you’d like visitors to take, such as ‘Support My Page’ or ‘Join My Membership’
  • Choose your Button Colour to fit your WordPress theme
  • Select a Title and Description as a prompt to your link
  • Choose where you want your link to go using Button Alignment
🖼️ As a tip panel in a widget area

ko-fi.com/winelovermagazine

Widget areas and sidebars, like footers on a page, are perfect spots to add Ko-fi widgets.

The ‘Ko-fi Tip Panel’ in a widget area is always visible. Use this widget to allow readers to support easily without leaving the post they are reading or your site.

Add a widget by heading to ‘Appearance’ > ‘Widgets’, selecting your preferred area (like the sidebar), and pick ‘Ko-fi Tip Panel’.

Customize your Tip Panel by clicking on the widget for available options. If you want to change the text on the panel itself, head to your Payment Page.

  • Select a Title that goes above your Tip Panel
  • Add your Page Name, for example ko-fi.com/yourusername
🔘 As a shortcode, which adds an animated button on any post or page

ko-fi.com/kimurayuki

For your button to appear, just add ‘[kofi]’ as a shortcode in the text of your post or page. When clicked, it directs to your ko-fi page. Encourage your readers to commission you, join your membership tiers, tip, and much more.

It’s perfect to end a post and get support from your readers.

Change the text and appearance of your button in Settings > Ko-fi Settings

  • Add your Page Name, for example ko-fi.com/yourusername
  • Update your button text to reflect the action you’d like visitors to take, such as ‘Support My Page’ or ‘Join My Membership’
  • Choose your Button Colour to fit your WordPress theme

Remember to press “Save Changes” below.

And… voilà! 🎉 Your button is now live and ready to collect some love from your readers!

🧱 Using Ko-fi Blocks (Block Editor)

If you’re using the WordPress Block Editor, you can add Ko-fi buttons and panels directly as native blocks — no shortcodes needed.

To add a new block, click the + icon in the editor and search for Ko-fi. You’ll see two options:

  • Ko-fi Donate Button — adds an animated Ko-fi button to your post or page
  • Ko-fi Donation Panel — adds the full tip panel inline

Both blocks offer the same customisation options as before — button text, colour, and your Ko-fi page name.

Converting existing shortcodes to blocks

If you have existing pages using Ko-fi shortcodes, you can convert them to blocks in a couple of clicks:

  • Open the page or post in the Block Editor
  • Your existing Ko-fi shortcode will appear as a Classic block
  • Click the block, then select Transform to > Ko-fi Donate Button — your shortcode will be replaced with the native Ko-fi block, keeping all your existing settings

Converting widgets to block widgets

You can also upgrade your Ko-fi widgets to block-based widgets:

  • Head to Appearance > Widgets
  • Find your existing Ko-fi Button or Ko-fi Panel widget and click Transform to > Ko-fi Donate Button or Ko-fi Donation Panel
  • Your widget will be updated to use the block version, with all settings carried over

Note

Ko-fi Blocks are available from the latest version of the Ko-fi WordPress plugin. Make sure your plugin is up to date to see them in the editor.

To learn more about WordPress, visit the WordPress Support Center.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

Connect Ko-fi to thousands of apps with Zapier

Zapier lets you connect Ko-fi to thousands of online services like Google Sheets, Mailchimp, Trello, Discord, and more. Create “zaps” to automate what happens when you receive a payment on Ko-fi. For example, you can automatically add new Ko-fi Supporters to your mailing list on Mailchimp.

What is a zap?

A zap is an automated workflow you create to accomplish a task. It consists of:

  • a Trigger what event starts the workflow
  • an Action what task is to be completed by the workflow.

Here’s an example of a zap:

When I receive a new tip on Ko-fi, add this payment as a row in my Google sheet.

In this example, receiving a new tip is the trigger. Adding the payment as a row in the sheet is the action.

You can create zaps like these without needing any coding knowledge.

Create your first zap

Zapier connects one app as the Trigger (where your information comes from) with one or more Actions in other apps (where your data gets sent automatically).

Let’s create your Trigger:

  • Log in to your Zapier account or create a new account.
  • Use the search bar to find Ko-fi.
  • Click “Connect to 6000+ apps” under Ko-fi.
  • Follow the prompts to fill in your Ko-fi details and set up your zap:
    • App & event: Choose “New payment” as your event.
    • Account: Connect your Ko-fi account using your Page ID and API key.
    • You can find your Page ID and API key in the Zapier tab on your Ko-fi page. This allows Zapier to access your page’s information whenever you receive a payment, ensuring your Zap works smoothly.
    • Trigger: Choose the types of payments you want to trigger a zap. You can change this later if needed.
    • Test: If you’ve received any payments on Ko-fi, you can test your Trigger. If not, you can also test it later in your Zapier tab on Ko-fi.
  • After setting up your Trigger, it’s time to add an Action to complete your zap. You can follow our Discord example (link below to discord) or explore more ideas here (link below to popular lists).

That’s it! You’ve created your first automation. You can start with a pre-made zap or create your own with the zap Editor.

Note

You can duplicate zaps you’ve made before, or Zapier can guide you through creating a new one. For more help, check out their Help Center.

Send a message on Discord when you receive a payment

Now that you’ve set up your trigger when you receive a payment on Ko-fi, let’s create an action of sending a message to a Discord channel when you get paid:

  • Select Discord as your Action
  • Search for and select Discord as your Action app.
  • Set up the action by deciding what happens when a payment triggers your zap:
  • App & Event: Choose an event, such as “Send Channel Message” when a payment is received. Other options include sending a DM or posting in a forum.
  • Account: Select the Discord account where the action will take place.
  • Action: Customize the message, including options like pinging a specific role or formatting the message content.
  • Test: You can test the action using a sample payment provided by Zapier. Just press “Test.”
  • Complete your zap: That’s it! Now, each time you receive a payment on Ko-fi, your chosen message will automatically appear in Discord.

Popular things to do with Zapier

Zapier offers endless possibilities for automating tasks with Ko-fi. To help you get started, here are some popular integrations:

  • Ko-fi > Google Sheets
  • Create new sheet rows for each payment you receive on Ko-fi, including tips, subscriptions, commissions, and shop purchases.
  • Ko-fi > Discord
  • Automatically send a custom channel message in Discord every time you receive a payment on Ko-fi.
  • Ko-fi > Mailchimp
  • Keep in touch with your supporters by adding them to your Mailchimp lists whenever they tip, subscribe, commission, or buy from you on Ko-fi.
  • Ko-fi > Trello
  • Manage your commissions by creating new Trello cards for each new commission on Ko-fi. Track progress and never miss a commission!
  • Ko-fi > Gmail
  • Use your Gmail account to automatically send personalized emails to supporters each time you receive a payment.

Explore more integrations in Zapier’s full library for Ko-fi!

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way - if you have any feedback, let us know.💌

Connect Ko-fi to Twitch for chat alerts and more

Connect Ko-fi to Twitch to get payment alerts in your stream chat, promote your Ko-fi page, and even create Twitch clips when you get a payment!

Connecting to Twitch

It’s simple and easy to connect to Twitch and set up alerts, commands, and more!

  • Head over to Stream Alerts and tap Connect to Twitch.
  • Ko-fi will ask permission to access basic info to post alerts, respond to commands, and create clips.
  • Once connected, you will be able to customize your Twitch settings (do this at any time from the Settings). You’ll also see a message in your Twitch chat confirming your connection.

Twitch settings

Decide what you want the Ko-fi stream bot to do from your Twitch Settings. Just go to your Stream Alert settings and tap Settings.

  • Twitch chat alerts - Toggle this on if you’d like to show payment alerts live in your Twitch chat.
  • Chat goal progress - If you have a goal, this will show your progress in your Twitch chat each time you get a payment to encourage more support.
  • Respond to commands - This will let your audience easily locate your Ko-fi links by typing a command in the chat. The available commands are:
    • !kofi or !ko-fi - Share your Ko-fi page.
    • !kofishop - Share your Shop link.
    • !koficommissions -Share your Commissions link.
    • !kofitiers - Share your Memberships link. Add these commands to your stream title or about section for easy access!
  • Create Twitch clips - The Ko-fi stream bot will create a Twitch clip when you receive a payment while streaming. We’ll email you the link to the clip to share, or you can access it from your Payments & Orders. Just look for the Twitch icon next to your payment to see it!
  • Timed messages in chat - You can set the Ko-fi stream bot to automatically share your Ko-fi page with timed messages. This is a really useful way to remind viewers of the best way to support you.
  • Disconnect - This disconnects Ko-fiStreamBot from your Twitch.

Check out our help article here for more info on stream alerts!

We’re here if you need us or if you have any suggestions. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

Frequently Asked Questions

How do I send a test alert?

You can send a test alert to test your connection and confirm what will be sent in chat when you receive a tip.

  • Go to your Stream Alert settings.
  • Scroll to Twitch Chatbot.
  • Click the link to send a test alert.

The test alert will then show in your Twitch chat.

How do I change the message sent in chat when I receive a payment?

Choose the message that displays when you receive a payment.

  • Go to your Stream Alert settings > Alert Text.
  • Enter the message you want to be displayed when someone tips. Use placeholders to show the Supporter’s display name, the value of the tip, and the message they left when they supported you.

Learn more about Ko-fi stream alerts here!

Can I change the frequency of timed messages in chat?

Decide how often the bot should promote your page via your Stream Alert settings by clicking Settings under Twitch Chatbot.

How do I disconnect from Twitch?

If you need to disconnect the Ko-fi stream bot from Twitch, you can do so by going to your Stream Alert settings > Twitch Chatbot > Settings > Disconnect.

You can also disconnect via your Twitch Connections by scrolling to Other Connections and selecting Disconnect.

Ko-fi tip widget

The Ko-fi tip widget lets you embed a tip button or widget directly onto your website or blog. Supporters can easily tip or become a member without leaving your site. It’s hassle-free support, right where your community is. ☕

ko-fi.com/psuzume

Types of widgets

When it comes to widgets, Ko-fi offers two options - a floating button and a tip panel.

Floating Button Tip Panel

The Floating Button expands when clicked, revealing your tip panel.

To set it up, head to your Widget setup and click Floating Button.

  • You can change the Call-to-Action that will be shown on the button, e.g. Support Me, Support Us, Donate, etc. You can also choose the background color of the button here.

  • Once you’ve customized your button, just copy the script and paste it into your site’s code before the closing body tag. It should look something like this:

    <p>My website</p>

    Welcome to my website!

    Paste Widget Script here, just before the closing body tag.

Tip

You can check how your Floating Button will appear by clicking the preview button on the bottom left side of your screen.

If you have any questions on the Floating Button widget, feel free to reach out!

Opt for the Tip Panel if you prefer a dedicated space, such as a sidebar, footer, or page on your website or blog, exclusively for tip. Unlike the Floating Button, the Tip Panel remains fully visible at all times, offering immediate access without the need to click to expand.

To set up your Tip Panel, go to your Widgets page and click Tip Panel. Here you can see a preview of your widget and find the code. To embed the panel on your site, just copy the code and paste it anywhere that lets you embed HTML.

Questions on how the tip panel works? Contact us here!

Adding your widget

Ko-fi provides you with code for both the floating button and the tip panel, which you can use on your own website or blog. Here are a few places you can use them.

WordPress Self-Hosted Squarespace Wix Google

Creators who use WordPress can use the official Ko-fi Button plugin. To install the plugin, you’ll need a WordPress Business account for sites on wordpress.com, or hosting with a third-party service such as Hostgator, GoDaddy, Bluehost, among thousands of others.

If you use WordPress, you can use the official Ko-fi plugin. To do this, you’ll need to be using a version of WordPress that allows custom plugins. If you use wordpress.com, you’ll need a Business account. If you host elsewhere, custom plugins are usually allowed.

For an in-depth guide on how to set up your widget on WordPress, check out our help article here.

For self-hosted sites, it’s just a matter of adding the code to your site’s HTML.

The floating button will need to be added to your site’s footer. The tip panel can be added anywhere you have dedicated space for it, such as the Header, Footer, Sidebar, or even its own page!

ko-fi.com/scorelines

You can use Code Injection to add the Ko-fi Widget to your Squarespace site.

Note

Code injection is a premium feature on Squarespace, requiring one of their Business, Commerce, Professional, or Premium plans.

  • Open the Code Injection panel.
  • Navigate to the Footer field.
  • Go to your Ko-fi Widget page and copy & paste the Floating Button code.
  • After adding your code, click Save.

For a full guide on code injection, check out Squarespace’s help article here.

ko-fi.com/kenonjeffers

To add the widget to your Wix site, you must have a connected domain, which requires a Wix Premium Plan.

If you have a connected domain and want to add the Ko-fi widget, you can follow Wix’s guide here.

You can also add the Ko-fi Tip Panel to your Wix blog if you have one.

  • Go to Blog in your site’s dashboard.
  • Click Edit on the relevant blog post, or Create New Post to write a new one.
  • Click Add on the left side, and select HTML code.
  • Click Edit HTML and select HTML Code.
  • Copy & Paste the code for the tip Panel via the widget page.
  • Click Save.

Voila! Now your blog post will have your tip panel embedded, allowing readers to support you directly.

Note

This method works with the tip panel only.

Check out Wix’s full guide on adding HTML to a blog post here.

You can add the tip panel to your Google Site

  • Navigate to your site editor and click Embed on the right side of the page.
  • Click Embed Code.
  • Navigate to your Ko-fi Widget page and copy the code for the Tip Panel.
  • Copy & Paste the code into the field and click Next.
  • A pop-up will appear previewing your widget. After confirming it’s appearing properly, hit Insert.
  • Position the tip panel where you’d like.

And there you have it! Your Tip Panel will now show on your site, allowing visitors to support you without leaving the page!

More information can be found on Google’s official help article here.

FAQ

My widget is not showing on my site!

If you’re using one of the hosts above, check out the guide to make sure you’ve followed the steps and have a website hosting plan that supports adding custom code. If you’ve done that and you’re still having trouble, feel free to reach out!

Can I offer memberships/monthly payments through the widget?

Yes! Memberships/monthly payments are offered through the widget as well as one-time tips.

Your widget can even default to memberships so that’s the first thing offered. Simply go to your Payment Settings > enable Default to Memberships.

Can I customize the appearance of my floating button widget?

You can change the floating button’s color and the call-to-action (Support Me, Donate, Tip Me, etc.).

Consider creating buttons in your own style and linking them to your Ko-fi page directly!

Can I offer my Ko-fi Shop/Commissions through the widget?

At this time, there isn’t a way to embed your Ko-fi Shop or Commissions using the widget.

You can link directly to your shop or commissions page by adding /shop or /commissions to your Ko-fi page link. Consider creating custom buttons that link directly to your Shop and Commissions!

Is the widget free to use?

The widget lets you implement the Ko-fi tip panel on your page. Payments will have a 0-5% fee.

For a breakdown of how fees work on Ko-fi, check out our help article here.

Can supporters tip using Apple Pay/Google Pay/etc. in the widget?

This is not currently possible, however this is something we’re looking into!

Setting up Discord rewards with Ko-fi

Connect your Discord server to Ko-fi to grant your supporters and members exclusive roles and invites.

Note

To set up Discord rewards, you’ll need to be a Contributor. Learn more about contributor status here. If you’re not already a Contributor, you’ll be prompted to opt in when you add your Discord server or adjust your roles.

Setting up Discord roles

1. Connect Discord to Ko-fi
  • Head over to your Discord Settings and hit “Connect Discord Server”. Once you’re logged in to Discord, choose the server you wish to link and click “Continue”.
  • Next, grant Ko-fi the necessary permissions to Manage Roles and Create Invites. Simply keep these options selected and hit “Authorize”.
  • Voilà 🎉 Your Discord server is now connected to Ko-fi.

Note

You can disconnect Discord from Ko-fi anytime by clicking the “Connected” button. However, doing so will prevent Ko-fi bot from managing roles.

2. Make Ko-fi bot the top role

You’ll need to make sure that Ko-fi bot is at the top of your Roles list (Server Settings > Roles). Keep Ko-fi bot at the top of the roles list to make sure your supporters get the roles they expect. It’s normal for Ko-fi bot to appear offline. Ko-fi bot can invite users to your server and assign roles while it is offline.

For further guidance on setting up roles in Discord, take a look at Discord’s own “How do I set up Permissions?” article.

3. Assign roles

You can easily assign Discord roles to your supporters, whether they’re one-time or recurring supporters or members of one of your membership tiers.

To assign the role, head to your Discord settings.

Ko-fi bot will automatically remove roles from monthly supporters and members when their support stops. For one-time supporters, you’ll need to remove the role yourself in the Discord server.

Assigning specific roles for tier members

In your Membership settings, you can attach Discord roles to any existing tier.

Additionally, when you create a new tier, you’ll be prompted to add a Discord role.

How supporters claim Discord roles

After making a payment, supporters without a Ko-fi account will be prompted to create one and then link their Discord account. Once this is done, Ko-fi bot will automatically apply the roles they’re entitled to.

If supporters already have a Ko-fi account, they simply need to connect their Discord account, and Ko-fi will handle the rest.

For those without a Discord account, they’ll need to sign up for one on Discord’s platform.

If your supporters have trouble, lead them to our guide here.

Supporter didn’t get their role

If your supporter is missing their role on Discord, here are a few troubleshooting steps to resolve the issue:

  • Check Discord connection: Ensure that your Discord server is connected in your Discord settings.
  • Note
  • You can re-authorize Discord by clicking “Connected” to disconnect. Then reconnect and log in to Discord again if needed. Your roles are re-synced if you edit your roles, you get paid, or reconnect your Discord.
  • Check role setup: Confirm the roles you’re offering are set up correctly, and make sure your role is not managed by a third party (Twitch, Patreon, etc). Review your Discord tab and your Membership Tiers.
  • Priority for Ko-fi bot: It’s essential to place Ko-fi bot at the top of your permissions list in Discord to ensure smooth role assignment and removal.
  • Supporter assistance: Encourage your supporter to refer to our help article on How to Join a Creator’s Discord Server for guidance. You can quickly check that a Supporter has connected their Discord in My Supporters/Members tab.

If you’ve completed the checklist and you’re still having problems, please don’t hesitate to raise a support ticket for further assistance. We’re here to help!

We’d love you to join our vibrant community of Ko-fi creators on Discord.

🚀 Join Ko-fi’s Discord here and meet like-minded creators, grow your income together and much more!

We’re here if you need us or if you have any suggestions. Feel free to send any questions our way or visit Discord support. 💌

Frequently Asked Questions

Will my existing monthly subscribers/members get access to their Discord roles?

Yes, the Discord roles will automatically be assigned to existing subscribers or members within 10 minutes. The supporter must have their Discord connected to Ko-fi in their Discord settings.

Please note that one-off supporters who contributed before you set up Discord won’t automatically receive Discord roles. They’ll need to support you again to access them.

Is it possible to add Discord roles to Membership tiers?

Yes, you can set up different roles for each one of your Membership Tiers.

Do role privileges expire for one-off supporters?

No, these roles do not expire. If necessary, you will have to remove their privileges manually within Discord.

Do role privileges expire for monthly subscribers and tier members?

Yes, when a subscriber or member cancels their subscription, their Discord role will be removed at the same time their subscription ends.

Why is Ko-fi bot appearing offline in Discord?

It is normal for Ko-fi bot to appear offline. The bot can invite users to your server and assign roles while it is offline.

When you authorize the bot to join your server, it gains all the necessary permissions to perform its functions. Just ensure that you don’t alter any of its settings or permissions.

Does my supporter need a Ko-fi account to claim a Discord role?

Yes, your supporter will need to have a Ko-fi account to claim a Discord role. Learn more about the process in our article here.

What happens when my member’s payment is overdue?

When a member’s payment is overdue, it means the recurring charge has failed, and they will promptly lose their Discord role while remaining in your server. Once the payment succeeds, Ko-fi bot will reassign their role automatically.

For further guidance, supporters can refer to our Supporter FAQ when a subscription goes overdue.

Will my other Discord bots affect Ko-fi bot?

Other bots might interfere with the Ko-fi bot if these other bots manage user permissions.

Make sure that Ko-fi bot has “Create Invite” and “Manage Roles” permissions.

How can I find a Supporter’s Discord username?

You might need a supporter’s username to confirm payments or help confirm roles.

To find this, you can click on the supporter in My Supporters/Members tab and view their connected Discord.

or

  • On a desktop device, download your CSV via your Payments and Orders tab, Supporter’s tab, or Member’s tab.
  • Head to the “Discord Username” column on the CSV spreadsheet.

🔗 Connect your Ko-fi page to Google Analytics

Link your Ko-fi page with Google Analytics to learn more about visitors to your page. Find out where they’re coming from, which of your posts get the most views and much more. Use this information to make your page and content even better.

Note

To use Google Analytics, you’ll need to be a Contributor. Learn more about Contributors here. If you’re not already a Contributor, you’ll be prompted to opt in when you add your Google Analytics ID to Ko-fi.

How to connect Google Analytics to my Ko-fi page

You’ll have to create something called a Data Stream. This lets Google Analytics keep an eye on your page. You’ll get a special code called a Measurement ID. Paste this code into your Ko-fi page settings. This helps Google Analytics see who’s visiting your page and what they’re doing.

  • Head to your Google Analytics Dashboard.
  • Look for the Admin Button on the bottom left.
  • On the right, under Data collection and modification, click Data Streams .
  • Click Add Stream and select Web .
  • Add in your Ko-fi URL for the Website URL (e.g., ko-fi.com/yourusername).
  • Enter a Stream Name, which can be your Ko-fi username.
  • Copy your Measurement ID.
  • Paste it into your Page Settings on Ko-fi.

That’s it! Within a few hours, you should start seeing data in Google Analytics.

What can I use Google Analytics data for?

By linking Google Analytics to your Ko-fi page, you can open up a world of possibilities.

Here are just a few examples:

  • 🌍 Perfect timing for your posts: Use viewer traffic by country data to schedule your content when your audience is most active. Increase views and engagement by sharing your posts at the best time.
  • Head to: Reports User Demographic Details
  • 📈 Content tailored to your viewer’s preferences: Look at the “Pages and Screens” section to discover your most popular posts. Make your content based on what your viewers like.
  • Head to: Reports Engagement Pages and screens
  • 🔗 Get the most out of sharing your links: Discover insights from traffic acquisition data. Find out which channels or websites bring traffic to your page and share again to reach a larger audience.
  • Head to: Reports Acquisition Traffic Acquisition

How do I find my Google Analytics ID?

To find your Google Analytics ID:

  • Head to your Google Analytics Dashboard.
  • Look for the Admin Button on the bottom left.
  • Under Data collection and modification, click Data Streams .
  • Select the stream you want the Google Analytics ID for.
  • The Measurement ID is your Google Analytics ID.

Tip

The previous “Tracking ID or Universal Analytics property” was formatted with a ’U-12345”. Make sure to use your Google Analytics 4 ID (formatted as “G-12345”) to utilize the latest updates. For more information, refer to Google’s help article here.

What if I don’t have a Google Analytics account

If you don’t have a Google Analytics account yet, follow these steps to sign up:

- Go to [Google Analytics](https://analytics.google.com/analytics/web/provision/?authuser=0#provision/SignUp/) and set up your new account.
- Enter an Account Name, which can be your Ko-fi username.
- Input a Property Name, such as "Most Popular Posts" for tracking purposes.
- Type in your Business Details, including your Industry and business size.
- Choose the relevant Business Objectives.
- For Data Collection, select Website.
- Add your Ko-fi URL as the Website URL (e.g., ko-fi.com/yourusername).
- Copy and use your Measurement ID.
- Paste it into your [Page Settings](https://ko-fi.com/settings?tab=page) on Ko-fi.

Within a few hours, you should start seeing data in Google Analytics.

Where can I find more information on Google Analytics

To learn more about how to use Google Analytics, visit their Help Centre here.

FAQ

What fees does Stripe charge?

Stripe charges their regular transaction fees, which can vary depending on your account type, location, and the currencies used. Generally, it’s around 3% + $0.30.

For more info on Stripe’s fees, check out their page here.

How do I connect my Stripe account?

Connecting your Stripe account is easy! To find out how to link your Stripe account and start accepting payments, check out our help article here.

What are Stripe’s payout wait times?

When you receive a payment through Ko-fi, the money goes directly to your Stripe account.

Stripe’s payout times may vary, usually within 7-14 days after the first successful payment. You can manage your payout schedule in your Stripe Dashboard.

For additional details on payouts, take a look at Stripe’s article here.

How does Stripe handle currency conversion?

Stripe will automatically convert payments to your set currency. More info on currency can be found here.

Can I use the same PayPal or Stripe account for multiple Ko-fi pages?

If you need multiple Ko-fi pages, that’s fine. You can even link them all to the same PayPal and Stripe account so the money from your different projects or personas is sent to the same place.

How to set up multiple Ko-fi pages

To create a page when you already have one:

  • Whilst logged out of your current Ko-fi, visit Ko-fi’s sign-up page to start.
  • You’ll need to use an email address that’s unique to that Ko-fi account.
  • You can connect to the same PayPal and Stripe accounts to all your pages. Just set this up in your payment settings.

These steps will have your new pages up and running smoothly! 🌈👍

Do I need multiple Ko-fi pages?

It depends on your interests and goals:

Multiple accounts are best if you have different passions or businesses and want to keep them separate. This is especially useful if you have different audiences.

Single Ko-fi accounts are best if you create within one niche and want to offer different features like Commissions, Memberships, and Ko-fi Shop.

FAQ

Can I merge Ko-fi pages?

Currently, account merging is not available, but we’ve taken note of this as a feature that creators and supporters might find useful.

Can I share my logins with anyone?

We don’t recommend sharing your logins; they should be kept for your eyes only.

If I pay for Ko-fi Gold membership, can it apply to both pages?

You’ll need a separate Ko-fi Gold subscription for each Ko-fi account you have.

Get the most out of Stripe: setting up payment notifications if you offer memberships

Once your Stripe account is connected, there are a couple of settings worth switching on to help your memberships run as smoothly as possible.

By turning on Stripe’s built-in notification emails, members are automatically guided through fixing any payment issues — whether that’s an expired card, updated details, or a bank security check (3D Secure).

It only takes a few minutes to set up, and it’s a great way to make sure your members have the best experience supporting you. Here’s how to do it:

Turn on failed payment emails

This lets Stripe reach out to members automatically if a payment doesn’t go through, with a link to update their details.

  • Head to your Stripe Billing Settings.
  • Under Billing > Subscriptions and emails, head to Email notifications and customer management.
  • Under Customer emails, turn on: “Send emails when card payments fail.”
  • Select Link to a Stripe-hosted page.
  • Select Save once finished.

Turn on 3DS authentication emails

This makes sure members are notified whenever their bank needs them to approve a payment, so nothing slips through the gaps.

  • From the same Subscriptions and emails page, scroll down to Manage payments that require confirmation.
  • Enable 3D Secure.
  • Turn on customer emails to notify members when authentication is needed.
  • Select Save once finished.

With both settings on, Stripe will handle the communication with your members whenever their payment needs attention, so you can focus on creating. 💪

Note

These settings may look slightly different depending on your location or how your Stripe account is set up. If you’re having trouble finding them, feel free to reach out to us or contact Stripe support directly.

For more help with Stripe, check out our guide on connecting your Stripe account.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

FAQ

My supporter says their subscription keeps cancelling

If a payment requires authentication and isn’t completed, or fails to renew multiple times, the subscription may be cancelled automatically.

This is usually a sign that authentication was required, or there was an issue collecting the funds, but your member wasn’t notified in time.

Turning on the settings above will help prevent this from happening.

Why does my member need to re-approve their payment?

Some banks require extra verification for recurring payments as a security measure; this is called 3D Secure (3DS).

When this is required, your member needs to approve the payment before it can go through.

Neither Ko-fi or Stripe have control over when a bank requests this, which is why having notifications turned on is so helpful.

How can I tell if a payment has failed because of 3DS?

Head to your Stripe Dashboard and look for any payments with a status of Incomplete linked to a subscription update. If you click into the individual payment, you’ll be able to see if 3DS authentication was requested. If you’re seeing this regularly, the settings above are the fix.

Can I see if a member has received an email from Stripe?

In your Stripe Dashboard, head to the Customers tab and select the member you’d like to check. Scroll down to Sent emails to see a history of all emails Stripe has sent them in the last 30 days.

Helping your supporters with PayPal errors

Are your supporters seeing PayPal issues when they try to support you? As the creator, it’s your PayPal or Stripe account that gets paid directly through Ko-fi. The error might be in your account or on your supporter’s side. No worries, we’re here to help figure out what could be going wrong.

Solving PayPal errors

Encountering PayPal errors can be a headache. Here’s a quick guide to check through:

Note

If you’re receiving payments from other supporters without issues, the issue might be on your supporter’s side. Check out our help article here, and it could help to send this guide to your supporter.

  • Check the PayPal error message that your supporters are seeing. This can point to specific issues such as problems with your account, currency issues, or insufficient funds. These messages often provide hints about what’s going wrong.
  • Visit your Payment settings on Ko-fi. Look for any alerts or notices – these will usually come with instructions on what you need to do with your PayPal account.
  • Log in to PayPal on a desktop and check for any restrictions or important messages.
  • Make sure your PayPal email for payments matches the one linked to your Ko-fi account.
  • Verify your bank account on PayPal; this might smooth things out for your supporters.
  • Try a test payment on your page using a card that’s not linked to your PayPal account. If there’s an error, it might point to a problem with your account. If it works, the issue may be on your supporter’s end.

Got questions? We’re always here to help. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

Trouble joining monthly memberships or recurring payments

When it comes to monthly memberships or recurring support, your supporters need

to use a bank card which allows ongoing payments. Prepaid cards or PayPal balances

won’t work for recurring payments like joining a membership tier.

Other reasons for payment errors

Here are some other reasons why your supporter’s payment might not have been successful:

🌍 Country restrictions

PayPal has country-specific payment rules. It’s a good idea to check if PayPal supports transactions between your countries. For example:

  • Some countries may not allow creators to receive payments on PayPal from supporters within their own country due to local restrictions.
  • Some countries might not allow commercial transactions due to international regulations.
  • Some PayPal accounts can send payments but cannot receive them.

Remember, you can also take payments by adding Stripe as a payment option.

💲Local payment processors

PayPal offers local payment options based on your supporter’s location and payment methods available, so different supporters see different payment options.

Options such as Venmo, iDeal, Giropay, and Przelewy24 could be offered depending on their country. Sometimes, supporter payments look successful but fail to complete. If a payment fails using one of these local payment methods, your supporter will usually get a refund within 5 working days.

Advise your supporter to try and pay directly by PayPal or a card payment if available. If you or your supporter needs more help, you can contact PayPal or the local payment processor directly.

If you have more questions, reach out to our support team and let us know what you’ve tried with any screenshots of the error, if you can.

FAQ:

Can I use PayPal in my country?

You can check PayPal’s list here or you can reach out to PayPal directly for clarification.

Remember, you may also be able to offer payments by adding Stripe as a payment method.

I am seeing the error code “requirements.disabled_reason’?

It might mean that there is an issue with your Stripe account. Log in to your Stripe account and resolve any issues. If you have any more questions, please raise a support ticket.

My supporter has been charged, but I have not received the funds. Will they get a refund?

If a payment fails, it’s usually a pending charge. Don’t worry PayPal or the payment processor should return it to your supporter in 3-5 working days.

How to issue a refund

Refunds on Ko-fi are handled directly by you, the creator. Ko-fi won’t issue refunds in your place.

You can refund a supporter by:

  1. Going to My Supporters or Payments & Orders

  2. Finding the payment

  3. Clicking the three dots ⋯ and choosing Refund

Refunds issued via Ko-fi are full amount only (not partial) and must be issued within 180 days.

PayPal refunds are usually instant.

Stripe refunds can take 5–10 business days.

Refunds remove access to shop downloads and supporter-only content for one-time tips. Membership content remains available until the billing period ends.

💡If a refund fails or you can’t find the transaction, check your payment provider (PayPal or Stripe) or contact us for help.

On Ko-fi, payments are made directly from supporters to you, which also means you’re in control of refunds. Let’s take a look at the different ways to refund a supporter based on how they supported you.

Refunding via My Supporters

The easiest way to initiate a refund is through My Supporters.

  • Go to My Supporters and locate the supporter you wish to refund by searching their name or email.
  • Click on the supporter’s name and find the payment you want to refund.
  • Click the “three dots” and select Refund. A pop-up will appear, allowing you to give a reason for the refund (optional).
  • Once you’ve refunded the payment, the funds will be removed from your PayPal/Stripe balance and returned to the supporter. The supporter will receive an email notifying them of the refund.
  • If they paid by PayPal, refunds are usually immediate and the supporter should see the funds returned to their PayPal balance.
  • For Stripe, it can take up to 5-10 business days for supporters to see their refund returned to their bank account.

Note

Check out the section here to find out what happens to exclusive content or previous purchases after a refund.

Refunding via Payments & Orders

You can also issue a refund directly from your Payments & Orders page.

  • Navigate to Payments & Orders and find the payment you want to refund. You can filter by payment type in the top left corner
  • Click the “three dots” next to the payment and choose Refund. Enter a refund reason if needed and complete the refund.
  • Once you’ve refunded the payment, the funds will be removed from your PayPal/Stripe balance and returned to the supporter. The supporter will receive an email notifying them of the refund.
  • If they paid by PayPal, refunds are usually immediate and the supporter should see the funds returned to their PayPal balance.
  • For Stripe, it can take up to 5-10 business days for supporters to see their refund returned to their bank account.

Canceling a subscription

To cancel a supporter’s subscription, head over to the “Members” tab of your Memberships page.

  • Search for the member using their email or name, then click on their profile.
  • Click the “three dots” and select Cancel Membership.

Supporters can cancel their subscriptions anytime using this guide. If a supporter can’t find their subscription, they can reach out to us here, and we’ll get it sorted for them.

When you cancel a Stripe subscription, access to content is revoked immediately. Canceling through PayPal revokes access to content at the end of the subscription period.

Refund failed

If a refund fails, first ensure your associated PayPal/Stripe account has enough funds to cover the refund. Remember, refunds can only be initiated within 180 days of the payment date.

If you continue having trouble refunding through Ko-fi, try initiating the refund directly through PayPal/Stripe.

Still having trouble issuing a refund? Reach out to us here and we’ll be happy to help.

Supporter access after refunds

Here’s a breakdown of what supporter-only content and previous purchases will be accessible after you refund a supporter:

  • For one-time tips, supporters lose immediate access to your content upon refund.
  • For recurring monthly or membership tier payments, access remains until the subscription period ends.
  • For digital shop orders, supporters won’t be able to download the item again.

FAQ

Can I issue a partial refund?

When you issue a refund through Ko-fi, you can only refund the full amount. To issue a partial refund, you’ll need to refund directly through the payment provider PayPal/Stripe.

How much time do I have to issue a refund?

Both PayPal and Stripe allow refunds for up to 180 days after the payment is completed.

Is there a fee for refunding a payment?

You can refer to Stripe’s Refunding Rules and PayPal’s Refunding Statement under “Commercial Transaction Refunds” for more information on this.

What usually happens is PayPal/Stripe keeps the transaction fee from the original payment.

How long does a refund take to process?

PayPal refunds are typically instant, while Stripe advises 5-10 business days.

Can Ko-fi refund a payment for me?

Since Ko-fi facilitates direct payments between you and your supporters, you must initiate refunds.

My Supporter has not received their refund?

If a refund was issued on the same day as the transaction, it might have been canceled instead, meaning the charge will disappear rather than be returned to them.

For supporters who were refunded through PayPal, you can forward them PayPal’s help article here.

I can’t find the transaction to refund

If you can’t find the transaction you need to refund, first check that the email/name of the supporter is correct in My Supporters or by checking with the supporter directly.

If the supporter didn’t create a Ko-fi account or leave a name when they supported you, then it might be easier to find the payment directly in your Stripe or PayPal account.

If you don’t see the payment on Ko-fi or in your payment account, it’s possible the payment did not go through. Failed payments are typically returned to supporters within 2-3 business days. If you still need help, feel free to raise a support ticket and we’ll be happy to lend a hand.

Still have questions? Feel free to reach out to us here. We’re here to help! 💌

My Ko-fi Gold subscription payment failed

If you received an email about your Ko-fi Gold subscription payment failing, don’t worry. We usually retry the payment a few times before anything happens to your Gold status. However, if the payment continues to fail, you might need to take action. This guide will help you.

Did you know you can get all of the benefits of Gold, without the monthly subscription? Learn about Contributor status here. You get all the benefits of Gold membership but only pay 5% service fee of each transaction, excluding any payment processor fees.

Common reasons for subscription failures

Subscriptions usually fail because of insufficient funds or expired cards:

Insufficient funds

If there aren’t enough cleared funds in your PayPal account or linked payment source, you’ll need to top up. Check your balance by logging into PayPal or your payment source.

Expired cards

If your card has expired, you’ll need to update your details.

If you paid with your credit or debit card:

  • Go to the Ko-fi menu: More > Account & Billing.
  • Under “Subscriptions”, find your Gold subscription.
  • Choose Update Card and enter your new card information.

If you paid via PayPal:

  • You’ll need to log-in to your PayPal account.
  • Find your subscription to Ko-fi Gold under “Pre-approved payments”.
  • Select Ko-fi and check your Gold subscription.
  • Choose your funding source.
  • Then save.

This will be active for future payments, though it may take a few days to process.

If your payment method is working

If you have enough funds and your card is working, we recommend canceling your Gold subscription via your Account Settings and then signing up again here. If you’re worried about keeping legacy pricing or discounts, reach out to our support team here.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

Offer Apple Pay to your supporters

Apple Pay allows supporters to make quick, convenient, and secure payments on their Apple devices like iPhone, iPad, and Mac. This means fewer clicks, easier payments, and more viewers converting to supporters!

You can let supporters use Apple Pay for one-time tips, Commissions, and Shop orders. To get started, all you need is a Stripe account connected to Ko-fi to begin accepting Apple Pay.

Setting up Apple Pay

To offer Apple Pay as a payment option, simply head to Payment Settings and connect to Stripe (you can create a Stripe account from here). Need help setting up Stripe? Check out our help article here.

Once you’ve connected a Stripe account, Apple Pay will automatically appear as a payment option to all supporters using Safari on an Apple device.

Note

Apple Pay is exclusively available for one-time tips, commissions, and shop orders. It cannot be used for monthly tips or memberships.

Do I need an Apple Pay account to accept payments?

In short, no! Since payments are processed through Stripe, all funds will appear in your Stripe account, just like any other payment through Stripe. Having an Apple Pay account is not needed to accept payments through Apple Pay.

If you don’t have a Stripe account, you can simply go to your Payment Settings and follow the steps to create your Stripe account.

More info on Stripe can be found here. You can also check if Stripe is available in your location here.

My supporters don’t see the option to use Apple Pay

Make sure you have a Stripe account connected to your Ko-fi profile to accept Apple Pay. The Apple Pay option pops up when supporters use Safari on an Apple device, such as a Mac, iPhone, or iPad. They will also need a saved payment card stored in their Apple Wallet.

Remember, Apple Pay is not currently available for monthly tips or memberships.

For more info on Stripe and Apple Pay, check out our help article here.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

Received payment to the wrong PayPal account

If you haven’t connected your PayPal account to Ko-fi and instead added your PayPal email for payments, you might have accidentally used the wrong email address. If you’ve received a payment to this PayPal account, don’t worry, we’re here to help!

There’s a way for supporters to cancel the payment to the incorrect account, which we’ll explain below. However, if that’s not an option, they can start a dispute process with PayPal.

Let’s guide you through the steps to sort out this situation:

1. Update your connected PayPal email:

If you’ve used the wrong PayPal email address on Ko-fi, don’t worry. The first step is to correct your PayPal email address in your payment settings. This ensures that future payments are directed to the correct PayPal account.

2. What if payments have already been made to the wrong account?

If payments have already been sent to the wrong account, here’s what you or your supporter can do next:

PayPal’s guide for supporters provides a helpful guide with detailed steps depending on the payment status. It might be beneficial to share this guide with your supporter.

  • Payment still unclaimed: your supporter can cancel it by following the guide above. Once they receive their payment back, they can pay you again, to the correct account.
  • Payment already completed: your supporter can email and request a refund using the email address the payment was mistakenly sent to.
    • No response from the recipient: In the unfortunate event of no response from the recipient, your supporter can dispute the payment following PayPal’s guide here.

Note

If a payment remains “unclaimed” for 30 days, it will be automatically refunded to the supporter. If this doesn’t happen, please reach out to PayPal support for further assistance.

If you still have any questions about PayPal or need help with anything else, feel free to raise a support ticket. We’re here to support you! 💌

Trouble finding Ko-fi payments

If you can see a payment on Ko-fi, but the funds aren’t in your connected payment account, here’s what to do.

1. Check your payment status

Visit Payments & Orders and review the payment status. If it’s marked as ‘Pending’ or anything other than completed, your payment is not likely to be cleared in your payment account. Learn more about payments with a pending status.

To check where a payment has gone, click “View Details.” At the bottom of the transaction, it should indicate whether it was paid to Stripe or PayPal. You can click the link to view it within your Stripe or PayPal account.

Additionally, PayPal offers a guide on payment statuses and how to manage them.

Payment does not appear in my payment history

If the payment doesn’t appear in your history, it’s possible the supporter’s payment may have failed. Refer to our help article to troubleshoot issues or direct them to our guide on failed payments.

Check there are no restrictions or warnings on your Stripe and PayPal accounts that could stop you from receiving payments. Log in to PayPal and/or Stripe on a desktop/laptop (not a phone or app) to check for notifications or status updates like ‘limited’ or ‘restricted.’ Resolve any issues directly within the payment platform.

2. Check the PayPal email address and Stripe account linked with your Ko-fi

On Ko-fi, supporters pay you directly into your own payment account (we don’t hold funds or process payments). If a payment appears in your Ko-fi account but not in your connected payment account:

  • Check your transaction history to confirm if the payment was sent to your PayPal or Stripe account.
  • If you can’t find the payment in the relevant payment account, check any previously used or connected accounts. If you’ve changed your payment details recently, payments may have been sent to an old account.
  • If you’ve followed these steps and still can’t find your payment, contact our support team here.

3. Check PayPal or Stripe’s service status

Confirm that PayPal and Stripe services are functioning normally by checking their status pages: PayPal status and Stripe status. If either service is anything other than ‘Operational,’ you may encounter payment-related issues or delays.

We’re here if you need us or if you have any suggestions. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

FAQ

How do I get paid with Ko-fi?

You get paid directly and instantly into your own PayPal or Stripe account, no waiting for payouts! Supporters can pay you via PayPal, cards, Apple Pay, Google Pay, Venmo, Cash App, and local payment options. Learn more here.

Do I need a PayPal or Stripe account before I open a Ko-fi page?

No, you don’t! Head to Payment tab and click “Connect” for PayPal or Stripe. You can create one or link your existing one in a few easy steps.

Do I need to do anything to enable these payment methods?

You’re ready to start receiving support as soon as you connect PayPal or Stripe. We automatically enable the most relevant payment methods for you based on your device, location and payment type.

If your supporters aren’t seeing a specific payment method, check supporter requirements in the tables above. If you’re still having trouble, reach out and we’ll take a look.

Can I request a new payment method not currently available?

We love hearing your ideas! Popular requests like direct bank transfer options and Payoneer are already on our radar. Keep sharing your favorites here, your feedback helps shape our roadmap.

Does Ko-fi take a fee?

Ko-fi is creator-friendly with a 0-5% service fee. There’s no monthly subscription, so we only earn when you do. Learn more about our pricing here.

What if I can’t get a PayPal or Stripe account?

Right now, we support PayPal and Stripe. However, we’re always exploring new options! If you’d like to see alternatives like Payoneer, Wise, GCash, Payeer, or direct bank transfers, let us know here. We’re constantly working to expand payment options to better support creators.

Why are my PayPal funds on hold?

Sometimes, creators mention that PayPal has placed their funds “on hold.” This means a portion of your funds is temporarily locked by PayPal. This can happen with new accounts or if there’s unexpected activity, such as receiving an unusually large payment or many payments in a short time. PayPal usually sends an email about these holds. In most cases, PayPal releases the funds within 21 days.

Reasons why your PayPal funds might be on hold, learn more from PayPal’s Help Article here. Some common reasons include:

  • Your PayPal account is new.
  • You’ve received a new payment after not receiving payments for some time.
  • You’ve received payments that are different from your usual transactions.

Even if your PayPal funds are on hold, you can still receive payments on Ko-fi. Most creators can expect to wait up to 21 days to have their funds released from PayPal, assuming there are no issues with the order.

How can I get my funds released sooner?

How can I get my funds released sooner? It’s common for PayPal to keep funds on hold for the full 21 days. Here are some things you can do to try and get your funds released earlier:

  • Check your PayPal account and respond to any messages or notifications from PayPal’s support team.
  • New PayPal accounts may need to verify details or complete account checks. Learn more here.
  • If you sold physical items from your Ko-fi shop, providing shipping/tracking information to PayPal can help in releasing your funds. Details here.
  • Payments from tips, memberships, digital shop items or commissions may be held for 21 days by PayPal. Contacting your supporter to confirm they received your content or service could help. More on this.
  • You can also consider reaching out to PayPal’s Support Team or learn more from their help centre.

Note

For a complete view of your PayPal account, it’s recommended to use the full PayPal Dashboard on a desktop, rather than the App or Mobile version.

Offer Stripe as a payment option

To gain access to future payments faster (while PayPal funds are “on hold”), you might want to consider adding Stripe as a payment option. With Stripe, supporters pay you directly with their card. Learn more about connecting to Stripe.

Once you have successfully linked your Stripe account to Ko-fi, you can decide to offer PayPal and Stripe or just Stripe as a payment option. When a supporter pays via Stripe, you’ll see the money in your Stripe dashboard and be able to schedule Payouts directly to your bank at any time.

Note

You should receive an automatic Payout every 7 days.

Will my supporter get access to my content whilst my funds are on hold?

Usually, when your funds are “on hold”, Supporters can access locked content, membership tier benefits, or digital shop items.

But, “on hold” is different from “pending”. “Pending” means PayPal is verifying the supporter’s payment. During this time, supporters usually will not have access to your content and benefits.

If a pending payment is made via an eCheck, which is a type of bank transfer, it could take around 5 business days to clear. Supporters only gain access to content once their payment has cleared. Further details here.

To learn more access PayPal Help Centre here.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

Why are shipping options showing up in PayPal for my Ko-fi payments?

When you receive payments through Ko‑fi, you might notice PayPal asking for shipping information or showing shipping options.

This is a normal PayPal security measure. It sometimes happens if your account is new, you’ve received an unusually large payment, or there’s a sudden increase in activity. It’s PayPal’s way of confirming that transactions are legitimate and helping protect both you and your supporters.

If you’re only receiving digital payments or tips, don’t worry, you don’t need to add anything here. It’s optional and won’t affect your payments if you leave the shipping details blank.

If a payment is ever on hold, check out our article Why are my PayPal funds on hold?.

We’re always happy to help or hear your feedback. Send us a message anytime. 💌

Why is my payment showing as pending?

Ko-fi operates on direct payments, meaning the money from your supporters goes straight into your PayPal or Stripe account. Sometimes, payments may be ‘pending’ due to security checks or errors.

How to check your payment status

Visit the Payments & Orders tab to see your payment status. Look for a grey status note at the bottom left of your transaction details.

A ‘pending’ status indicates your transaction isn’t complete yet. Click the “i” help dot for more info on your pending payment.

What to do if a payment is pending?

Here’s what to check if a payment to you hasn’t gone through:

  • eCheck payments Payments via eCheck take a while to process, as PayPal checks for safety. Payments usually clear in 5 working days. If it’s taking longer, there might be an issue with the supporter’s payment. Get more details on eCheck transactions.
  • Verify your PayPal account Unverified PayPal accounts can cause pending payments. Log into PayPal on a desktop and check for any alerts to fix this issue.
  • Payments in a new currency Payments in a new currency may need your approval on PayPal. Look for messages in your PayPal account. Accepting the first payment in a new currency might require manual approval. See PayPal’s currency management help article for more info.
  • Check your PayPal email A pending status can also mean an incorrect PayPal email linked to your Ko-fi account. Make sure the email in Payment Settings matches your PayPal login email. Learn how to fix a wrong PayPal address in our help article here.

Other PayPal payment statuses

Check out this PayPal guide to understand different statuses and any actions you may need to take.

Exclusive content access for pending payments

Supporters won’t be able to access exclusive content while their payment is pending. They’ll gain access once the payment is complete.

Helping supporters with pending payments

You can help supporters with payment issues, by directing them to our help article for assistance. If you need more help, contact our support team by submitting a ticket.

We’re here if you need us. Feel free to send any questions our way. 💌

Policies

Content guidance on physical items

This article provides some clarification relating to some of the most common questions we’ve been asked about selling or offering physical items on Ko-fi.

This is not a comprehensive, exhaustive guide - please get in touch if you are not sure if your physical items can be offered on Ko-fi. The broader guidelines and terms of use can be found here:

  • Prohibited Content and Uses of Ko-fi
  • Using the NSFW Tag
  • Terms of Service

A Few Reminders

  • These guidelines apply to all areas of the Ko-fi service, including where physical items are offered elsewhere as a result of a payment made on Ko-fi.
  • As Ko-fi facilitates payments using PayPal and Stripe, content being sold must adhere to PayPal’s acceptable use policy and Stripe’s prohibited businesses terms.
  • Please ensure any physical items not allowed on Ko-fi are fully removed from the service e.g. Shop listings must be fully deleted from the service. It is not acceptable to revert a listing to draft mode or temporarily close your shop.

Ko-fi Physical Items Guidance

Key:

✅ = OK

❌ = Not allowed on Ko-fi

✅ Breastplates - if these are being modelled, the content should remain non-sexual.

❌ Dirtied clothing items (e.g. used underwear, etc.).

✅ Items featuring fictional violence.

✅ Literary items featuring personal stories of abuse, mistreatment or neglect.

❌ Items that glorifies/supports violence, abuse, discrimination or hate.

✅ Cosplay and movie props made from non-metal materials (e.g. foam, fibreglass, latex, wood) that do not fire projectiles.

✅ Models and figures equipped with weapons.

❌ Real weapons of any kind, including parts and ammunition, are not permitted. Note: We distinguish between weapons and tools intended for practical or culinary use (e.g., handcrafted general-purpose knives, kitchen knives, etc.). Due to sensitivities around this content, Ko-fi pages featuring such items will be unpublished until you can provide verifiable evidence that you are a licensed seller in your country or region - this requirement is for general business licensing and is not specific to knives or other weapons unless local laws require separate licensing for those items.

❌ In the case of replicas, highly realistic firearms.

❌ Any toy/replica weapons that fire projectiles e.g. BB guns, air rifles, etc.

Giveaways/raffles/lotteries:

❌ Pay-to-enter giveaways/raffles/lotteries.

❌ No mystery items at all.

❌ Giveaways or raffle/lottery entries.

Food:

⚠️ Important: creators are responsible for ensuring their products meet the legal requirements for selling food.

✅ Food items should only be made available in countries/territories for which they are compliant.

Creators planning on selling food may first be required to obtain a license/register a food business. We strongly advise researching the regulations for your home country.

For countries that are part of the European Union, there are other rules that you may have to oblige, such as food labelling rules. You can access a more comprehensive database of EU food rules here.

For regulations in the United States of America, visit fda.gov.
