Sending physical shop items to the US: a guide to new customs & tariff rules (August 2025)

Changes to US customs rules mean it’s more important than ever to plan ahead before shipping physical items to US supporters. This guide explains what’s changed, how it affects Ko-fi creators, and how you can help your buyers have a smooth experience.

Note

This guide is for creators and doesn’t replace official advice from your courier, postal service, or government websites. Customs requirements can change, so always double-check with your shipping provider before sending parcels overseas.

What’s new?

From August 29, 2025, all parcels sent to the United States from outside the US will face import tariffs and will need to clear customs. There is no longer a duty-free amount for low-value goods. Most US buyers will now need to pay customs duty (and sometimes a handling fee) before they receive their items. Learn more here.

Who is affected?

If you use Shop, Commissions, or Membership tiers to send physical items (like stickers, pins, posters, and more) to US buyers, this guide is for you. Digital items and rewards aren’t affected by these changes.

What does this mean for Ko-fi creators?

  • US buyers now pay customs duty on most physical items sent from outside the US. Buyers are usually asked to pay fees when their parcel arrives.
  • Creators must include full customs documents with every US order:
    • A commercial invoice listing for each item, its value, country of origin, and HS/HTS code. Most couriers will guide you through this step when you ship.
    • The buyer’s phone number and email. US customs won’t deliver without these. Ko-fi collects these for Shop, Commissions, and Membership rewards (see guide below).
  • Make it clear to US buyers. Add a note to your shop, commissions, and membership tiers so buyers know about possible fees and the need for contact details.
  • Be ready for returns and delays. Parcels with missing paperwork, or where buyers refuse to pay duty, may be delayed or sent back to you. Updating your terms to specify who is responsible for customs fees can help avoid confusion if there are any issues. (see guide below).

Checklist for sending physical items to the US

  • Update your shop item listings, commission descriptions, membership tier descriptions and your terms to warn buyers about possible customs charges and delays.
  • Always collect buyers’ phone numbers and emails for customs forms (see guide below).
  • Fill out commercial invoices for all US parcels. Your courier or postal service will help with this.
  • Double check product details, use the right HS/HTS codes and list each item’s value and country of origin. Learn more here.

Note

If you prefer not to ship to the US, update your listings, descriptions, and shop terms to let buyers know.

Tip

Couriers and postal services are your best resource! Customs paperwork and tariffs can feel overwhelming, but your courier or local post office will have the latest info and forms for shipping to the US. Always check their website or ask directly to make sure you’re following the most up-to-date rules.

Collecting phone numbers and emails from buyers

The good news is, Ko-fi always collects your buyer’s email address when a payment is made.

Collecting phone numbers for physical items works a little differently depending on the feature:

Commissions:

  • If you’re sending a physical commission, Ko-fi automatically collects the buyer’s shipping address and telephone number.
  • Find these details by going to Payments and Orders, selecting your order, and clicking “View details.” Learn more here.

Membership tiers:

  • When setting up a membership tier, select “Request Supporter’s Address” to collect a shipping address and phone number when a member joins.
  • If you start collecting details later, you can add a shipping address or your member can add their details at anytime.

Shop:

  • To collect phone numbers for physical shop orders, turn on “Require supporter telephone number for physical shipping” in your shop settings.
  • If you prefer not to require this for all physical orders, you can leave instructions for buyers to share their number via DM instead. Just note, this may result in fewer buyers providing their phone number.

Still have questions? Contact us anytime, we’re always here to help. 💌

FAQ

Do I need to work out US customs duty for the buyer?

Most likely you won’t, but best to check with your courier. US customs will calculate any duty and ask the buyer to pay when their parcel arrives. Unless you’re using an advanced courier with “DDP” (Delivered Duty Paid) services, buyers pay on delivery.

What happens if customs duty isn’t paid?

Parcels may be held, delayed, or returned to you. There can also be extra costs for returns.

Does Ko-fi supply customs forms or invoices?

No, these will need to be provided by you or your courier/postal service.

Are digital shop content affected?

No, this only affects physical shop items sent to US addresses.

Can I exclude the United States from my shipping destinations?

Currently, the best way is to mention this in your item descriptions or shop terms.

Using the NSFW tag

On Ko-fi you can tag your page as Not Safe For Work (NSFW). NSFW is an advisory label used for content or activity suitable only to be accessed/viewed by an 18+ audience.

However, not all NSFW content is allowed.

The NSFW tag does not provide an option for pornography or sexually explicit nudity; harmful or hateful conduct; or other entirely prohibited activities and forms of content.

In this article, which is an extension of our main content guidelines, we look at what kind of content is allowed with the NSFW tag and what kind of content is not allowed on Ko-fi at all.

The basics

  • Your page must be compliant with Ko-fi’s content guidelines regardless of using the NSFW tag.
  • If any part of your page contains any content that could be deemed by some audiences as offensive or of a mature nature (examples below), please tag your page as NSFW. This must be applied even where the content is locked (i.e. membership tiers and supporters-only content).
  • If any part of your page links to any content that could be deemed by some audiences as offensive or of a mature nature (examples below), please also tag your page as NSFW.

NSFW content vs. prohibited content

This non-exhaustive list of examples aims to help creators understand how Ko-fi defines NSFW and the appropriate use of the NSFW tag.

Key:

✅ = OK with NSFW tag

❌ = Not allowed on Ko-fi (regardless of NSFW tag)

Adult Content

This section expands on some of the more nuanced points in our main content guidelines, such as sexualised nudity; select uses of covered and censored sexualised nudity; high-risk fetish and paraphilia content; and other examples of what’s okay with the NSFW tag versus what’s not allowed on Ko-fi at all.

Content warning: some of the examples provided are explicit in their detail.

Nudity and partial nudity

✅ Non-sexualised nudity, including partial, in a cultural or lifestyle context (e.g. naturism; tribal nudity; tattoo work; etc.) - this applies to real and fictional content.

✅ Non-sexualised nudity, including partial, in an educational and/or medical context (e.g. breastfeeding; personal journeys relating to gender-affirming surgery; breast cancer; sex education; historic photographs; etc.) - this applies to real and fictional content.

✅ Non-sexualised nudity, including partial, in an artistic context (e.g. professional modeling photoshoots; stock image modeling; body painting with evident artistic intent; statues; illustrations; paintings (think of nudity as it is typically portrayed in classical or renaissance paintings); mods in which nudity is displayed but is not sexualised and is not the purpose of the mod e.g. a tattoo mod may have to show nudity; etc. - this applies to real and fictional content.

✅ Lightly sensual nudity, including partial. By this, we mean illustrations/sketches/digital art featuring: breast nudity that is paired with other suggestive but non-explicit visuals such as protruding tongue; flushed skin; placement of hands on breasts; embracing; etc. - this applies to fictional content.

✅ Lightly sensual content featuring clothed side or underboob - this applies to real and fictional content.

✅ Partial display of buttocks and buttocks cleft above trousers/pants - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ Sexualised nudity, including partial, such as when paired with kink or fetish elements; pornographic content (sex acts and explicit emulation of sex acts); an adult content creator context; bodily fluids including lookalike substances; exhibitionist poses (splayed legs, on-all-fours, flashing, etc.); squeezing, groping or pulling at breasts and/or genitalia; and/or when offered as a ‘product’ for sale - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ Exhibitionist poses (splayed legs, on-all-fours, etc.) in which underwear/thong g-string is thin enough to reveal the outer labia or skin which may be mistaken for the outer labia; testicles; or the anal opening/periphery - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ Exhibitionist poses or sexualised framing (e.g. close-up shots) of breasts partially displaying the areolae - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ Tip drive content that promises forthcoming sexualised nudity/partial nudity - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ AI-generated content that uses a known person’s likeness (e.g. a celebrity) and features nudity, including partial, regardless of whether sexual or non-sexual - this applies to fictional content.

Covered and censored nudity

✅Non-sexualised covered or censored nudity in an artistic or lifestyle context (e.g. professional photoshoots, including in the bath; naturism; stock image modeling; the censoring of nudity as a social statement; illustrations; paintings; mods; etc.) - this applies to real and fictional content.

✅ Lightly sensual content in which nudity is substantially covered or censored (e.g. nudity covered by towels; plushies; pillows; cushions; and, for breasts, hair that is sufficiently long and thick enough to provide full coverage) - this applies to real content.

✅ Lightly sensual content in which nudity is censored or covered either substantially or minimally - this applies to fictional content (note: for photorealistic AI content, this should be substantially covered/censored).

✅ Non-sexualised clothed content in an artistic, commercial, or sporting context featuring nipple imprints or the outline of genitalia (e.g. modeling underwear or related products; athletes in tight clothing; paintings; illustrations; mods; etc.) - this applies to real and fictional content.

✅ Lightly sensual content featuring either the bulge of the scrotum or in which the outline of the genitalia is ambiguous (e.g. due to artistic elements such as shading, linework, etc.) - this applies to fictional content.

❌ Pornographic content (sex acts and explicit emulation of sex acts) regardless of the extent to which it has been covered/censored - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ Sexualised content (exhibitionist poses such as splayed legs, on-all-fours; close-up shots of breasts or genitalia; inclusion of kink or fetish elements; etc.) in which nudity is covered/censored very minimally (e.g. hands; fingers; forearm; tape; emoji; dots; tassels; thoroughly soaked clothing; etc.) - this applies to real content and photorealistic AI content

❌ Nude content that is covered/censored as a form of paywall (i.e. using a censored/covered preview to solicit tips or purchase of the uncensored version) - this applies to real and fictional content.

Suggestive content

✅ Suggestive poses (clothed) with emphasis on buttocks and/or crotch or cleavage - this applies to real and fictional content.

✅ Implied nudity (i.e. nudity that may or may not be present - whereas we class covered/censored nudity as an obvious obstruction of nudity) - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ Content featuring children in suggestive clothing and/or poses - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ Lightly sensual content that appears to depict or uses lookalike substances for explicit bodily fluids (e.g. semen) - this applies to real and fictional content.

Fetishes and paraphilia

✅ Non-explicit content that poses no harm to participants (e.g. wearing latex, etc.) or viewers - this applies to real and fictional content.

✅ Non-explicit content that is harmless and rooted entirely in fantasy (e.g. drawing balloon-like or otherwise large or small characters; characters with lots of muscles; etc.) - this applies to fictional content.

✅ Cultural and/or artistic depictions of content that are sometimes associated with fetish content e.g. shibari - note: we do not allow sexual bondage scenarios - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ Content that poses harm to participants (e.g. addiction; weight gain via excessive eating or force-feeding; fainting; asphyxiation; hyperventilation; choking; biting; torture; knife play; self-harm; abuse, including animal abuse or death; etc.) or viewers - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ Content, including adult roleplay, that sexualises infants/children - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ Content focused on or resulting in bodily fluids (e.g. feces, urine, semen, vomit, breast discharge, or lookalike substances) - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ Content focusing on or containing elements of pornography (including emulation of sex acts), sexualised nudity, and/or explicit BDSM themes - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ Foot fetish content or themes - this applies to real and fictional content.

Sexual acts

✅ Tame images that imply sexual activity (e.g. interlocking fingers; under the bed sheets; etc.) - this applies to real and fictional content.

✅ Significantly cropped content in which possible sexual activity is not apparent, explicit, or distasteful (note: we may take action if our systems flag the original pre-cropped image or if accompanying text describes the cropped activity and we consider this to be explicit ) - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ Depictions of implied sexual activity that are nonetheless relatively explicit (e.g. implied oral sex, penetration, masturbation, rape, or consensual non-consent; use of fluids; etc.) - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ Content that has been cropped but is still identifiable as pornography (this may be due to minimal cropping, a text description accompanying the image, or our systems having flagged the original pre-cropped image) - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ Content that implies sexual activity and that is being used to solicit the purchase of or tips in exchange for the full explicit version - this applies to real and fictional content.

❌ Sale/provision of AI models trained to engage in explicit activities or training instructions for this purpose - this applies to fictional content.

❌ Development or streaming of pornographic/explicit erotic/sex simulator games - this applies to real and fictional content.

Non-visual content

✅ Blogs/articles/posts discussing sexual health/advice/education/lifestyles

✅ Discussion pieces, analyses, or reviews of other media (e.g. films, TV series, books, etc.) that reference sexual and/or explicit themes

✅ Adult-themed fiction containing conventional/mainstream sexual content and themes

✅ Non-explicit audio content (e.g. relating to voice acting, roleplay, harmless stimulation such as whispering)

❌ Fiction eroticizing and/or glorifying taboo sexual themes (e.g. underage, consensual non-consent, bestiality, etc.)

❌ ASMR and voice acting relating to pornographic scenarios or prohibited fetish areas

❌ NSFW AI chatbots, e.g. chatbots that enable the user to engage in NSFW roleplay.

Violence, wounds and injuries

Content warning: some of the examples provided are explicit in their detail.

Visual content

✅ Moderate fictional violence and gore

✅ Results of medical operations or accidental wounds

✅ Results of violence or injury sustained as a victim of an attack or accident

✅ SFX makeup effects

✅ Footage/images of violence or abuse shown to raise awareness or to educate (e.g. news journalism, historical footage, documentaries, animal charity content)

❌ Excessively graphic fictional depictions of violence/gore/abuse such as torture

❌ Sexual violence such as rape, whether real or fictional

❌ Real footage/images of violent acts, torture, or death provided as entertainment and/or for financial gain. Includes seemingly real content such as snuff footage content

❌ Real torture footage/images regardless of intent or context

❌ Graphic and/or instructional depictions of self-harm or suicide

❌ Illegal content (e.g. violence associated with child sexual abuse material; hateful content that glorifies, promotes, or incites violence and prejudice toward an individual or group; committing and circulating an act of assault or murder; terrorist propaganda)

❌ Content related to streaming all games forbidden by major streaming platforms (e.g. Twitch)

Non-visual content

✅ Fictional written or audio violence and gore (e.g. a horror or war book)

✅ Discussion pieces, analyses, or reviews of other media (e.g. films, TV series, books, etc.) that reference violent themes

✅ Graphic personal stories of abuse, mistreatment, or neglect

❌ Illegal content (e.g. violence associated with child sexual abuse material; hateful content that glorifies, promotes, or incites violence and prejudice toward an individual or group; committing and circulating an act of assault or murder; terrorist propaganda)

❌ Audio re-enactments or gratuitous fictional descriptions of sexual violence or torture

❌ Detailed written or audio accounts or instructions for suicide, self-harm

Language

Profanity

✅ Frequent use of strong profanity (e.g. ct, fk) in content means that your page must be tagged as NSFW.

For lighter profanity, regardless of frequency, (e.g. sh*t), your page doesn’t need to be tagged.

Hate speech

❌ Hate speech, harassment, intimidation, or abuse targeting any individual, group, or institution is prohibited.

See our main content guidelines for our full hate speech policy.

Harmful misinformation

❌ Harmful misinformation, such as denial of the Holocaust and other genocidal events, is prohibited.

See our main content guidelines for more details and examples.

Drugs and Alcohol

Drugs

✅ Content that glorifies or promotes cigarettes, vapes or marijuana

✅ Real-life content that features the use of cigarettes, vapes or marijuana

✅ Fictional content (e.g. artwork, a movie scene) depicting legal or illegal recreational drugs or prescription drug misuse

✅ Discussion about the use of either legal or illegal recreational drugs or prescription misuse

❌ Content that instructs people on how to obtain or administer drugs and psychoactive substances

❌ Content that glorifies, promotes, or instructs on illegal recreational drug use or prescription drug misuse

❌ Real-life content that features the use of illegal recreational drugs or prescription drug misuse

❌ Real-life content that is either explicit or in which the person is incapacitated or in danger/at risk of harm as a result of drugs

Alcohol

✅ Content that glorifies, promotes, discusses, or depicts alcohol/alcohol use

❌ Promotion of dangerous drinking games/challenges

❌ Real-life content that is either explicit or in which the person is incapacitated or in danger/at risk of harm as a result of intoxication

If anything still isn’t clear or you have a question about something that we haven’t covered, please get in touch.

How to tag your page as NSFW

Log in to your Ko-fi account, go to Settings, and scroll down to the ‘What do you do?’ field where you can add ‘NSFW’ as a tag.

What happens when I apply the NSFW Tag?

When you tag your page as NSFW, a pop-up notification will warn visitors to your page that the content on your page may not be safe for work and that they must be 18 or older to view the page. Visitors will need to click ‘Agree and continue’ before they can view your page.

What happens if I don’t apply the NSFW Tag?

As minors might encounter your page, it’s important to try and remember to do this if applicable to you. If your page is NSFW and you have not applied the NSFW tag, we may proactively apply this to your page or ask you to apply it.

Although we don’t unpublish pages solely because they haven’t been tagged as NSFW, we may unpublish such untagged pages that also contain or in some cases are connected to outright prohibited content/activity.

Once the tag has been applied, you should continue to use it as long as NSFW content is on or linked to your Ko-fi page.

What does “Page Unpublished” mean and what should I do?

An unpublished Ko-fi page means that the page has been suspended - usually temporarily. Unpublished pages can still be accessed/updated by you but will not be viewable to the public nor will it be possible to send Direct Messages (DMs) to other users while the page remains unpublished.

Why Do Pages Get Unpublished?

Ko-fi pages are usually unpublished if they violate our guidelines and/or terms.

There are some other reasons why a page might alternatively be unpublished, which are explained in our guidelines here.

Ko-fi pages…

  • Can be reported by anyone and we’ll investigate
  • May be flagged by our automated screening systems
  • Are reviewed by our Trust & Safety team after being flagged or reported

Why Can Links to/From Other Places Result in My Page Being Unpublished?

Because people earn money through Ko-fi, we need to consider what connection if any, their content/activity on external pages, profiles or sites has to their Ko-fi page.

However, even if there is no financial connection, a Ko-fi page may be affected if it has linked to or from external pages/profiles/sites of a predominantly illegal, hateful, sexually explicit, or otherwise violative nature that contravenes our guidelines. In circumstances where a Ko-fi page specifically links from Ko-fi to illegal content/activity, the page will be unpublished.

We recommend reading in more detail about our linking rules here.

Requesting Republishing

If your page is unpublished, you’ll receive an email from us; we’ll send this to the email address that is connected to your Ko-fi page. As well as explaining why your page has been unpublished, this email contains a direct link through which you can submit an appeal. You can only use this link once.

If you have not received this email, your link is broken or are having trouble accessing your email account, you can contact us here.

Please only use this link if you have not received an email, are experiencing access trouble, or have been notified that your original appeal has been closed due to inactivity; submitting duplicate appeals may delay your Ko-fi page being reviewed.

We aim to get back to you within 7 days although we often respond sooner.

If you have an appeal open and we have not heard from you within 30 days of sending you a response, the appeal case will be closed. However, you can re-appeal at your convenience.

Do Pages Always Get Republished?

Ko-fi pages are republished in most cases, but not always.

Pages that are not eligible for republishing will have been permanently unpublished or banned.

Reasons for this include:

  • Reaching three out of three strikes on your Ko-fi page(s), resulting in permanent suspension - this means that any remaining or newly created Ko-fi pages will also be unpublished or banned as this will count as a permanent suspension. Read more about our strike system
  • Permanent suspension on a previous page for a reason other than strikes - this is likelier to be connected to the severity of an action or behaviour and/or the overall unsuitability of a service or content as per our guidelines

Did this article cover everything you were looking for? This link at the end is only for feedback on the article itself and cannot be used to appeal your unpublished status. Leave feedback here.

Your guide to GPSR compliance for selling in the EU

Starting December 13, 2024, the General Product Safety Regulation (GPSR) introduced new rules to help ensure products sold to EU consumers are safe and traceable. These updates are designed to make things clearer and safer for everyone, from creators and supporters alike.

If you sell physical or digital items through Ko-fi, don’t worry, we’re here to help you navigate it all. Let’s break it down step by step so you can keep creating and sharing with confidence.

Do I need to comply?

If you’re selling to the EU, the answer is yes.

As a Ko-fi creator, you’re the merchant responsible for ensuring your products (physical or digital) meet GPSR requirements. This applies to all items listed after December 13, 2024, whether you sell one or a hundred. Think of it as a way to build trust with your supporters while keeping your shop running smoothly.

What are the conditions for selling in the EU?

Complying with GPSR is simpler than it sounds. Here’s what you need to know:

  • Keep your products safe: Ensure your items are safe to use as intended or in any way they’re reasonably expected to be used.
  • Add compliance information: Include information like CE markings (if required) in your product listings. Learn more about CE markings here.
  • Share important info with orders: Provide clear instructions, safety warnings, product identifiers, and your contact details. For digital products, mention any potential risks, like software compatibility.
  • Save your records: Keep documentation about your products (like safety checks or risk assessments) for 10 years. Adding batch codes or unique identifiers can help with tracking or recalls.
  • Be ready to help if asked: If EU authorities request information about your products, you’ll need to provide it promptly.
  • Appoint an EU authorized representative: If you’re outside the EU, the official recommendation is to appoint someone in the EU to act as your representative. They’d be the go-to person for any questions about the technical or safety side of your product. If you’re in the EU, you’ll be the contact.

You can find the full details here. These steps are all about keeping your products safe and easy to trace if questions or concerns come up.

How can you comply on Ko-fi?

Ko-fi makes GPSR compliance simple! Here’s how to get started:

  • Ensure product safety
  • Before listing a product, make sure it’s safe for your supporters. Check for anything that could cause harm, like sharp edges, unstable parts, choking hazards, or fragile materials. For digital products, make sure they’re virus-free and compatible with common devices.
  • Add compliance info to your listings
  • Include key details in your product descriptions, such as CE markings (if needed) or other safety information. This helps your supporters feel confident in their purchase. Learn how to update listings here.
  • Include safety details with every order
  • Add any instructions, warnings, or contact information to your physical product packaging. For digital products, include these details in the download or confirmation email.
  • Keep records for 10 years
  • Use Ko-fi’s CSV download tool to save your sales records easily. It’s a handy way to stay organized and meet this requirement without any extra hassle. Learn more here.
  • Cooperate with authorities
  • If an EU official asks for information about your products, you’ll need to provide it. Staying open and cooperative keeps things running smoothly.
  • Appoint an EU Authorized Representative
    • If you’re in the EU, that’s you!
    • Outside the EU? Nominate a trusted representative and add their contact info to your product listings.

Can I stop selling to the EU?

Yes, you can adjust your shop settings if you’d prefer not to sell to EU customers.

  • For physical products, you can limit shipping destinations. This ensures only your chosen regions can place orders.
  • Digital products don’t currently have regional restrictions on Ko-fi, so they’ll remain available worldwide.

Ko-fi’s commitment to helping you

We understand that keeping up with regulations can feel like a lot, but you’re not alone. Ko-fi is here to provide practical tips and keep you updated as rules change, so you can focus on doing what you love.

If you have any questions contact us anytime, we’d love to hear from you! 💌

FAQs

Does GPSR apply even if I sell just one item to the EU?

Yes, it applies to all sales, big or small.

Does this apply to digital items too?

Yes, digital products like e-books, videos, or software are included.

What if I give away products for free?

Even freebies, like samples, gifts, or promotional items, must meet safety requirements like providing contact details and safety information.

Can I limit sales to certain countries?

For physical products, yes, - you can restrict shipping destinations. For digital items, regional restrictions aren’t available yet.

Do I need to update older listings?

Yes, if you want to keep selling them to EU customers. All products sold after December 13, 2024, must meet the new requirements.

Please make sure anything you create with these images follows Ko-fi’s content policy. As long as your design is in line with our guidelines, you’re free to get creative!

You can find all the official assets and images on the widgets page here.

If you’re ever unsure or have questions, send any questions our way 💌!

How to use Ko-fi with Github

Have you thought about giving your GitHub visitors a way to support your work? Start your free Ko-fi page to earn an income and connect with your GitHub community.

Make Ko-fi your “Sponsor” funding link in your repository

GitHub lets you add your Ko-fi page as a funding link so visitors to your repository can support your work! Here’s how:

  • On GitHub.com, head over to the main page of your repository.
  • Under your repository name, click Settings.
  • On the “General” settings page, in the “Features” section, select Sponsorships.
  • In the “Sponsorships” box, click Set up sponsor button or Override funding links.
  • In the file editor, follow the instructions in the FUNDING.yml file to add your Ko-fi page in the following format.
  • Click Commit changes or Propose changes.

That’s it, anyone seeing your repository will be invited to support your project on your Ko-fi page.

SacchanVRC Lotte The Narcolept

Signpost your Ko-fi page

Make it easy for people to find out how to support your work.

  • Add your Ko-fi link to your README: Your project’s README file is the first thing people see. Embed your Ko-fi link prominently, perhaps with a badge or a simple call to action like “Support this project on Ko-fi!”
  • Include Ko-fi in your repository descriptions: A quick mention of Ko-fi support in your repo descriptions can direct potential supporters to take a look at your Ko-fi.
  • Mention Ko-fi in your GitHub Pages: If you use GitHub Pages to showcase your projects, include a Ko-fi tip button or link on your site.

How developers earn more on Ko-fi

Thousands of developers are using Ko-fi to receive one off and monthly tips to help support their projects. There are loads of other ways to make a meaningful income from your work.

  • Acknowledge supporters in your project: Consider adding a Supporters section in your README or documentation, listing everyone who supports your project through Ko-fi.
  • Give early access to supporters: Let your Ko-fi supporters get early access to new features or versions of your software.
  • Create a membership: Connect with your most generous fans through Memberships. Give supporters perks like Discord roles, shout outs, steering group membership, early access etc.
  • Sell exclusive items or merch: Open your Ko-fi Shop to sell add-ons or merch to support your project.
  • Offer your services as requests: If you’re a developer, you have in-demand skills. Open up Ko-fi Commission requests to let people request a code review or request your help with a custom project.

Connect with your community

Talk with your supporters about your progress, thank them and get their input to improve your projects.

  • Update your community on your Ko-fi goals: Use the GitHub Discussions feature to talk about your Ko-fi goals and celebrate milestones.
  • Celebrate achievements and thank supporters: Whenever you hit a major milestone with your project, share an update thanking your Ko-fi supporters for helping to make it possible.
  • Engage with your supporters through Issues and Pull Requests: Acknowledge those who contribute, not just with code, but also those who support your work through Ko-fi. A thank-you can go a long way.

With Ko-fi, every commit, every line of code, and every project update can become an opportunity to build a community of supporters and drive your projects forward. Let’s code, connect, and create together!

How to use Ko-fi with Medium

Desktop Mobile

  • On your homepage, click on your profile picture

  • Click Settings from the menu

  • In the Publishing tab, click Manage tipping on your stories

  • Enter your Ko-fi link e.g. ko-fi.com/supportkofi

  • Click Save changes

  • In the app, tap your profile picture in the nav bar

  • Tap Settings then go to Account and payment

  • In the Publishing tab, click Manage tipping on your stories

  • Enter your Ko-fi link e.g. ko-fi.com/supportkofi

  • Click Save changes

How to use Ko-fi with Substack

If you’re looking to increase your income from your Substack, Ko-fi can really help! For readers who aren’t quite ready to subscribe monthly, Ko-fi is a great low-pressure way to support you. Here are some examples and how-to’s to get you started.

Add your Ko-fi link to your homepage

Substack lets you display links on your publication homepage so readers can easily find you on other websites.

To add your Ko-fi link to your Substack homepage, simply go to:

Settings > Website, then click ‘Edit’ next to ‘Homepage links’. Tap ‘Add Link’ and fill in the details:

  • Title: Ko-fi
  • Subtitle: [Your desired subtitle]
  • Group: Support
  • Link: [Your Ko-fi page link]

Then click ‘Add Link’. That’s it!

Add a button to your articles

Add a custom button or download one of our Ko-fi buttons to include in your posts! At the end of every post, readers will see a call to action to support you.

The Notts Edit

Custom button

  • When you’re writing a post, click Buttons.
  • Choose Custom.
  • Add your chosen text and your Ko-fi link e.g. ko-fi.com/supportkofi.
  • Click Ok.

Ko-fi button

  • Download a Ko-fi button image from our brand assets.
  • Add the button as an image to your post.
  • Highlight the image then click “cmd + k” (mac) or “ctrl + k” (PC) to add a link to the image.
  • Paste in your Ko-fi link so the image links to your page.

Or simply add a text link to the bottom of your articles prompting your readers to check out your Ko-fi page!

How to use Ko-fi with Twitch

Keep more of your earnings by asking your Twitch viewers to support you on Ko-fi (just 0-5% service fees). There’s loads of cool ways to engage with your supporters during your streams. Let’s get started!

Connect to Twitch

Head over to Stream Alerts and connect to Twitch. It only takes a few seconds and does so much to promote your Ko-fi on autopilot. By connecting you’ll be able to:

  • Receive payment alerts in chat: When you receive a payment a message will show in your chat. It’s a nice easy way to acknowledge your supporter and encourage more tips.
  • Show goal progress in live chat: An emoji based goal progress bar will appear in chat after you receive a payment.
  • Let the chatbot respond to commands like !kofi: Chatbot will share your links when someone uses the commands !kofi, !ko-fi, !kofishop, !koficommissions, !kofitiers. Top tip, put your commands in your stream description.
  • Send timed messages promoting your Ko-fi: Choose an interval to share your page in chat from every 15 minutes to 90 mins.

“I have Ko-fi alerts on and I have a random wheel that I spin when people donate. Fun things can happen and the supporter gets cool perks and prizes!”

ko-fi.com/wordigirl

Leafbear Studio Ashleigh Emilia Peter Webby

Put Ko-fi in your stream title and add a panel

Add your Ko-fi command or a call to action like ‘support me on Ko-fi’ to your stream titles. Update your Twitch bio to make your Ko-fi page easy to find, especially when you’re not online.

  • Mention Ko-fi in your stream title: Include a short call-to-action, inviting viewers to check out your Ko-fi page and include !kofi to signpost the chat command for your page.
  • Update your About section: Add your Ko-fi link to your ‘About’ section and update your bio to explain how Ko-fi supports your streaming and creative work.
  • Add your Ko-fi link to your Twitch panels: Create a dedicated Ko-fi panel under your stream. This makes it super easy for viewers to support you with just a click. Need some Ko-fi visuals? Check out these brand assets.

Set up Stream Alerts

Make your streams more interactive by setting up Stream Alerts for new tips, memberships, or sales. This immediate acknowledgement can encourage more viewers to support you.

Read this guide to learn how to set up stream alerts.

  • Setup a custom Stream Alert overlay: A simple overlay of your Ko-fi link with a custom call to action, color, sound and message. You can even set it to read out messages live.
  • Customize alerts with GIFs: Pick a fun GIF to celebrate getting support and make your supporter feel appreciated.
  • Add a goal overlay: Display your goal live on stream. It’s a visual motivator for your viewers to help you hit those milestones.

“I’ve found having my Ko-fi link on screen is very helpful! A constant reminder to all where to support me. I also have one of the Ko-fi gifs next to it for extra eye grabbiness!”

ko-fi.com/imagicnary_fae

Make your Ko-fi supporters feel like VIP’s

  • Shout out Ko-fi supporters during streams: Live acknowledgements create a personal connection and encourage others to support as well.
  • Offer Twitch-exclusive rewards for Ko-fi supporters: This could be anything from exclusive game nights to Q&A sessions, adding value to supporting you on Ko-fi.
  • Use stream descriptions to detail Ko-fi perks: Make sure your viewers know what they gain by supporting you on Ko-fi, whether it’s exclusive content, behind-the-scenes access, or other perks.
  • Highlight Ko-fi support in Twitch clips: Whenever you receive support we’ll take a short 30 second clip. Find your clips in Payments & Orders (you’ll notice a Twitch icon if there’s a clip). Share your clips to thank your supporters and share your reactions.

“The number one tip for anyone streaming - have fun! If you’re not enjoying the experience the level of entertainment you’re presenting to others will be severely diminished.”

ko-fi.com/midnighterknight

We can’t wait to see you earning more on Twitch with Ko-fi. If you need any help setting up alerts feel free to reach out to us. Happy streaming!

Every video you post, every comment you reply to, and every live session you host brings you closer to your community. And with Ko-fi, you give them a meaningful way to support all the hard work and love you put into your content. Let’s turn those views into support on Ko-fi!

Can I tweak the Ko-fi logo a bit so it fits the look of my page or website better?

We would prefer you to keep the logo as consistent as possible so people know it’s Ko-fi. However, ensure the logo isn’t stretched, distorted, or changed too much from the original design, and that it aligns with our content guidelines. 💌

Using Ko-fi with Tumblr

Hey, Tumblr creators! If you’re using Tumblr to share your art, writing, and ideas, setting up a Ko-fi page can help you turn those reblogs and likes into meaningful support on Ko-fi.

Graciedoesart fashionrunways voidcatgalaxy

Make sure your followers know you’re on Ko-fi

  • Link to Ko-fi in your bio: Start by adding your Ko-fi link to your Tumblr bio. It’s a simple, effective way to direct your followers to where they can support you.
  • Include Ko-fi links in your posts: Whether you’re sharing artwork, stories, or personal posts, include a call-to-action with your Ko-fi link. A gentle reminder at the end of your posts can encourage your followers to show their support.
  • Customize your theme to include Ko-fi: Many Tumblr themes allow you to add custom links or buttons. Add a Ko-fi button or link prominently on your page to catch the eye of your visitors. Grab a button from here or free gifs from here.

Add Ko-fi content

  • Pin a Ko-fi post: Pin a post about your Ko-fi page at the top of your blog. This could be an introduction to your Ko-fi or what you offer in return for support.
  • Create content about your Ko-fi rewards: Share posts about what supporters can expect -exclusive content, behind-the-scenes looks, or any perks you offer on Ko-fi.
  • Use tags: When posting about your Ko-fi content, use relevant tags to reach a broader audience interested in supporting creators like you.

Offer Tumblr-exclusive perks for Ko-fi supporters

  • Build a membership: Invite your biggest fans on Tumblr to become members of your Ko-fi Membership Tiers. Reward them with exclusive content, behind-the-scenes access, and input on your work.
  • Custom requests: Offer to create custom content (like writing prompts, art commissions, etc.) for Ko-fi supporters, and share examples on your Tumblr. Open Ko-fi Commissions.
  • Exclusive content: Tease content that’s only available to your Ko-fi supporters, and share snippets or previews on Tumblr.

Engage with your Tumblr audience

  • Answer asks about Ko-fi: If followers send you questions (asks) about how they can support you, use these as opportunities to share your Ko-fi link and explain how their support helps.
  • Share thank-yous: Write and post thank-you messages to the people who have supported you on Ko-fi to build trust and encourage more support.
  • Share your Ko-fi achievements: Regularly update your Tumblr followers on your Ko-fi goals, milestones reached, and how their support makes a difference.
  • Engage your followers in setting new goals and ideas: Create posts asking for input on what they’d like to see next, making them feel more invested in your journey.

Let’s turn your Tumblr into a thriving hub of creativity and support with Ko-fi!

Your Ko-fi QR code

Share your Ko-fi page in an instant with your own QR code, perfect for both online and offline!

Print it on stickers, posters, and business cards. You can also share it on your blog, server, or favorite social platform. It’s an easy way to invite more support. 💌

What is a QR code?

A QR code (short for Quick Response code) is a type of barcode that can be scanned with a phone camera. It opens a link instantly, so no typing is needed!

Creators often use QR codes to provide quick access to their Ko‑fi page through physical items like flyers, posters, zines, and more.

Downloading your Ko-fi QR code

It only takes a few seconds to download your QR code:

  • Head to Buttons & Widgets and select “QR Code” or go directly to ko-fi.com/QRCode
  • Download your QR code and start sharing!

Where to share your QR code

Once you’ve got your QR code, you can get creative with where you share it. Here are a few ideas:

  • Add it to flyers, posters, or business cards
  • Print it on packaging, tags, or thank-you notes
  • Include it in zines, art prints, or comics
  • Display it at your table during events or markets
  • Stick it on your laptop, sketchbook, or water bottle
  • Add it to the back of a handmade item or print
  • Use it in your stream overlay, end screen, or channel panels
  • Share it in a slide deck or during a talk or workshop
  • Include it in your portfolio or press kit

Wherever you share your work, your QR code helps fans easily support you.

Does changing my username affect my QR code?

No, you can change your Ko-fi username any time, and your QR code will still work and link to your page. No need to reprint anything!

Still have questions? Feel free to reach out to us. We’re here to help! 💌
